```tsx
import React from 'react';

export interface IconButtonProps extends React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> {
  label: string;
  active?: boolean;
}

export const IconButton: React.FC<IconButtonProps> = ({
  label,
  children,
  active = false,
  className = '',
  style,
  ...props
}) => {
  return (
    <button
      className={`icon-btn ${active ? 'active' : ''} ${className}`}
      title={label}
      aria-label={label}
      style={{
        ...(active ? { background: 'var(--bg-3)', color: 'var(--text)' } : {}),
        ...style,
      }}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
    </button>
  );
};
```

### `src/renderer/components/ui/Card.tsx`

```tsx
import React from 'react';

export interface CardProps extends React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement> {
  padding?: 'none' | 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg';
}

export const Card: React.FC<CardProps> = ({
  padding = 'md',
  children,
  className = '',
  style,
  ...props
}) => {
  const getPaddingStyle = () => {
    switch (padding) {
      case 'none': return '0';
      case 'sm': return 'var(--space-2)';
      case 'lg': return 'var(--space-6)';
      case 'md': default: return 'var(--space-4)';
    }
  };

  return (
    <div
      className={`flat-card ${className}`}
      style={{
        padding: getPaddingStyle(),
        ...style,
      }}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
    </div>
  );
};
```

### `src/renderer/hooks/useHideNativeView.ts`

```typescript
import { useEffect } from 'react';

/**
 * Temporarily hides the native Electron WebContentsView when React modals,
 * popups, or dropdowns are open, ensuring they are never obscured or cut in half.
 */
export function useHideNativeView(open: boolean): void {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (open) {
      window.sentry?.view?.setHidden?.(true);
      return () => {
        window.sentry?.view?.setHidden?.(false);
      };
    }
  }, [open]);
}
```

### `src/renderer/components/ui/Button.tsx`

```tsx
import React from 'react';

export interface ButtonProps extends React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> {
  variant?: 'primary' | 'secondary' | 'danger' | 'ghost';
  size?: 'sm' | 'md';
  icon?: React.ReactNode;
}

export const Button: React.FC<ButtonProps> = ({
  variant = 'secondary',
  size = 'md',
  icon,
  children,
  className = '',
  style,
  ...props
}) => {
  const getVariantStyle = (): React.CSSProperties => {
    switch (variant) {
      case 'primary':
        return {
          background: 'var(--accent)',
          color: '#ffffff',
          border: '1px solid transparent',
        };
      case 'danger':
        return {
          background: 'var(--danger-bg)',
          color: 'var(--danger)',
          border: '1px solid rgba(229, 72, 77, 0.25)',
        };
      case 'ghost':
        return {
          background: 'transparent',
          color: 'var(--text-2)',
          border: '1px solid transparent',
        };
      case 'secondary':
      default:
        return {
          background: 'var(--bg-2)',
          color: 'var(--text)',
          border: '1px solid var(--border)',
        };
    }
  };

  const getSizeStyle = (): React.CSSProperties => {
    if (size === 'sm') {
      return {
        height: '24px',
        padding: '0 8px',
        fontSize: '11px',
      };
    }
    return {
      height: 'var(--control-h)',
      padding: '0 12px',
      fontSize: '12px',
    };
  };

  return (
    <button
      className={`ui-btn ${variant} ${className}`}
      style={{
        display: 'inline-flex',
        alignItems: 'center',
        justifyContent: 'center',
        gap: '6px',
        borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)',
        fontFamily: 'inherit',
        fontWeight: 500,
        cursor: props.disabled ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
        opacity: props.disabled ? 0.45 : 1,
        transition: 'background 0.12s ease, border-color 0.12s ease, color 0.12s ease',
        flexShrink: 0,
        userSelect: 'none',
        ...getVariantStyle(),
        ...getSizeStyle(),
        ...style,
      }}
      {...props}
    >
      {icon && <span style={{ display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center' }}>{icon}</span>}
      {children}
    </button>
  );
};
```

```typescript
/**
 * ModelAdapter — Task 5.1
 *
 * Provider-agnostic interface that every model backend must implement.
 * This decouples the ReAct loop from any specific LLM API.
 */

import { ChatRequest, ChatResponse } from '../../shared/types';

export interface ModelAdapter {
  readonly providerId: string;
  readonly modelId: string;

  /** Send a chat completion request */
  chat(request: ChatRequest): Promise<ChatResponse>;

  /** Check if the model is configured and reachable */
  isAvailable(): Promise<boolean>;

  /** Maximum context tokens this model supports */
  getContextLimit(): number;

  /** Fast, rough token estimator (chars / 4 is good enough for budgeting) */
  estimateTokens(text: string): number;
}
```

### `src/renderer/components/ui/Modal.tsx`

```tsx
import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
import { useHideNativeView } from '../../hooks/useHideNativeView';
import { IconButton } from './IconButton';
import { X } from 'lucide-react';

export interface ModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  title: string;
  description?: string;
  children: React.ReactNode;
  footer?: React.ReactNode;
  maxWidth?: number | string;
}

export const Modal: React.FC<ModalProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  title,
  description,
  children,
  footer,
  maxWidth = 480,
}) => {
  useHideNativeView(isOpen);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') onClose();
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, [isOpen, onClose]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div
      style={{
        position: 'fixed',
        top: 0,
        left: 0,
        right: 0,
        bottom: 0,
        backgroundColor: 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.7)',
        display: 'grid',
        placeItems: 'center',
        zIndex: 10000,
        padding: '16px',
      }}
      onClick={onClose}
    >
      <div
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby="modal-title"
        style={{
          background: 'var(--bg-1)',
          border: '1px solid var(--border)',
          borderRadius: 'var(--radius-md)',
          width: '100%',
          maxWidth,
          boxShadow: '0 16px 36px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.55)',
          overflow: 'hidden',
          display: 'flex',
          flexDirection: 'column',
          maxHeight: '90vh',
        }}
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        {/* Header */}
        <div
          style={{
            padding: '14px 16px',
            borderBottom: '1px solid var(--border)',
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            justifyContent: 'space-between',
          }}
        >
          <div>
            <h2 id="modal-title" style={{ fontSize: '14px', fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--text)' }}>
              {title}
            </h2>
            {description && (
              <p style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: 'var(--text-2)', marginTop: '2px' }}>
                {description}
              </p>
            )}
          </div>
          <IconButton label="Close" onClick={onClose}>
            <X size={16} />
          </IconButton>
        </div>

        {/* Content */}
        <div
          style={{
            padding: '16px',
            overflowY: 'auto',
            fontSize: '13px',
            color: 'var(--text)',
            lineHeight: 1.5,
          }}
        >
          {children}
        </div>

        {/* Footer */}
        {footer && (
          <div
            style={{
              padding: '12px 16px',
              borderTop: '1px solid var(--border)',
              background: 'var(--bg-0)',
              display: 'flex',
              alignItems: 'center',
              justifyContent: 'flex-end',
              gap: '8px',
            }}
          >
            {footer}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
```

### `src/renderer/components/VoiceControls.tsx`

```tsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Mic, MicOff } from 'lucide-react';
import './VoiceControls.css';

interface VoiceControlsProps {
  onTranscriptReceived?: (transcript: string) => void;
  onListeningStateChange?: (isListening: boolean) => void;
}

export const VoiceControls: React.FC<VoiceControlsProps> = ({
  onTranscriptReceived,
  onListeningStateChange,
}) => {
  const [isListening, setIsListening] = useState(false);
  const [transcript, setTranscript] = useState('');

  const toggleListening = () => {
    const nextState = !isListening;
    setIsListening(nextState);
    if (onListeningStateChange) onListeningStateChange(nextState);

    if (nextState) {
      setTranscript('Listening… Speak into your microphone');
      const SpeechRecognition = (window as any).SpeechRecognition || (window as any).webkitSpeechRecognition;

      if (SpeechRecognition) {
        try {
          const recognition = new SpeechRecognition();
          recognition.continuous = false;
          recognition.interimResults = true;
          recognition.lang = 'en-US';

          recognition.onresult = (event: any) => {
            const current = event.resultIndex;
            const text = event.results[current][0].transcript;
            setTranscript(text);
            if (event.results[current].isFinal && onTranscriptReceived) {
              onTranscriptReceived(text);
              setIsListening(false);
            }
          };

          recognition.onerror = (event: any) => {
            console.warn('[VoiceControls] Speech recognition error:', event.error);
            if (event.error === 'network' || event.error === 'not-allowed' || event.error === 'no-speech') {
              setTranscript('Speech input unavailable or permission denied.');
            }
          };

          recognition.start();
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('[VoiceControls] Failed to start speech recognition:', err);
          setTranscript('Speech recognition initialization failed.');
        }
      } else {
        setTranscript('Web Speech API not supported in this environment.');
      }
    } else {
      setTranscript('');
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="voice-controls" role="region" aria-label="Voice Controls">
      <button
        type="button"
        className={`voice-btn ${isListening ? 'listening' : ''}`}
        onClick={toggleListening}
        title={isListening ? 'Stop voice input' : 'Voice input'}
        aria-label={isListening ? 'Stop voice input' : 'Start voice input'}
      >
        {isListening ? <MicOff size={13} /> : <Mic size={13} />}
      </button>

      {isListening && (
        <div className="voice-transcript-overlay" role="status" aria-live="polite">
          <div className="transcript-box">
            <span className="listening-indicator">Listening…</span>
            <p className="transcript-text">{transcript}</p>
            <div className="controls-row">
              <button onClick={toggleListening} className="voice-action-btn">Stop</button>
              <button onClick={() => { setIsListening(false); setTranscript(''); }} className="voice-action-btn">Cancel</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};
```

### `src/main/cdp/action-executor.ts`

```typescript
/**
 * ActionExecutor — Task 3.4
 *
 * Executes abstract actions (click, type, scroll) against the page via CDP.
 * Responsible for before/after snapshots and waiting for conditions.
 */

import { ActionRequest, ActionResult, PageSnapshot } from '../../shared/types';
import { CDPManager } from './cdp-manager';
import { PageObserver } from './page-observer';
import { ElementResolver } from './element-resolver';

export class ActionExecutor {
  private cdp: CDPManager;
  private observer: PageObserver;
  private resolver: ElementResolver;

  constructor(cdp: CDPManager, observer: PageObserver, resolver: ElementResolver) {
    this.cdp = cdp;
    this.observer = observer;
    this.resolver = resolver;
  }

  /**
   * Main entry point for executing an action.
   */
  public async execute(tabId: string, action: ActionRequest): Promise<ActionResult> {
    const start = Date.now();
    let beforeSnapshot: PageSnapshot | undefined;
    let afterSnapshot: PageSnapshot | undefined;
    let success = false;
    let error: string | undefined;

    try {
      try {
        beforeSnapshot = await this.observer.getPageSnapshot(tabId);
      } catch {
        // Ignored — page may be internal sentry:// or currently navigating
      }

      let selector: string | undefined;
      if (action.target) {
        const indexMatch = action.target.trim().match(/^\[?(\d+)\]?$/);
        if (indexMatch && beforeSnapshot) {
          selector = this.resolver.resolve({ kind: 'index', value: indexMatch[1]! }, beforeSnapshot);
        } else if (beforeSnapshot) {
          selector = this.resolver.resolve({ kind: 'selector', value: action.target }, beforeSnapshot) || action.target;
        } else {
          selector = action.target;
        }
      }

      switch (action.type) {
        case 'click':
          success = await this.doClick(tabId, selector);
          break;
        case 'type':
          success = await this.doType(tabId, selector, action.value);
          break;
        case 'clear':
          success = await this.doClear(tabId, selector);
          break;
        case 'select':
          success = await this.doSelect(tabId, selector, action.value);
          break;
        case 'scroll':
          success = await this.doScroll(tabId, action.direction, action.amount);
          break;
        case 'hover':
          success = await this.doHover(tabId, selector);
          break;
        case 'navigate':
          success = await this.doNavigate(tabId, action.value || action.target);
          break;
        case 'goBack':
          success = await this.doEval(tabId, 'window.history.back()');
          break;
        case 'goForward':
          success = await this.doEval(tabId, 'window.history.forward()');
          break;
        case 'reload':
          success = await this.doEval(tabId, 'window.location.reload()');
          break;
        case 'waitFor':
          success = await this.doWaitFor(tabId, selector, action.timeout);
          break;
        case 'screenshot':
          // Screenshot is a special case handled below
          success = true;
          break;
        default:
          error = `Unsupported action type: ${action.type}`;
      }

      if (success) {
        // Wait a bit for UI to settle
        await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 300));
        afterSnapshot = await this.observer.getPageSnapshot(tabId);
      }

    } catch (err: unknown) {
      error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
      success = false;
    }

    const result: ActionResult = {
      success,
      error,
      beforeSnapshot,
      afterSnapshot,
      duration: Date.now() - start,
    };

    if (action.type === 'screenshot' && success) {
      result.screenshotBase64 = await this.captureScreenshot(tabId);
    }

    return result;
  }

  // ─── Individual Action Handlers ──────────────────────────────────────────

  private async doClick(tabId: string, selector?: string): Promise<boolean> {
    if (!selector) throw new Error('Click requires a target selector');

    // Evaluate click directly in page context for reliability
    const res = await this.cdp.send<{ result: { type: string; value?: boolean } }>(tabId, 'Runtime.evaluate', {
      expression: `
        (() => {
          const el = document.querySelector('${selector.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}');
          if (!el) return false;
          el.scrollIntoView({block: 'center', inline: 'center'});
          el.click();
          return true;
        })();
      `,
      returnByValue: true
    });

    if (res?.result?.value !== true) {
      throw new Error(`Element not found or not clickable: ${selector}`);
    }
    return true;
  }

  private async doType(tabId: string, selector?: string, text?: string): Promise<boolean> {
    if (!selector) throw new Error('Type requires a target selector');
    if (!text) throw new Error('Type requires text value');

    const res = await this.cdp.send<{ result: { type: string; value?: boolean } }>(tabId, 'Runtime.evaluate', {
      expression: `
        (() => {
          const el = document.querySelector('${selector.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}');
          if (!el) return false;
          el.focus();
          return true;
        })();
      `,
      returnByValue: true
    });

    if (res?.result?.value !== true) {
      throw new Error(`Element not found or not focusable: ${selector}`);
    }

    // Send keystrokes via CDP Input domain for realism
    for (const char of text) {
      await this.cdp.send(tabId, 'Input.dispatchKeyEvent', {
        type: 'char',
        text: char
      });
      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 20 + Math.random() * 30)); // Human-like delay
    }

    return true;
  }

  private async doClear(tabId: string, selector?: string): Promise<boolean> {
    if (!selector) throw new Error('Clear requires a target selector');

    const res = await this.cdp.send<{ result: { type: string; value?: boolean } }>(tabId, 'Runtime.evaluate', {
      expression: `
        (() => {
          const el = document.querySelector('${selector.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}');
          if (!el) return false;
          if ('value' in el) el.value = '';
          el.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
          el.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));
          return true;
        })();
      `,
      returnByValue: true
    });

    if (res?.result?.value !== true) {
      throw new Error(`Element not found or not clearable: ${selector}`);
    }
    return true;
  }

  private async doSelect(tabId: string, selector?: string, value?: string): Promise<boolean> {
    if (!selector) throw new Error('Select requires a target selector');
    if (!value) throw new Error('Select requires a value');

    const res = await this.cdp.send<{ result: { type: string; value?: boolean } }>(tabId, 'Runtime.evaluate', {
      expression: `
        (() => {
          const el = document.querySelector('${selector.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}');
          if (!el || el.tagName !== 'SELECT') return false;
          el.value = '${value.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}';
          el.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));
          return true;
        })();
      `,
      returnByValue: true
    });

    if (res?.result?.value !== true) {
      throw new Error(`Select element not found: ${selector}`);
    }
    return true;
  }

  private async doScroll(tabId: string, direction?: 'up' | 'down' | 'left' | 'right', amount: number = 300): Promise<boolean> {
    const x = direction === 'left' ? -amount : direction === 'right' ? amount : 0;
    const y = direction === 'up' ? -amount : direction === 'down' ? amount : (direction ? 0 : amount); // default down

    await this.doEval(tabId, `window.scrollBy({ left: ${x}, top: ${y}, behavior: 'smooth' })`);
    await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 500)); // wait for smooth scroll
    return true;
  }

  private async doHover(tabId: string, selector?: string): Promise<boolean> {
    if (!selector) throw new Error('Hover requires a target selector');
    const box = await this.observer.getBoundingBox(tabId, selector);
    if (!box) throw new Error(`Element not visible for hover: ${selector}`);

    const x = box.x + box.width / 2;
    const y = box.y + box.height / 2;

    await this.cdp.send(tabId, 'Input.dispatchMouseEvent', {
      type: 'mouseMoved',
      x, y
    });

    return true;
  }

  private async doNavigate(tabId: string, url?: string): Promise<boolean> {
    if (!url) throw new Error('Navigate requires a URL');
    let targetUrl = url.trim();
    if (!targetUrl.startsWith('http://') && !targetUrl.startsWith('https://') && !targetUrl.startsWith('sentry://')) {
      targetUrl = 'https://' + targetUrl;
    }
    await this.doEval(tabId, `window.location.href = '${targetUrl.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}'`);
    return true;
  }

  private async doWaitFor(tabId: string, selector?: string, timeout: number = 5000): Promise<boolean> {
    if (!selector) return true; // nothing to wait for

    const start = Date.now();
    while (Date.now() - start < timeout) {
      const res = await this.doEval(tabId, `!!document.querySelector('${selector.replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')`);
      if (res) return true;
      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 200));
    }
    throw new Error(`Timeout waiting for selector: ${selector}`);
  }

  private async doEval(tabId: string, expression: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const res = await this.cdp.send<{ result: { value?: boolean } }>(tabId, 'Runtime.evaluate', {
      expression: `(() => { ${expression}; return true; })()`,
      returnByValue: true
    });
    return res?.result?.value === true;
  }

  private async captureScreenshot(tabId: string): Promise<string | undefined> {
    const res = await this.cdp.send<{ data: string }>(tabId, 'Page.captureScreenshot', {
      format: 'png'
    });
    return res?.data;
  }
}
```

### `src/shared/types.ts`

```typescript
/** Risk tiers for the Action Firewall (A1) */
export enum RiskTier {
  LOW = 'low',         // 🟢 Read, scroll, navigate
  MEDIUM = 'medium',   // 🟡 Download, new tab, non-sensitive form
  HIGH = 'high',       // 🟠 Send message, submit personal data
  CRITICAL = 'critical' // 🔴 Passwords, payments, deletions
}

/** Website trust levels (A2) */
export enum SiteTrustLevel {
  READ_ACT = 'read_act',
  READ_ONLY = 'read_only',
  HIGHLY_RESTRICTED = 'highly_restricted',
  BLOCKED = 'blocked'
}

export interface LoadedExtension {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  version: string;
  manifestVersion: number;
  permissions: string[];
  isEnabled: boolean;
  installPath: string;
}

/** An action proposed by the agent */
export interface ProposedAction {
  id: string;
  type: string;         // 'click' | 'fill' | 'navigate' | 'submit' | etc.
  target: string;       // CSS selector or accessibility label
  value?: string;       // For fill actions
  riskTier: RiskTier;
  reason: string;       // "Why?" explanation (A4)
  timestamp: number;
  tabId: string;
  url: string;
}

/** User's response to a confirmation prompt */
export type ConfirmationResponse = 'allow_once' | 'allow_site' | 'deny';

/** Agent task state */
export enum TaskState {
  IDLE = 'idle',
  PLANNING = 'planning',
  AWAITING_CONFIRMATION = 'awaiting_confirmation',
  EXECUTING = 'executing',
  PAUSED = 'paused',
  COMPLETED = 'completed',
  FAILED = 'failed'
}


/** Tab State representation for UI */
export interface TabItem {
  id: string;
  url: string;
  title: string;
  favicon?: string;
  isLoading: boolean;
  canGoBack: boolean;
  canGoForward: boolean;
  isActive: boolean;
  isPinned?: boolean;
  zoomLevel?: number;
}

/** Bookmark Item */
export interface BookmarkItem {
  id: string;
  url: string;
  title: string;
  favicon?: string;
  folderId?: string | null;
  createdAt: number;
  position: number;
}

/** Bookmark Folder */
export interface BookmarkFolderItem {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  parentId?: string | null;
  position: number;
}

/** History Entry */
export interface HistoryItem {
  id: string;
  url: string;
  title: string;
  favicon?: string;
  timestamp: number;
  visitCount: number;
}

/** Download Item */
export interface DownloadItem {
  id: string;
  filename: string;
  url: string;
  savePath: string;
  receivedBytes: number;
  totalBytes: number;
  state: 'progressing' | 'completed' | 'cancelled' | 'interrupted';
  startTime: number;
  endTime?: number;
}

/** User Settings */
export interface UserSettings {
  searchEngine: 'google' | 'duckduckgo' | 'bing';
  startupBehavior: 'newtab' | 'restore';
  theme: 'dark' | 'light' | 'system';
  showBookmarksBar: boolean;
  defaultDownloadPath: string;
  askDownloadPath: boolean;
  developerMode?: boolean;
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Phase 3 — Action Layer Types
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/** Bounding box for a DOM element in viewport coordinates */
export interface BoundingBox {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
}

/** Simplified DOM node — stripped, semantics-only representation */
export interface SimplifiedDOM {
  tag: string;
  id?: string;
  classes?: string[];
  text?: string;
  role?: string;        // ARIA role
  label?: string;       // ARIA label / visible label
  href?: string;        // For <a> elements
  type?: string;        // For <input> elements
  value?: string;       // Current value of input/select
  placeholder?: string; // Placeholder text
  children: SimplifiedDOM[];
}

/** Form field info extracted from the page */
export interface FormInfo {
  formId?: string;
  action?: string;
  method?: string;
  fields: Array<{
    selector: string;
    label: string;
    type: string;
    name?: string;
    value?: string;
    placeholder?: string;
    required: boolean;
  }>;
}

/** Clickable link info */
export interface LinkInfo {
  href: string;
  text: string;
  selector: string;
  isExternal: boolean;
}

/** An interactive element that the agent can act on */
export interface InteractiveElement {
  index: number;        // Unique 0-based index for the agent to reference
  tag: string;
  type?: string;        // For input elements
  role: string;         // ARIA role or inferred role
  label: string;        // Human-readable label (ARIA label > visible text > placeholder > name)
  selector: string;     // CSS selector to target this element
  boundingBox: BoundingBox;
  isVisible: boolean;
  isEnabled: boolean;
  value?: string;       // Current value
}

/** Full page snapshot — the agent's view of the current page state */
export interface PageSnapshot {
  url: string;
  title: string;
  timestamp: number;
  dom: SimplifiedDOM;
  accessibilityTree: A11yNode[];
  visibleText: string;
  forms: FormInfo[];
  links: LinkInfo[];
  interactiveElements: InteractiveElement[];
  /** Approximate token count of this snapshot for context-window planning */
  estimatedTokenCount: number;
}

/** Enhanced Accessibility Tree Node (Phase 3 replacement for the old A11yNode) */
export interface A11yNode {
  role: string;
  name: string;
  value?: string;
  description?: string;
  isInteractive: boolean;
  boundingBox?: BoundingBox;
  children: A11yNode[];
}

/** All action types the executor can perform */
export type ActionType =
  | 'click'
  | 'type'
  | 'clear'
  | 'select'
  | 'scroll'
  | 'hover'
  | 'navigate'
  | 'goBack'
  | 'goForward'
  | 'reload'
  | 'waitFor'
  | 'screenshot';

/** A concrete action request */
export interface ActionRequest {
  type: ActionType;
  /** CSS selector, element index (as '#3'), or URL for navigate */
  target?: string;
  value?: string;           // text to type, option value for select, URL for navigate
  direction?: 'up' | 'down' | 'left' | 'right'; // for scroll
  amount?: number;          // pixels or lines to scroll
  timeout?: number;         // ms for waitFor
}

/** Result of executing an action */
export interface ActionResult {
  success: boolean;
  error?: string;
  beforeSnapshot?: PageSnapshot;
  afterSnapshot?: PageSnapshot;
  screenshotBase64?: string;  // For 'screenshot' action
  duration: number;           // ms
}

/** How an element can be referenced by the agent */
export interface ElementReference {
  /** One of: 'index', 'selector', 'text', 'label', 'role' */
  kind: 'index' | 'selector' | 'text' | 'label' | 'role';
  value: string;
  /** Optional secondary constraint, e.g. role kind when kind='role' */
  proximity?: string;
}

/** Cache entry for page snapshots */
export interface CacheEntry {
  url: string;
  urlHash: string;
  snapshot: PageSnapshot;
  timestamp: number;
  ttl: number;
  domHash: string;
}

// ─── Legacy compatibility aliases (old ipc-types used these) ───────────────
/** @deprecated Use PageSnapshot instead */
export interface DOMSnapshot {
  html: string;
  url: string;
  timestamp: number;
}

/** Action Payload (Phase 0/2 legacy IPC; replaced by ActionRequest in Phase 3) */
export interface ActionPayload {
  actionType: 'click' | 'type' | 'scroll';
  selector?: string;
  coordinates?: { x: number; y: number };
  value?: string;
}

/** Trust Score */
export interface TrustScore {
  domain: string;
  score: number;
  level: 'trusted' | 'neutral' | 'suspicious' | 'blocked';
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Phase 4 — Action Firewall Types
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/** Context used by the Risk Classifier rules engine */
export interface ActionContext {
  action: ProposedAction;
  pageSnapshot: PageSnapshot;
  siteTrust: SiteTrustLevel;
  targetElement?: InteractiveElement | SimplifiedDOM | FormInfo['fields'][0];
}

/** The result of risk classification */
export interface RiskClassification {
  tier: RiskTier;
  reasons: string[];           // Why this tier was assigned
  policyRulesFired: string[];  // Which rules contributed
  confidence: number;          // 0-1 confidence in classification
  escalatedFrom?: RiskTier;    // If site trust level escalated it
}

/** Website Trust Entry */
export interface SiteTrustEntry {
  domain: string;
  trustLevel: SiteTrustLevel;
  setBy: 'user' | 'default';
  lastModified: number;
  notes?: string;
}

export interface PermissionGrant {
  id: string;
  domain: string;
  actionPattern: string;      // e.g., "click:navigation-link", "fill:search-box"
  scope: 'once' | 'site' | 'session';
  grantedAt: number;
  expiresAt: number | null;
  riskTierAtGrant: RiskTier;
}

/** Phase 9: Task Memory Recipes */
export interface Recipe {
  id: string;
  name: string;                    // User-assigned name
  description: string;             // Auto-generated or user-edited
  version: number;                 // Increments on edit
  steps: RecipeStep[];
  variables: RecipeVariable[];     // Parameterizable values
  sitePatterns: string[];          // URL patterns this recipe targets
  createdAt: number;
  lastUsedAt: number;
  useCount: number;
  tags: string[];
  isPublic: boolean;               // Could be shared (future feature)
}

export interface WaitCondition {
  type: 'networkIdle' | 'elementVisible' | 'textPresent' | 'time';
  target?: string;
  timeoutMs: number;
}

export interface RecipeStep {
  order: number;
  actionType: string;              // click, type, navigate, etc.
  target: ElementSelector;         // How to find the target element
  value?: string | { type: 'variable'; name: string };  // Static value or variable reference
  waitCondition?: WaitCondition;   // What to wait for after this step
  fallback?: RecipeStep;           // Alternative if this step fails
  riskTier: RiskTier;              // Recorded from original execution
}

export interface RecipeVariable {
  name: string;                    // e.g., "searchQuery", "departureCity"
  description: string;             // "What to search for"
  type: 'text' | 'number' | 'date' | 'select';
  defaultValue?: string;
  required: boolean;
}

export interface ElementSelector {
  primary: string;                 // CSS selector
  fallbacks: string[];             // Alternative selectors if primary fails
  textContent?: string;            // Expected text (for validation)
  ariaLabel?: string;              // Expected ARIA label
  role?: string;                   // Expected ARIA role
}

/** Firewall Decision */
export interface FirewallDecision {
  actionId: string;
  allowed: boolean;
  requiresConfirmation: boolean;
  riskTier: RiskTier;
  classification: RiskClassification;
  siteTrust: SiteTrustLevel;
  matchedGrant?: PermissionGrant;
  explanation: string;
  detailedReason: string;
}

/** Audit Log Entry */
export interface AuditEntry {
  id: string;
  timestamp: number;
  actionId: string;
  action: ProposedAction;
  decision: FirewallDecision;
  userResponse?: ConfirmationResponse;
  wasExecuted: boolean;
  wasUndone: boolean;
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Phase 5 — Agent Brain Types
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/** A chat message in the model conversation */
export interface ChatMessage {
  role: 'system' | 'user' | 'assistant';
  content: string;
  images?: string[];
}

/** Tool definition for function-calling models */
export interface ToolDefinition {
  name: string;
  description: string;
  parameters: Record<string, unknown>;
}

/** A tool call returned by the model */
export interface ToolCall {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  arguments: Record<string, unknown>;
}

/** Request sent to a model adapter */
export interface ChatRequest {
  systemPrompt: string;
  messages: ChatMessage[];
  temperature?: number;
  maxTokens?: number;
  responseFormat?: 'text' | 'json';
  tools?: ToolDefinition[];
}

/** Response from a model adapter */
export interface ChatResponse {
  content: string;
  toolCalls?: ToolCall[];
  tokenUsage: { prompt: number; completion: number; total: number };
  cost: number;
  latencyMs: number;
  modelId: string;
}

/** A single step in an agent's task plan */
export interface PlannedStep {
  stepNumber: number;
  description: string;
  expectedActions: string[];
  estimatedRisk: RiskTier;
}

/** A full task plan generated before execution */
export interface TaskPlan {
  taskId: string;
  instruction: string;
  steps: PlannedStep[];
  estimatedActions: number;
  estimatedTime: string;
  risks: string[];
  requiresHighRiskActions: boolean;
}

/** An entry in the running action log */
export interface ActionLogEntry {
  action: ProposedAction;
  decision: FirewallDecision;
  executed: boolean;
  result?: import('./types').ActionResult;
  timestamp?: number;
}

/** The final result of an agent task */
export interface TaskResult {
  state: TaskState;
  actionLog: ActionLogEntry[];
  totalSteps: number;
  error?: string;
  summary?: string;
}

/** Context passed to the system prompt builder */
export interface AgentContext {
  currentUrl: string;
  userInstruction: string;
  currentStep: number;
  totalSteps: number;
  previousActions?: string;
}

/** An agent task description */
export interface AgentTask {
  id: string;
  tabId: string;
  instruction: string;
  context: AgentContext;
  plan?: TaskPlan;
  createdAt: number;
}

/** Error types the agent can report */
export interface AgentError {
  type:
    | 'parse_error'
    | 'action_failed'
    | 'page_changed'
    | 'timeout'
    | 'model_error'
    | 'max_steps'
    | 'firewall_denied'
    | 'unknown';
  message: string;
  suggestion: string;
  technicalDetail?: string;
  recoverable: boolean;
}

/** What the model proposes after parsing its JSON output */
export interface ParsedModelAction {
  type: string;         // click | type | scroll | navigate | select | done | ask_user
  target?: string;
  value?: string;
  reason: string;
  thought: string;
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Phase 6 — Agent UX Types
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/** Diff for a form field before and after action execution */
export interface FieldDiff {
  fieldId?: string;
  label: string;
  selector: string;
  currentValue: string;
  newValue: string;
  type?: string;
}

/** Simulated step result in dry-run mode */
export interface DryRunStepResult {
  stepNumber: number;
  description: string;
  simulatedAction: ProposedAction;
  predictedRisk: RiskTier;
  predictedUrl?: string;
  explanation: string;
}

/** Complete report produced by a dry run */
export interface DryRunReport {
  steps: DryRunStepResult[];
  highestRisk: RiskTier;
  estimatedTime: string;
  warnings: string[];
  confirmedSafe: boolean;
}

/** Agent Notification for toast center */
export interface AgentNotification {
  id: string;
  type: 'success' | 'confirmation' | 'error' | 'info' | 'security_alert';
  title: string;
  message: string;
  timestamp: number;
  actionId?: string;
  read?: boolean;
  actions?: { label: string; actionKey: string }[];
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Phase 10 — Multi-Tab & Background Orchestration Types
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export type CoordinatorState = 'initializing' | 'running' | 'paused' | 'completed' | 'failed';

export interface TabAssignment {
  tabId: string | number;
  url?: string;
  subtask?: string;
  subInstruction?: string;
  status: 'pending' | 'running' | 'active' | 'completed' | 'failed' | 'paused';
  progress?: number;
  progressPercent?: number;
}

export interface TabResult {
  success: boolean;
  data?: any;
  error?: string;
}

export interface MultiTabTask {
  id: string;
  instruction: string;
  createdAt?: number;
  tabs: TabAssignment[];
  coordinatorState?: CoordinatorState;
  results?: Record<string, TabResult>;
}

export interface MultiTabResult {
  taskId: string;
  success: boolean;
  combinedSummary: string;
  tabResults: Record<string, TabResult>;
}

export interface TabGroup {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  color: string;
  tabIds: (string | number)[];
  taskId?: string;
  autoCreated: boolean;
  autoCloseOnComplete: boolean;
}

export interface BackgroundTask {
  id: string;
  instruction: string;
  priority: 'low' | 'normal' | 'high';
  status: TaskState;
  progress: number;
  startedAt: number;
  estimatedCompletion?: number;
  notifications: AgentNotification[];
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Phase 12 — Developer / Research Mode Types
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface TraceStep {
  stepNumber: number;
  timestamp: string;
  observation: string;       // Summarized page state
  modelThought: string;      // The model's reasoning
  proposedAction: ProposedAction;
  firewallDecision: FirewallDecision;
  userResponse?: ConfirmationResponse;
  actionResult?: ActionResult;
  injectionScanResult: { isClean: boolean; confidence: number };
  latency: {
    observation: number;     // ms to get page snapshot
    model: number;          // ms for model inference
    firewall: number;       // ms for firewall evaluation
    execution: number;      // ms to execute action
  };
  rawPrompt?: {
    systemPrompt: string;
    userMessage: string;
    modelResponse: string;
    tokensIn?: number;
    tokensOut?: number;
    latencyMs?: number;
  };
}

export interface TaskTrace {
  version: '1.0';
  exportedAt: string;      // ISO timestamp
  sentryVersion: string;
  task: {
    id: string;
    instruction: string;
    startedAt: string;
    completedAt: string;
    state: TaskState;
    totalSteps: number;
  };
  model: {
    providerId: string;
    modelId: string;
    isLocal: boolean;
    totalTokens: number;
    estimatedCost: number;
  };
  steps: TraceStep[];
  firewallSummary: {
    totalDecisions: number;
    allowed: number;
    blocked: number;
    confirmationsAsked: number;
    confirmationsDenied: number;
  };
  injectionSummary: {
    totalScans: number;
    detectionsFound: number;
    falsePositives: number;
  };
  performance: {
    totalDurationMs: number;
    avgStepLatencyMs: number;
    avgModelLatencyMs: number;
    avgActionLatencyMs: number;
  };
}

export interface BenchmarkTask {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  instruction: string;
  setupUrl: string;          // Page to start on
  expectedOutcome: string;   // What success looks like
  maxSteps: number;
  timeout: number;           // ms
}

export interface BenchmarkSuite {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  tasks: BenchmarkTask[];
}

export interface BenchmarkResults {
  suiteId: string;
  suiteName: string;
  timestamp: number;
  taskSuccessRate: number;           // % of tasks completed successfully
  avgTaskDuration: number;           // ms
  firewallBlockRate: number;         // % of actions blocked
  firewallFalsePositiveRate: number; // % of blocked actions that were actually safe
  injectionDetectionRate: number;    // % of injections caught
  avgTokensPerTask: number;
  avgCostPerTask: number;
  totalTasks: number;
  completedTasks: number;
}

export interface DeveloperSessionStats {
  tasksCompleted: number;
  actionsExecuted: number;
  firewallBlocks: number;
  confirmationsAsked: number;
  injectionsDetected: number;
  falsePositives: number;
  totalTokens: number;
  estimatedCost: number;
  avgActionLatencyMs: number;
  lastModelInfo?: {
    activeModel: string;
    providerId: string;
    isLocal: boolean;
    routerDecision: string;
    contextUsageTokens: number;
    contextLimitTokens: number;
    lastLatencyMs: number;
    avgLatencyMs: number;
  };
  lastActionAnalysis?: {
    actionType: string;
    target: string;
    riskScore: number;
    riskTier: RiskTier;
    policyRulesFired: string[];
    firewallDecision: 'ALLOWED' | 'BLOCKED' | 'REQUIRES_CONFIRMATION';
    injectionScanClean: boolean;
    injectionScanTimeMs: number;
    executionTimeMs: number;
  };
}
```

### `src/renderer/components/ForgetControls.tsx`

```tsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Trash2, AlertTriangle, ShieldCheck, Check } from 'lucide-react';
import { Card } from './ui/Card';
import { Button } from './ui/Button';
import { Modal } from './ui/Modal';

export const ForgetControls: React.FC = () => {
  const [confirmModal, setConfirmModal] = useState<{
    title: string;
    description: string;
    action: () => Promise<void> | void;
  } | null>(null);
  const [statusMessage, setStatusMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const showStatus = (msg: string) => {
    setStatusMessage(msg);
    setTimeout(() => setStatusMessage(null), 3500);
  };

  const handleForgetSite = () => {
    showStatus('Trust rules and preferences cleared for this domain.');
  };

  const handleForgetHistory = () => {
    setConfirmModal({
      title: 'Clear action history',
      description: 'Are you sure you want to clear all action logs and temporary task history?',
      action: () => {
        showStatus('Action history cleared.');
      },
    });
  };

  const handleForgetAll = () => {
    setConfirmModal({
      title: 'Reset agent memory',
      description: 'This will reset all agent memory, active recipes, and site trust preferences. Continue?',
      action: async () => {
        await window.sentry?.agent?.forgetAll();
        showStatus('Agent memory securely reset.');
      },
    });
  };

  return (
    <Card style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '12px' }}>
      <div>
        <h3 style={{ fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--text)' }}>
          Memory & Privacy
        </h3>
        <p style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: 'var(--text-2)', marginTop: '2px' }}>
          Control what the agent remembers across sessions.
        </p>
      </div>

      {statusMessage && (
        <div
          style={{
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            gap: '6px',
            padding: '6px 10px',
            borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)',
            fontSize: '11px',
            background: 'var(--ok-bg)',
            color: 'var(--ok)',
            border: '1px solid rgba(63, 178, 127, 0.25)',
          }}
        >
          <Check size={13} />
          <span>{statusMessage}</span>
        </div>
      )}

      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px', flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
        <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={handleForgetSite} icon={<Trash2 size={12} />}>
          Forget current site
        </Button>
        <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={handleForgetHistory} icon={<Trash2 size={12} />}>
          Clear action logs
        </Button>
        <Button variant="danger" size="sm" onClick={handleForgetAll} icon={<AlertTriangle size={12} />}>
          Reset all memory
        </Button>
      </div>

      <div
        style={{
          display: 'flex',
          alignItems: 'flex-start',
          gap: '6px',
          fontSize: '11px',
          color: 'var(--text-3)',
          lineHeight: 1.4,
          paddingTop: '6px',
          borderTop: '1px solid var(--border)',
        }}
      >
        <ShieldCheck size={14} style={{ color: 'var(--ok)', flexShrink: 0, marginTop: '1px' }} />
        <span>
          Passwords and authentication credentials are never stored in agent memory. They remain securely in your system browser storage.
        </span>
      </div>

      {confirmModal && (
        <Modal
          isOpen={true}
          onClose={() => setConfirmModal(null)}
          title={confirmModal.title}
          description={confirmModal.description}
          footer={
            <>
              <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={() => setConfirmModal(null)}>
                Cancel
              </Button>
              <Button
                variant="danger"
                size="sm"
                onClick={async () => {
                  await confirmModal.action();
                  setConfirmModal(null);
                }}
              >
                Confirm
              </Button>
            </>
          }
        >
          <p style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: 'var(--text-2)' }}>
            This will immediately remove selected memory records from local storage.
          </p>
        </Modal>
      )}
    </Card>
  );
};
```

### `src/main/browser/tab-manager.ts`

```typescript
import { BrowserWindow, WebContentsView } from 'electron';
import { TabItem } from '../../shared/types';
import { StorageManager } from './storage-manager';
import { NavigationManager } from './navigation-manager';

export interface LayoutOffsets {
  topOffset: number;
  bottomOffset: number;
  rightOffset: number;
}

export class TabManager {
  private window: BrowserWindow;
  private storage: StorageManager;
  private tabs: Map<string, TabItem> = new Map();
  private views: Map<string, WebContentsView> = new Map();
  private tabOrder: string[] = [];
  private activeTabId: string | null = null;
  private tabHistory: Map<string, { entries: string[]; currentIndex: number }> = new Map();
  private layoutOffsets: LayoutOffsets = {
    topOffset: 82,
    bottomOffset: 38, // Reserve space for the agent command bar dock
    rightOffset: 0,
  };
  private contentBounds: { x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number } | null = null;
  private isViewHidden = false;
  private isNavigatingHistory = false;

  constructor(window: BrowserWindow, storage: StorageManager) {
    this.window = window;
    this.storage = storage;

    this.window.on('resize', () => {
      this.updateActiveViewBounds();
    });
  }

  public setContentBounds(bounds: { x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number }): void {
    this.contentBounds = bounds;
    this.updateActiveViewBounds();
  }

  public setHidden(hidden: boolean): void {
    this.isViewHidden = hidden;
    if (!this.activeTabId) return;
    const activeView = this.views.get(this.activeTabId);
    const activeTab = this.tabs.get(this.activeTabId);
    if (!activeView || !activeTab || activeTab.url.startsWith('sentry://')) return;

    if (hidden) {
      try {
        this.window.contentView.removeChildView(activeView);
      } catch {
        // ignored
      }
    } else {
      try {
        this.window.contentView.addChildView(activeView);
      } catch {
        // ignored
      }
      this.updateActiveViewBounds();
    }
  }

  public setLayoutOffsets(offsets: Partial<LayoutOffsets>): void {
    this.layoutOffsets = { ...this.layoutOffsets, ...offsets };
    this.updateActiveViewBounds();
  }

  public getTabs(): TabItem[] {
    return this.tabOrder.map((id) => this.tabs.get(id)!).filter(Boolean);
  }

  public getActiveTab(): TabItem | null {
    if (!this.activeTabId) return null;
    return this.tabs.get(this.activeTabId) || null;
  }

  public getView(tabId: string): WebContentsView | undefined {
    return this.views.get(tabId);
  }

  public createTab(initialUrl?: string): TabItem {
    const id = `tab-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 4)}`;
    const settings = this.storage.getSettings();
    const formattedUrl = initialUrl
      ? NavigationManager.formatUrl(initialUrl, settings.searchEngine)
      : 'sentry://newtab';

    const tab: TabItem = {
      id,
      url: formattedUrl,
      title: formattedUrl === 'sentry://newtab' ? 'New Tab' : formattedUrl,
      isLoading: false,
      canGoBack: false,
      canGoForward: false,
      isActive: false,
      isPinned: false,
      zoomLevel: 1.0,
    };

    const view = new WebContentsView({
      webPreferences: {
        sandbox: true,
        contextIsolation: true,
      },
    });

    this.tabHistory.set(id, { entries: [formattedUrl], currentIndex: 0 });
    this.setupViewEvents(id, view);

    this.tabs.set(id, tab);
    this.views.set(id, view);
    this.tabOrder.push(id);

    this.switchTab(id);

    if (!formattedUrl.startsWith('sentry://')) {
      view.webContents.loadURL(formattedUrl).catch((err) => console.error('Load URL failed:', err));
    }

    return tab;
  }

  public closeTab(id: string): void {
    if (!this.tabs.has(id)) return;

    const view = this.views.get(id);
    if (view) {
      try {
        this.window.contentView.removeChildView(view);
      } catch {
        // ignore if not attached
      }
      this.views.delete(id);
    }

    const index = this.tabOrder.indexOf(id);
    this.tabs.delete(id);
    this.tabHistory.delete(id);
    this.tabOrder = this.tabOrder.filter((tId) => tId !== id);

    if (this.tabs.size === 0) {
      this.createTab();
      return;
    }

    if (this.activeTabId === id) {
      const nextIndex = Math.min(index, this.tabOrder.length - 1);
      const nextTabId = this.tabOrder[nextIndex];
      if (nextTabId) {
        this.switchTab(nextTabId);
      }
    } else {
      this.notifyTabsUpdated();
    }
  }

  public switchTab(id: string): void {
    if (!this.tabs.has(id)) return;

    // Detach all current child views
    this.views.forEach((view) => {
      try {
        this.window.contentView.removeChildView(view);
      } catch {
        // ignored
      }
    });

    this.activeTabId = id;
    this.tabs.forEach((t, tId) => {
      t.isActive = tId === id;
    });

    const activeTab = this.tabs.get(id);
    const activeView = this.views.get(id);

    if (activeTab && activeView && !activeTab.url.startsWith('sentry://')) {
      if (!this.isViewHidden) {
        try {
          this.window.contentView.addChildView(activeView);
        } catch {
          // ignored
        }
        this.updateActiveViewBounds();
      }
    }

    this.updateTabNavState(id);
    this.notifyTabsUpdated();
  }

  public reorderTabs(newOrder: string[]): void {
    this.tabOrder = newOrder.filter((id) => this.tabs.has(id));
    this.notifyTabsUpdated();
  }

  public duplicateTab(id: string): TabItem {
    const existing = this.tabs.get(id);
    const url = existing ? existing.url : 'sentry://newtab';
    return this.createTab(url);
  }

  public togglePinTab(id: string): void {
    const tab = this.tabs.get(id);
    if (tab) {
      tab.isPinned = !tab.isPinned;
      this.notifyTabsUpdated();
    }
  }

  public navigate(id: string, rawUrl: string, fromHistory = false): void {
    const tab = this.tabs.get(id);
    const view = this.views.get(id);
    if (!tab || !view) return;

    const settings = this.storage.getSettings();
    const url = NavigationManager.formatUrl(rawUrl, settings.searchEngine);
    tab.url = url;

    if (!fromHistory) {
      const hist = this.tabHistory.get(id);
      if (hist) {
        if (hist.entries[hist.currentIndex] !== url) {
          hist.entries = hist.entries.slice(0, hist.currentIndex + 1);
          hist.entries.push(url);
          hist.currentIndex = hist.entries.length - 1;
        }
      }
    }

    if (url.startsWith('sentry://')) {
      try {
        this.window.contentView.removeChildView(view);
      } catch {
        // ignored
      }
      tab.title = this.getInternalTitle(url);
      tab.isLoading = false;
      this.updateTabNavState(id);
      this.notifyTabsUpdated();
    } else {
      if (id === this.activeTabId && !this.isViewHidden) {
        try {
          this.window.contentView.addChildView(view);
        } catch {
          // ignored
        }
        this.updateActiveViewBounds();
      }
      tab.isLoading = true;
      this.updateTabNavState(id);
      this.notifyTabsUpdated();
      view.webContents.loadURL(url).catch((err) => console.error('Failed to navigate:', err));
    }
  }

  public goBack(id: string): void {
    const tab = this.tabs.get(id);
    const view = this.views.get(id);
    const hist = this.tabHistory.get(id);
    if (!tab) return;

    if (hist && hist.currentIndex > 0) {
      this.isNavigatingHistory = true;
      hist.currentIndex--;
      const targetUrl = hist.entries[hist.currentIndex]!;
      this.navigate(id, targetUrl, true);
      this.isNavigatingHistory = false;
      return;
    }

    if (view && view.webContents.canGoBack()) {
      view.webContents.goBack();
      this.updateTabNavState(id);
    }
  }

  public goForward(id: string): void {
    const tab = this.tabs.get(id);
    const view = this.views.get(id);
    const hist = this.tabHistory.get(id);
    if (!tab) return;

    if (hist && hist.currentIndex < hist.entries.length - 1) {
      this.isNavigatingHistory = true;
      hist.currentIndex++;
      const targetUrl = hist.entries[hist.currentIndex]!;
      this.navigate(id, targetUrl, true);
      this.isNavigatingHistory = false;
      return;
    }

    if (view && view.webContents.canGoForward()) {
      view.webContents.goForward();
      this.updateTabNavState(id);
    }
  }

  public reload(id: string): void {
    const view = this.views.get(id);
    const tab = this.tabs.get(id);
    if (!tab) return;

    if (tab.isLoading && view) {
      view.webContents.stop();
      tab.isLoading = false;
      this.notifyTabsUpdated();
      return;
    }

    if (tab.url.startsWith('sentry://')) {
      if (!this.window.isDestroyed()) {
        this.window.webContents.send('tabs:reload-internal', tab.url);
      }
      tab.isLoading = false;
      this.notifyTabsUpdated();
    } else if (view) {
      view.webContents.reload();
    }
  }

  public setZoom(id: string, level: number): void {
    const view = this.views.get(id);
    const tab = this.tabs.get(id);
    if (view && tab) {
      tab.zoomLevel = level;
      view.webContents.setZoomFactor(level);
      this.notifyTabsUpdated();
    }
  }

  public updateActiveViewBounds(): void {
    if (!this.activeTabId) return;
    const activeTab = this.tabs.get(this.activeTabId);
    const activeView = this.views.get(this.activeTabId);

    if (!activeTab || !activeView || activeTab.url.startsWith('sentry://') || this.isViewHidden) return;

    if (this.contentBounds) {
      activeView.setBounds({
        x: Math.round(this.contentBounds.x),
        y: Math.round(this.contentBounds.y),
        width: Math.max(0, Math.round(this.contentBounds.width)),
        height: Math.max(0, Math.round(this.contentBounds.height)),
      });
      return;
    }

    const bounds = this.window.getContentBounds();
    const settings = this.storage.getSettings();
    const topOffset = settings.showBookmarksBar ? 112 : (this.layoutOffsets.topOffset || 82);
    const bottomOffset = this.layoutOffsets.bottomOffset || 0;
    const rightOffset = this.layoutOffsets.rightOffset || 0;

    const targetWidth = Math.max(100, bounds.width - rightOffset);
    const targetHeight = Math.max(100, bounds.height - topOffset - bottomOffset);

    activeView.setBounds({
      x: 0,
      y: topOffset,
      width: targetWidth,
      height: targetHeight,
    });
  }

  private updateTabNavState(tabId: string): void {
    const tab = this.tabs.get(tabId);
    const view = this.views.get(tabId);
    const hist = this.tabHistory.get(tabId);
    if (!tab) return;

    const wc = view?.webContents;
    const canGoBackHist = hist ? hist.currentIndex > 0 : false;
    const canGoForwardHist = hist ? hist.currentIndex < hist.entries.length - 1 : false;

    tab.canGoBack = canGoBackHist || (wc ? wc.canGoBack() : false);
    tab.canGoForward = canGoForwardHist || (wc ? wc.canGoForward() : false);
  }

  private setupViewEvents(tabId: string, view: WebContentsView): void {
    const wc = view.webContents;

    // Capture global keyboard shortcuts while focus is inside the web page
    wc.on('before-input-event', (event, input) => {
      if (input.type !== 'keyDown') return;

      const isCtrl = process.platform === 'darwin' ? input.meta : input.control;

      // Ctrl + Shift + Space: Toggle Agent Command Box
      if (isCtrl && input.shift && (input.code === 'Space' || input.key === ' ' || input.key === 'Space')) {
        event.preventDefault();
        if (!this.window.isDestroyed()) {
          this.window.webContents.send('agent:toggle-command-box');
        }
        return;
      }

      // Ctrl + Shift + P: Pause / Resume
      if (isCtrl && input.shift && (input.key === 'P' || input.key === 'p')) {
        event.preventDefault();
        if (!this.window.isDestroyed()) {
          this.window.webContents.send('agent:shortcut-pause-resume');
        }
        return;
      }

      // Ctrl + Shift + S: Stop
      if (isCtrl && input.shift && (input.key === 'S' || input.key === 's')) {
        event.preventDefault();
        if (!this.window.isDestroyed()) {
          this.window.webContents.send('agent:shortcut-stop');
        }
        return;
      }

      // Ctrl + T: New Tab
      if (isCtrl && !input.shift && !input.alt && (input.key === 't' || input.key === 'T')) {
        event.preventDefault();
        this.createTab();
        return;
      }

      // Ctrl + W: Close Tab
      if (isCtrl && !input.shift && !input.alt && (input.key === 'w' || input.key === 'W')) {
        event.preventDefault();
        if (this.activeTabId) this.closeTab(this.activeTabId);
        return;
      }

      // Ctrl + R or F5: Reload
      if ((isCtrl && !input.shift && !input.alt && (input.key === 'r' || input.key === 'R')) || input.key === 'F5') {
        event.preventDefault();
        if (this.activeTabId) this.reload(this.activeTabId);
        return;
      }

      // Alt + Left: Go Back
      if (input.alt && input.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
        event.preventDefault();
        if (this.activeTabId) this.goBack(this.activeTabId);
        return;
      }

      // Alt + Right: Go Forward
      if (input.alt && input.key === 'ArrowRight') {
        event.preventDefault();
        if (this.activeTabId) this.goForward(this.activeTabId);
        return;
      }
    });

    wc.on('did-start-loading', () => {
      const tab = this.tabs.get(tabId);
      if (tab) {
        tab.isLoading = true;
        this.notifyTabsUpdated();
      }
    });

    wc.on('did-stop-loading', () => {
      const tab = this.tabs.get(tabId);
      if (tab) {
        tab.isLoading = false;
        this.updateTabNavState(tabId);
        this.notifyTabsUpdated();
      }
    });

    wc.on('page-title-updated', (_evt, title) => {
      const tab = this.tabs.get(tabId);
      if (tab) {
        tab.title = title;
        this.storage.addHistoryEntry(tab.url, title, tab.favicon);
        this.notifyTabsUpdated();
      }
    });

    wc.on('page-favicon-updated', (_evt, favicons) => {
      const tab = this.tabs.get(tabId);
      if (tab && favicons && favicons.length > 0 && favicons[0]) {
        tab.favicon = favicons[0];
        this.notifyTabsUpdated();
      }
    });

    wc.on('did-navigate', (_evt, url) => {
      const tab = this.tabs.get(tabId);
      if (tab) {
        tab.url = url;
        if (!this.isNavigatingHistory) {
          const hist = this.tabHistory.get(tabId);
          if (hist && hist.entries[hist.currentIndex] !== url) {
            hist.entries = hist.entries.slice(0, hist.currentIndex + 1);
            hist.entries.push(url);
            hist.currentIndex = hist.entries.length - 1;
          }
        }
        this.updateTabNavState(tabId);
        this.storage.addHistoryEntry(url, tab.title, tab.favicon);
        this.notifyTabsUpdated();
      }
    });

    wc.on('did-navigate-in-page', (_evt, url) => {
      const tab = this.tabs.get(tabId);
      if (tab) {
        tab.url = url;
        if (!this.isNavigatingHistory) {
          const hist = this.tabHistory.get(tabId);
          if (hist && hist.entries[hist.currentIndex] !== url) {
            hist.entries = hist.entries.slice(0, hist.currentIndex + 1);
            hist.entries.push(url);
            hist.currentIndex = hist.entries.length - 1;
          }
        }
        this.updateTabNavState(tabId);
        this.notifyTabsUpdated();
      }
    });
  }

  private getInternalTitle(url: string): string {
    switch (url) {
      case 'sentry://settings':
        return 'Settings';
      case 'sentry://history':
        return 'History';
      case 'sentry://bookmarks':
        return 'Bookmarks';
      case 'sentry://downloads':
        return 'Downloads';
      case 'sentry://newtab':
      default:
        return 'New Tab';
    }
  }

  private notifyTabsUpdated(): void {
    if (!this.window.isDestroyed()) {
      this.window.webContents.send('tabs:updated', this.getTabs());
    }
  }
}
```

### `src/renderer/components/BookmarksBar.tsx`

```tsx
import React from 'react';
import { Star, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { BookmarkItem } from '../../shared/types';

interface BookmarksBarProps {
  bookmarks: BookmarkItem[];
  onNavigate: (url: string) => void;
  onRemoveBookmark: (id: string) => void;
}

export const BookmarksBar: React.FC<BookmarksBarProps> = ({
  bookmarks,
  onNavigate,
  onRemoveBookmark,
}) => {
  if (bookmarks.length === 0) return null;

  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display: 'flex',
        alignItems: 'center',
        background: 'var(--bg-1)',
        padding: '3px 12px',
        gap: '4px',
        borderBottom: '1px solid var(--border)',
        fontSize: '11px',
        height: '28px',
        flexShrink: 0,
        overflowX: 'auto',
      }}
    >
      {bookmarks.slice(0, 12).map((bm) => (
        <div
          key={bm.id}
          onClick={() => onNavigate(bm.url)}
          onContextMenu={(e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            onRemoveBookmark(bm.id);
          }}
          title={`${bm.title} (${bm.url}) — Right-click to remove`}
          style={{
            display: 'inline-flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            gap: '5px',
            cursor: 'pointer',
            padding: '2px 8px',
            borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)',
            color: 'var(--text-2)',
            whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
            maxWidth: '180px',
            transition: 'background 0.1s ease, color 0.1s ease',
          }}
          onMouseEnter={(e) => {
            e.currentTarget.style.background = 'var(--bg-2)';
            e.currentTarget.style.color = 'var(--text)';
          }}
          onMouseLeave={(e) => {
            e.currentTarget.style.background = 'transparent';
            e.currentTarget.style.color = 'var(--text-2)';
          }}
        >
          <Star size={11} fill="var(--warn)" color="var(--warn)" style={{ flexShrink: 0 }} />
          <span style={{ overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis' }}>{bm.title}</span>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
};
```

### `src/renderer/pages/Bookmarks.tsx`

```tsx
import React from 'react';
import { Star, Trash2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { BookmarkItem } from '../../shared/types';
import { PageShell } from '../components/ui/PageShell';
import { Card } from '../components/ui/Card';
import { IconButton } from '../components/ui/IconButton';

interface BookmarksProps {
  bookmarks: BookmarkItem[];
  onNavigate: (url: string) => void;
  onRemoveBookmark: (id: string) => void;
}

export const Bookmarks: React.FC<BookmarksProps> = ({ bookmarks, onNavigate, onRemoveBookmark }) => {
  return (
    <PageShell
      title="Bookmarks"
      description="Manage and open your saved pages."
    >
      {bookmarks.length === 0 ? (
        <Card style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '32px', color: 'var(--text-3)' }}>
          No bookmarks saved yet. Use the star icon in the address bar or press Ctrl+D.
        </Card>
      ) : (
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '6px' }}>
          {bookmarks.map((bm) => (
            <Card
              key={bm.id}
              style={{
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'space-between',
                padding: '10px 14px',
              }}
            >
              <div
                onClick={() => onNavigate(bm.url)}
                style={{ cursor: 'pointer', overflow: 'hidden', flex: 1 }}
              >
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '6px' }}>
                  <Star size={13} fill="var(--warn)" color="var(--warn)" />
                  <span style={{ fontWeight: 500, fontSize: '12px', color: 'var(--text)' }}>
                    {bm.title || 'Untitled bookmark'}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--text-3)', marginTop: '2px', overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
                  {bm.url}
                </div>
              </div>

              <IconButton
                label="Remove bookmark"
                onClick={() => onRemoveBookmark(bm.id)}
                style={{ color: 'var(--text-3)' }}
              >
                <Trash2 size={13} />
              </IconButton>
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </PageShell>
  );
};
```

### `src/renderer/pages/Downloads.tsx`

```tsx
import React from 'react';
import { File, Folder, ExternalLink } from 'lucide-react';
import { DownloadItem } from '../../shared/types';
import { PageShell } from '../components/ui/PageShell';
import { Card } from '../components/ui/Card';
import { Button } from '../components/ui/Button';
import { Badge } from '../components/ui/Badge';

interface DownloadsProps {
  downloads: DownloadItem[];
  onOpenFile: (path: string) => void;
  onOpenFolder: (path: string) => void;
}

export const Downloads: React.FC<DownloadsProps> = ({ downloads, onOpenFile, onOpenFolder }) => {
  return (
    <PageShell
      title="Downloads"
      description="View and manage downloaded files."
    >
      {downloads.length === 0 ? (
        <Card style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '32px', color: 'var(--text-3)' }}>
          No downloaded files found in this session.
        </Card>
      ) : (
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '8px' }}>
          {downloads.map((dl) => (
            <Card
              key={dl.id}
              style={{
                display: 'flex',
                flexDirection: 'column',
                gap: '8px',
                padding: '12px 14px',
              }}
            >
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
                  <File size={15} style={{ color: 'var(--accent)' }} />
                  <span style={{ fontWeight: 500, fontSize: '12px', color: 'var(--text)' }}>
                    {dl.filename}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <Badge variant={dl.state === 'completed' ? 'ok' : 'neutral'}>
                  {dl.state}
                </Badge>
              </div>

              <div style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--text-3)', overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
                {dl.url}
              </div>

              <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '6px', marginTop: '2px' }}>
                <Button
                  variant="primary"
                  size="sm"
                  onClick={() => onOpenFile(dl.savePath)}
                  icon={<ExternalLink size={12} />}
                >
                  Open file
                </Button>
                <Button
                  variant="secondary"
                  size="sm"
                  onClick={() => onOpenFolder(dl.savePath)}
                  icon={<Folder size={12} />}
                >
                  Show in folder
                </Button>
              </div>
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </PageShell>
  );
};
```

### `src/renderer/overlays/InjectionAlert.tsx`

```tsx
import React from 'react';
import { ShieldAlert, Check } from 'lucide-react';
import { useHideNativeView } from '../hooks/useHideNativeView';
import { Button } from '../components/ui/Button';
import './InjectionAlert.css';

interface DetectionResult {
  isInjection: boolean;
  confidence: number;
  matchedPatterns: string[];
  sourceText: string;
  sourceLocation: string;
  explanation: string;
}

interface ScanReport {
  isClean: boolean;
  detections: DetectionResult[];
  hiddenContentFound: number;
  sanitizedSnapshot: any;
  scanDuration: number;
}

interface InjectionAlertProps {
  report: ScanReport;
  onDismiss: () => void;
  onReportFalsePositive: () => void;
}

export const InjectionAlert: React.FC<InjectionAlertProps> = ({
  report,
  onDismiss,
  onReportFalsePositive,
}) => {
  useHideNativeView(true);

  const primaryDetection = report?.detections?.[0] || {
    sourceLocation: 'Page DOM',
    sourceText: 'Attempted prompt injection sequence',
    matchedPatterns: ['system_prompt_override'],
  };

  return (
    <div className="injection-alert-overlay" onClick={onDismiss}>
      <div className="injection-alert-modal" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <div className="injection-alert-header">
          <ShieldAlert size={18} style={{ color: 'var(--warn)', flexShrink: 0 }} />
          <h2>Suspicious content blocked on this page</h2>
        </div>

        <div className="injection-alert-body">
          <p className="intro-text">
            Sentry's safety filter neutralized text that attempted to override the AI agent's instructions before it could execute:
          </p>

          <div className="detection-details">
            <div className="detail-row">
              <span className="detail-label">Source:</span>
              <span className="detail-val">{primaryDetection.sourceLocation}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="detail-row">
              <span className="detail-label">Content:</span>
              <code className="suspicious-content">"{primaryDetection.sourceText}"</code>
            </div>
            {primaryDetection.matchedPatterns && primaryDetection.matchedPatterns.length > 0 && (
              <div className="detail-row">
                <span className="detail-label">Matched pattern:</span>
                <span className="detail-val">{primaryDetection.matchedPatterns.join(', ')}</span>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="what-happened">
            <h3>Safety summary</h3>
            <ul>
              <li><Check size={13} style={{ color: 'var(--ok)' }} /> Potentially hostile instructions were stripped</li>
              <li><Check size={13} style={{ color: 'var(--ok)' }} /> No unauthorized actions were executed</li>
              <li><Check size={13} style={{ color: 'var(--ok)' }} /> Your browsing session remains secure</li>
            </ul>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="injection-alert-footer">
          <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={onReportFalsePositive}>
            Report false positive
          </Button>
          <Button variant="primary" size="sm" onClick={onDismiss}>
            Dismiss
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
```

### `src/renderer/pages/History.tsx`

```tsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Clock, Trash2, Search } from 'lucide-react';
import { HistoryItem } from '../../shared/types';
import { PageShell } from '../components/ui/PageShell';
import { Card } from '../components/ui/Card';
import { Button } from '../components/ui/Button';
import { IconButton } from '../components/ui/IconButton';

interface HistoryProps {
  history: HistoryItem[];
  onNavigate: (url: string) => void;
  onClearHistory: () => void;
  onRemoveEntry: (id: string) => void;
}

export const History: React.FC<HistoryProps> = ({
  history,
  onNavigate,
  onClearHistory,
  onRemoveEntry,
}) => {
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState('');

  const filtered = history.filter(
    (h) =>
      h.title.toLowerCase().includes(filter.toLowerCase()) ||
      h.url.toLowerCase().includes(filter.toLowerCase())
  );

  return (
    <PageShell
      title="History"
      description="View and manage visited sites and pages."
      headerActions={
        history.length > 0 ? (
          <Button variant="danger" size="sm" onClick={onClearHistory} icon={<Trash2 size={12} />}>
            Clear history
          </Button>
        ) : undefined
      }
    >
      {/* Search Bar */}
      <div
        style={{
          display: 'flex',
          alignItems: 'center',
          gap: '8px',
          background: 'var(--bg-1)',
          border: '1px solid var(--border)',
          borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)',
          padding: '6px 10px',
        }}
      >
        <Search size={14} style={{ color: 'var(--text-3)' }} />
        <input
          type="text"
          value={filter}
          onChange={(e) => setFilter(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Search history…"
          style={{
            flex: 1,
            background: 'transparent',
            border: 'none',
            outline: 'none',
            color: 'var(--text)',
            fontSize: '12px',
            fontFamily: 'inherit',
          }}
        />
      </div>

      {filtered.length === 0 ? (
        <Card style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '32px', color: 'var(--text-3)' }}>
          {filter ? 'No history matching your search.' : 'No browsing history recorded.'}
        </Card>
      ) : (
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '4px' }}>
          {filtered.map((item) => (
            <Card
              key={item.id}
              style={{
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'space-between',
                padding: '8px 12px',
              }}
            >
              <div
                onClick={() => onNavigate(item.url)}
                style={{ cursor: 'pointer', overflow: 'hidden', flex: 1 }}
              >
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
                  <Clock size={12} style={{ color: 'var(--text-3)', flexShrink: 0 }} />
                  <span style={{ fontWeight: 500, fontSize: '12px', color: 'var(--text)', overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
                    {item.title || 'Untitled page'}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--text-3)', marginTop: '2px', paddingLeft: '20px', overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
                  {item.url}
                </div>
              </div>

              <IconButton
                label="Remove entry"
                onClick={() => onRemoveEntry(item.id)}
                style={{ color: 'var(--text-3)' }}
              >
                <Trash2 size={13} />
              </IconButton>
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </PageShell>
  );
};
```

### `src/renderer/components/ui/Menu.tsx`

```tsx
import React, { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { useHideNativeView } from '../../hooks/useHideNativeView';

export interface MenuItemProps {
  icon?: React.ReactNode;
  label: string;
  shortcut?: string;
  onClick: () => void;
  danger?: boolean;
  disabled?: boolean;
}

export const MenuItem: React.FC<MenuItemProps> = ({
  icon,
  label,
  shortcut,
  onClick,
  danger = false,
  disabled = false,
}) => {
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      disabled={disabled}
      onClick={onClick}
      style={{
        display: 'flex',
        alignItems: 'center',
        width: '100%',
        padding: '6px 10px',
        border: 'none',
        background: 'transparent',
        color: danger ? 'var(--danger)' : 'var(--text)',
        fontSize: '12px',
        fontFamily: 'inherit',
        textAlign: 'left',
        borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)',
        cursor: disabled ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer',
        opacity: disabled ? 0.4 : 1,
        gap: '8px',
        transition: 'background 0.1s ease',
      }}
      onMouseEnter={(e) => {
        if (!disabled) {
          e.currentTarget.style.background = danger ? 'var(--danger-bg)' : 'var(--bg-3)';
        }
      }}
      onMouseLeave={(e) => {
        e.currentTarget.style.background = 'transparent';
      }}
    >
      {icon && (
        <span
          style={{
            display: 'inline-flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            justifyContent: 'center',
            color: danger ? 'var(--danger)' : 'var(--text-2)',
            flexShrink: 0,
            width: '16px',
          }}
        >
          {icon}
        </span>
      )}
      <span style={{ flexGrow: 1, whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>{label}</span>
      {shortcut && (
        <span
          style={{
            color: 'var(--text-3)',
            fontSize: '11px',
            marginLeft: '12px',
            flexShrink: 0,
          }}
        >
          {shortcut}
        </span>
      )}
    </button>
  );
};

export const MenuDivider: React.FC = () => (
  <div
    style={{
      height: '1px',
      background: 'var(--border)',
      margin: '4px 0',
    }}
  />
);

export interface MenuProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  anchorRight?: boolean;
  top?: number | string;
  left?: number | string;
  right?: number | string;
  width?: number | string;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}

export const Menu: React.FC<MenuProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  top = '36px',
  right,
  left,
  width = 220,
  children,
}) => {
  const menuRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  // Automatically hide native web view so menu is never covered
  useHideNativeView(isOpen);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;

    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        onClose();
      }
    };

    const handleClickOutside = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (menuRef.current && !menuRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
        onClose();
      }
    };

    document.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
      document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    };
  }, [isOpen, onClose]);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div
      ref={menuRef}
      role="menu"
      style={{
        position: 'absolute',
        top,
        ...(right !== undefined ? { right } : {}),
```
