```tsx
        ...(left !== undefined ? { left } : {}),
        width,
        background: 'var(--bg-1)',
        border: '1px solid var(--border)',
        borderRadius: 'var(--radius-md)',
        boxShadow: '0 8px 24px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.45)',
        padding: '4px',
        zIndex: 9999,
        animation: 'fadeIn 0.12s ease-out',
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </div>
  );
};
```

### `src/renderer/components/AgentResultCard.tsx`

```tsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Copy, Check, Volume2, Square, X, Sparkles } from 'lucide-react';
import { IconButton } from './ui/IconButton';
import { Card } from './ui/Card';

export interface AgentResultCardProps {
  instruction: string;
  summary: string;
  timestamp?: number;
  onClear?: () => void;
  style?: React.CSSProperties;
}

export const AgentResultCard: React.FC<AgentResultCardProps> = ({
  instruction,
  summary,
  timestamp,
  onClear,
  style,
}) => {
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const [isSpeaking, setIsSpeaking] = useState(false);

  const handleCopy = () => {
    if (!summary) return;
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(summary);
    setCopied(true);
    setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
  };

  const handleToggleSpeak = () => {
    if (!summary) return;
    if (isSpeaking) {
      window.sentry?.agent?.stopSpeech?.();
      setIsSpeaking(false);
    } else {
      window.sentry?.agent?.speak?.(summary);
      setIsSpeaking(true);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Card
      style={{
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        gap: 'var(--space-2)',
        background: 'var(--bg-2)',
        border: '1px solid var(--border)',
        borderRadius: 'var(--radius-md)',
        padding: 'var(--space-3)',
        ...style,
      }}
    >
      {/* Header */}
      <div
        style={{
          display: 'flex',
          alignItems: 'center',
          justifyContent: 'space-between',
          gap: '8px',
        }}
      >
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '6px' }}>
          <Sparkles size={14} style={{ color: 'var(--accent)' }} />
          <span style={{ fontSize: '11px', fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--text)' }}>
            AI Result
          </span>
          {timestamp && (
            <span style={{ fontSize: '10px', color: 'var(--text-3)' }}>
              {new Date(timestamp).toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })}
            </span>
          )}
        </div>

        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '2px' }}>
          <IconButton
            label={copied ? 'Copied' : 'Copy result'}
            onClick={handleCopy}
            style={{ width: '24px', height: '24px' }}
          >
            {copied ? <Check size={13} style={{ color: 'var(--ok)' }} /> : <Copy size={13} />}
          </IconButton>

          <IconButton
            label={isSpeaking ? 'Stop speaking' : 'Read aloud'}
            onClick={handleToggleSpeak}
            style={{ width: '24px', height: '24px' }}
          >
            {isSpeaking ? <Square size={12} style={{ color: 'var(--danger)' }} /> : <Volume2 size={13} />}
          </IconButton>

          {onClear && (
            <IconButton
              label="Dismiss output"
              onClick={onClear}
              style={{ width: '24px', height: '24px' }}
            >
              <X size={13} />
            </IconButton>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Instruction query quote */}
      {instruction && (
        <div
          style={{
            fontSize: '11px',
            color: 'var(--text-2)',
            fontStyle: 'italic',
            overflow: 'hidden',
            textOverflow: 'ellipsis',
            whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
          }}
          title={instruction}
        >
          "{instruction}"
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Result body */}
      <div
        style={{
          fontSize: '12px',
          color: 'var(--text)',
          lineHeight: 1.5,
          whiteSpace: 'pre-wrap',
          wordBreak: 'break-word',
          background: 'var(--bg-1)',
          padding: '8px 10px',
          borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)',
          border: '1px solid var(--border)',
          maxHeight: '260px',
          overflowY: 'auto',
          userSelect: 'text',
        }}
      >
        {summary}
      </div>
    </Card>
  );
};
```

### `src/renderer/App.tsx`

```tsx
import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useRef, useLayoutEffect } from 'react';
import { TabItem, BookmarkItem, HistoryItem, DownloadItem, UserSettings } from '../shared/types';
import { TabBar } from './components/TabBar';
import { AddressBar } from './components/AddressBar';
import { BookmarksBar } from './components/BookmarksBar';
import { NewTab } from './pages/NewTab';
import { Settings } from './pages/Settings';
import { History } from './pages/History';
import { Bookmarks } from './pages/Bookmarks';
import { Downloads } from './pages/Downloads';
import { TrustDashboard } from './pages/TrustDashboard';
import { ModelSettings } from './pages/ModelSettings';
import { RecipeManager } from './pages/RecipeManager';
import { DeveloperModePage } from './pages/DeveloperMode';
import { ExtensionManagerPage } from './pages/ExtensionManager';
import { AgentSidebar, AgentResultData } from './components/AgentSidebar';
import { AgentVision } from './overlays/AgentVision';
import { InjectionAlert } from './overlays/InjectionAlert';
import { MultiTabDashboard } from './overlays/MultiTabDashboard';
import { TaskState, ActionLogEntry, AgentNotification, PageSnapshot, MultiTabTask, DeveloperSessionStats, TaskTrace, TraceStep } from '../shared/types';
import './styles/index.css';

declare global {
  interface Window {
    sentry?: {
      window: {
        minimize: () => void;
        maximize: () => void;
        close: () => void;
      };
      tabs: {
        getAll: () => Promise<TabItem[]>;
        create: (url?: string) => Promise<TabItem>;
        close: (id: string) => Promise<void>;
        switch: (id: string) => Promise<void>;
        duplicate: (id: string) => Promise<TabItem>;
        togglePin: (id: string) => Promise<void>;
      };
      nav: {
        navigate: (id: string, url: string) => Promise<void>;
        goBack: (id: string) => Promise<void>;
        goForward: (id: string) => Promise<void>;
        reload: (id: string) => Promise<void>;
      };
      bookmarks: {
        getAll: () => Promise<BookmarkItem[]>;
        add: (item: Omit<BookmarkItem, 'id' | 'createdAt' | 'position'>) => Promise<BookmarkItem>;
        remove: (id: string) => Promise<void>;
      };
      history: {
        getAll: (query?: string) => Promise<HistoryItem[]>;
        clear: () => Promise<void>;
        removeEntry: (id: string) => Promise<void>;
      };
      downloads: {
        getAll: () => Promise<DownloadItem[]>;
        openFile: (path: string) => Promise<void>;
        openFolder: (path: string) => Promise<void>;
      };
      settings: {
        get: () => Promise<UserSettings>;
        update: (settings: Partial<UserSettings>) => Promise<UserSettings>;
      };
      view: {
        setLayoutOffsets?: (offsets: { topOffset?: number; bottomOffset?: number; rightOffset?: number }) => Promise<void>;
        setBounds?: (bounds: { x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number }) => Promise<void>;
        setHidden?: (hidden: boolean) => Promise<void>;
      };
      extensions: {
        getAll: () => Promise<import('../shared/types').LoadedExtension[]>;
        load: (path: string) => Promise<import('../shared/types').LoadedExtension>;
        chooseAndLoad: () => Promise<import('../shared/types').LoadedExtension | null>;
        unload: (id: string) => Promise<void>;
        toggle: (id: string, enabled: boolean) => Promise<void>;
      };
      trust: {
        getAll: () => Promise<import('../shared/types').SiteTrustEntry[]>;
        setLevel: (domain: string, level: string) => Promise<boolean>;
        getDefaults: () => Promise<string[]>;
      };
      agent: {
        runTask: (instruction: string) => Promise<{ success: boolean; result?: import('../shared/types').TaskResult; error?: string }>;
        pause: () => Promise<boolean>;
        resume: () => Promise<boolean>;
        stop: () => Promise<boolean>;
        getHistory: () => Promise<ActionLogEntry[]>;
        getRecipes: () => Promise<any[]>;
        saveRecipe: (instruction: string) => Promise<any>;
        deleteRecipe: (id: string) => Promise<boolean>;
        replayRecipe: (id: string, variables: Record<string, string>) => Promise<any>;
        forgetAll: () => Promise<boolean>;
        runMultiTab: (task: MultiTabTask) => Promise<any>;
        submitBackground: (task: any) => Promise<boolean>;
        speak: (text: string, priority?: string) => Promise<boolean>;
        stopSpeech: () => Promise<boolean>;
        getBenchmarkSuites: () => Promise<any[]>;
        runBenchmark: (suiteId: string) => Promise<any>;
        exportTrace: (trace: any, format: 'json' | 'md' | 'csv') => Promise<string>;
        getDeveloperStats: () => Promise<DeveloperSessionStats>;
        getLastTrace: () => Promise<TaskTrace | null>;
        resetDeveloperStats: () => Promise<boolean>;
        getModelConfig: () => Promise<{ localModel: string; cloudModel: string; routingMode: string }>;
        setModelConfig: (cfg: { localModel: string; cloudModel: string; routingMode: string }) => Promise<boolean>;
        listOllamaModels: () => Promise<string[]>;
        checkOllama: () => Promise<boolean>;
        onActionLogged: (callback: (entry: ActionLogEntry) => void) => () => void;
        onStateChanged: (callback: (state: TaskState) => void) => () => void;
        onInjectionDetected: (callback: (report: any) => void) => () => void;
        onRoutingDecision: (callback: (decision: any) => void) => () => void;
        onDeveloperStatsUpdated: (callback: (stats: DeveloperSessionStats) => void) => () => void;
        onStepTraceAdded: (callback: (step: TraceStep) => void) => () => void;
        onToggleCommandBox?: (callback: () => void) => () => void;
        onShortcutPauseResume?: (callback: () => void) => () => void;
        onShortcutStop?: (callback: () => void) => () => void;
      };
      onTabsUpdated: (callback: (tabs: TabItem[]) => void) => () => void;
      onDownloadsUpdated: (callback: (downloads: DownloadItem[]) => void) => () => void;
      onInternalPageReload?: (callback: (url: string) => void) => () => void;
    };
  }
}

export default function App(): React.JSX.Element {
  const [tabs, setTabs] = useState<TabItem[]>([]);
  const [activeTabId, setActiveTabId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [bookmarks, setBookmarks] = useState<BookmarkItem[]>([]);
  const [history, setHistory] = useState<HistoryItem[]>([]);
  const [downloads, setDownloads] = useState<DownloadItem[]>([]);
  const [settings, setSettings] = useState<UserSettings | null>(null);

  // Agent UX State
  const [taskState, setTaskState] = useState<TaskState>(TaskState.IDLE);
  const [actionLog, setActionLog] = useState<ActionLogEntry[]>([]);
  const [notifications, setNotifications] = useState<AgentNotification[]>([
    {
      id: 'welcome-1',
      type: 'info',
      title: 'Sentry Agentic Browser Ready',
      message: 'Press Ctrl+Shift+Space to command the AI agent.',
      timestamp: Date.now(),
      read: false,
    },
  ]);
  const [isSidebarOpen, setIsSidebarOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isVisionActive, setIsVisionActive] = useState(false);
  const [activeSnapshot] = useState<PageSnapshot | undefined>(undefined);
  const [injectionReport, setInjectionReport] = useState<any>(null);
  const [routingDecision, setRoutingDecision] = useState<any>(null);
  const [lastInstruction, setLastInstruction] = useState<string>('');
  const [multiTabTask, setMultiTabTask] = useState<MultiTabTask | null>(null);
  const [lastAgentResult, setLastAgentResult] = useState<AgentResultData | null>(null);
  const [focusTrigger, setFocusTrigger] = useState(0);

  // Step 2: Content measurement with ResizeObserver (no hardcoded offsets!)
  const contentRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useLayoutEffect(() => {
    const el = contentRef.current;
    if (!el) return;

    const send = () => {
      const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      window.sentry?.view?.setBounds?.({
        x: Math.round(r.left),
        y: Math.round(r.top),
        width: Math.round(r.width),
        height: Math.round(r.height),
      });
    };

    const ro = new ResizeObserver(send);
    ro.observe(el);
    window.addEventListener('resize', send);
    send();

    return () => {
      ro.disconnect();
      window.removeEventListener('resize', send);
    };
  }, []);

  // Load initial data
  const loadInitialData = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!window.sentry) return;
    try {
      const allTabs: TabItem[] = await window.sentry.tabs.getAll();
      setTabs(allTabs);
      const active = allTabs.find((t) => t.isActive);
      if (active) setActiveTabId(active.id);

      const bms: BookmarkItem[] = await window.sentry.bookmarks.getAll();
      setBookmarks(bms);

      const hist: HistoryItem[] = await window.sentry.history.getAll();
      setHistory(hist);

      const dls: DownloadItem[] = await window.sentry.downloads.getAll();
      setDownloads(dls);

      const s = await window.sentry.settings.get();
      setSettings(s);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to load initial data:', err);
    }
  }, []);

  // Toggle & focus agent sidebar
  const toggleAndFocusAgent = useCallback(() => {
    setIsSidebarOpen((prev) => {
      const next = !prev;
      if (next) {
        setFocusTrigger((f) => f + 1);
      }
      return next;
    });
  }, []);

  // Agent Operations
  const handleSendTask = async (instruction: string) => {
    setLastInstruction(instruction);
    setTaskState(TaskState.EXECUTING);
    setNotifications((prev) => [
      ...prev,
      {
        id: `notif-${Date.now()}`,
        type: 'info',
        title: 'Task Started',
        message: `Agent executing: "${instruction}"`,
        timestamp: Date.now(),
        read: false,
      },
    ]);

    if (window.sentry?.agent) {
      window.sentry.agent.speak?.(`Executing: ${instruction}`);

      if (instruction.toLowerCase().includes('compare') || instruction.toLowerCase().includes('multi-tab')) {
        const dummyMultiTab: MultiTabTask = {
          id: `mt-${Date.now()}`,
          instruction,
          createdAt: Date.now(),
          tabs: [
            { tabId: 'tab-1', subInstruction: `${instruction} on site A`, status: 'running', progressPercent: 30 },
            { tabId: 'tab-2', subInstruction: `${instruction} on site B`, status: 'pending', progressPercent: 0 }
          ],
          results: {}
        };
        setMultiTabTask(dummyMultiTab);
        try {
          await window.sentry.agent.runMultiTab(dummyMultiTab);
          setTaskState(TaskState.COMPLETED);
          window.sentry.agent.speak?.('Multi-tab task completed successfully');
        } catch (e: any) {
          setTaskState(TaskState.FAILED);
          window.sentry.agent.speak?.('Multi-tab task failed');
        }
        return;
      }

      const res = await window.sentry.agent.runTask(instruction);
      if (res.success) {
        setTaskState(TaskState.COMPLETED);
        const summary = res.result?.summary;
        if (summary) {
          setLastAgentResult({
            instruction,
            summary,
            timestamp: Date.now(),
          });
          const spoken = summary.length > 120 ? `${summary.slice(0, 120)}...` : summary;
          window.sentry.agent.speak?.(spoken);
        } else {
          window.sentry.agent.speak?.('Task completed');
        }
        setNotifications((prev) => [
          ...prev,
          {
            id: `notif-done-${Date.now()}`,
            type: 'success',
            title: 'Task Completed',
            message: summary || `Completed task: "${instruction}"`,
            timestamp: Date.now(),
            read: false,
          },
        ]);
      } else {
        setTaskState(TaskState.FAILED);
        const errMessage = res.error || res.result?.error || 'Failed to complete agent task';
        window.sentry.agent.speak?.('Task failed');
        setNotifications((prev) => [
          ...prev,
          {
            id: `notif-err-${Date.now()}`,
            type: 'error',
            title: 'Task Failed',
            message: errMessage,
            timestamp: Date.now(),
            read: false,
          },
        ]);
      }
    }
  };

  const handlePause = async () => {
    setTaskState(TaskState.PAUSED);
    if (window.sentry?.agent) await window.sentry.agent.pause();
  };

  const handleResume = async () => {
    setTaskState(TaskState.EXECUTING);
    if (window.sentry?.agent) await window.sentry.agent.resume();
  };

  const handleStop = async () => {
    setTaskState(TaskState.IDLE);
    if (window.sentry?.agent) await window.sentry.agent.stop();
  };

  const handleSaveRecipe = async (instruction: string) => {
    try {
      await window.sentry?.agent?.saveRecipe(instruction);
      setNotifications((prev) => [
        ...prev,
        {
          id: `recipe-saved-${Date.now()}`,
          type: 'success',
          title: 'Recipe Saved',
          message: 'Saved to your recipes. Access via the menu.',
          timestamp: Date.now(),
          read: false,
        },
      ]);
    } catch {
      setNotifications((prev) => [
        ...prev,
        {
          id: `recipe-err-${Date.now()}`,
          type: 'error',
          title: 'Save Failed',
          message: 'Failed to save recipe.',
          timestamp: Date.now(),
          read: false,
        },
      ]);
    }
  };

  const handleUndoLast = async (entry: ActionLogEntry): Promise<boolean> => {
    setActionLog((prev) => prev.filter((e) => e !== entry));
    return true;
  };

  const handleDismissNotification = (id: string) => {
    setNotifications((prev) => prev.filter((n) => n.id !== id));
  };

  const handleSwitchTab = async (id: string) => {
    if (window.sentry) {
      await window.sentry.tabs.switch(id);
      setActiveTabId(id);
    }
  };

  const handleCreateTab = async (url?: string) => {
    if (window.sentry) {
      const tab = await window.sentry.tabs.create(url);
      setActiveTabId(tab.id);
    }
  };

  const handleCloseTab = async (id: string) => {
    if (window.sentry) {
      await window.sentry.tabs.close(id);
    }
  };

  const handleDuplicateTab = async (id: string) => {
    if (window.sentry) {
      await window.sentry.tabs.duplicate(id);
    }
  };

  const handleTogglePinTab = async (id: string) => {
    if (window.sentry) {
      await window.sentry.tabs.togglePin(id);
    }
  };

  // Navigation operations
  const activeTab = tabs.find((t) => t.id === activeTabId) || null;

  const handleNavigate = useCallback(async (url: string) => {
    if (window.sentry && activeTabId) {
      await window.sentry.nav.navigate(activeTabId, url);
    }
  }, [activeTabId]);

  const handleGoBack = async () => {
    if (window.sentry && activeTabId) {
      await window.sentry.nav.goBack(activeTabId);
    }
  };

  const handleGoForward = async () => {
    if (window.sentry && activeTabId) {
      await window.sentry.nav.goForward(activeTabId);
    }
  };

  const handleReload = useCallback(async () => {
    if (window.sentry && activeTabId) {
      await window.sentry.nav.reload(activeTabId);
    }
  }, [activeTabId]);

  // Bookmark operations
  const handleToggleBookmark = useCallback(async (url: string, title: string) => {
    if (!window.sentry) return;
    const isBookmarked = bookmarks.some((b) => b.url === url);
    if (isBookmarked) {
      const bm = bookmarks.find((b) => b.url === url);
      if (bm) {
        await window.sentry.bookmarks.remove(bm.id);
      }
    } else {
      await window.sentry.bookmarks.add({ url, title });
    }
    const updated = await window.sentry.bookmarks.getAll();
    setBookmarks(updated);
  }, [bookmarks]);

  const handleRemoveBookmark = async (id: string) => {
    if (!window.sentry) return;
    await window.sentry.bookmarks.remove(id);
    const updated = await window.sentry.bookmarks.getAll();
    setBookmarks(updated);
  };

  // History operations
  const handleClearHistory = async () => {
    if (window.sentry) {
      await window.sentry.history.clear();
      setHistory([]);
      setNotifications((prev) => [
        ...prev,
        {
          id: `hist-clear-${Date.now()}`,
          type: 'info',
          title: 'History Cleared',
          message: 'All browsing history entries removed.',
          timestamp: Date.now(),
          read: false,
        },
      ]);
    }
  };

  const handleRemoveHistoryEntry = async (id: string) => {
    if (window.sentry) {
      await window.sentry.history.removeEntry(id);
      setHistory((prev) => prev.filter((h) => h.id !== id));
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    loadInitialData();

    if (window.sentry) {
      const unsubTabs = window.sentry.onTabsUpdated((updatedTabs: TabItem[]) => {
        setTabs(updatedTabs);
        const active = updatedTabs.find((t) => t.isActive);
        if (active) setActiveTabId(active.id);
      });

      const unsubDownloads = window.sentry.onDownloadsUpdated((updatedDownloads: DownloadItem[]) => {
        setDownloads(updatedDownloads);
      });

      const unsubActionLog = window.sentry.agent?.onActionLogged((entry: ActionLogEntry) => {
        setActionLog((prev) => [...prev, entry]);
      });

      const unsubState = window.sentry.agent?.onStateChanged((state: TaskState) => {
        setTaskState(state);
      });

      const unsubInjection = window.sentry.agent?.onInjectionDetected((report: any) => {
        setInjectionReport(report);
      });

      const unsubRouting = window.sentry.agent?.onRoutingDecision((decision: any) => {
        setRoutingDecision(decision);
      });

      const unsubReload = window.sentry.onInternalPageReload?.(() => {
        loadInitialData();
      });

      const unsubToggle = window.sentry.agent?.onToggleCommandBox?.(() => {
        toggleAndFocusAgent();
      });

      const unsubPause = window.sentry.agent?.onShortcutPauseResume?.(() => {
        if ([TaskState.PLANNING, TaskState.EXECUTING, TaskState.AWAITING_CONFIRMATION].includes(taskState)) {
          handlePause();
        } else if (taskState === TaskState.PAUSED) {
          handleResume();
        }
      });

      const unsubStop = window.sentry.agent?.onShortcutStop?.(() => {
        handleStop();
      });

      return () => {
        unsubTabs();
        unsubDownloads();
        if (unsubActionLog) unsubActionLog();
        if (unsubState) unsubState();
        if (unsubInjection) unsubInjection();
        if (unsubRouting) unsubRouting();
        if (unsubReload) unsubReload();
        if (unsubToggle) unsubToggle();
        if (unsubPause) unsubPause();
        if (unsubStop) unsubStop();
      };
    }
  }, [loadInitialData, taskState, toggleAndFocusAgent]);

  // Keyboard Shortcuts (single unified registry)
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      // Ctrl+Shift+Space: Toggle Agent sidebar & focus
      if (e.ctrlKey && e.shiftKey && (e.code === 'Space' || e.key === ' ' || e.key === 'Space')) {
        e.preventDefault();
        toggleAndFocusAgent();
        return;
      }

      if (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) {
        switch (e.key.toLowerCase()) {
          case 't':
            e.preventDefault();
            handleCreateTab();
            break;
          case 'w':
            e.preventDefault();
            if (activeTabId) handleCloseTab(activeTabId);
            break;
          case 'h':
            e.preventDefault();
            handleNavigate('sentry://history');
            break;
          case 'j':
            e.preventDefault();
            handleNavigate('sentry://downloads');
            break;
          case ',':
            e.preventDefault();
            handleNavigate('sentry://settings');
            break;
          case 'r':
            e.preventDefault();
            handleReload();
            break;
          case 'd':
            e.preventDefault();
            if (activeTab) handleToggleBookmark(activeTab.url, activeTab.title);
            break;
        }

        // Tab switching Ctrl+1 to Ctrl+9
        if (/^[1-9]$/.test(e.key)) {
          const idx = parseInt(e.key, 10) - 1;
          if (tabs[idx]) {
            e.preventDefault();
            handleSwitchTab(tabs[idx].id);
          }
        }
      } else if (e.key === 'F5') {
        e.preventDefault();
        handleReload();
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, [activeTabId, activeTab, tabs, handleNavigate, handleReload, handleToggleBookmark, toggleAndFocusAgent]);

  // Window Controls
  const handleMinimize = () => window.sentry?.window.minimize();
  const handleMaximize = () => window.sentry?.window.maximize();
  const handleCloseWindow = () => window.sentry?.window.close();

  // Bookmarks bar displays only if enabled AND has items
  const showBookmarksBar = (settings?.showBookmarksBar ?? true) && bookmarks.length > 0;

  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        height: '100vh',
        width: '100vw',
        background: 'var(--bg-0)',
        overflow: 'hidden',
      }}
    >
      {/* ── Titlebar + Tab Strip ───────────────────────────────── */}
      <div
        style={{
          display: 'flex',
          alignItems: 'center',
          background: 'var(--bg-0)',
          WebkitAppRegion: 'drag',
          userSelect: 'none',
          height: '38px',
          borderBottom: '1px solid var(--border)',
          flexShrink: 0,
        } as React.CSSProperties}
        onDoubleClick={handleMaximize}
      >
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', WebkitAppRegion: 'drag', flexGrow: 1, minWidth: 0, overflow: 'hidden' } as React.CSSProperties}>
          <TabBar
            tabs={tabs}
            activeTabId={activeTabId}
            onSwitchTab={handleSwitchTab}
            onCloseTab={handleCloseTab}
            onCreateTab={() => handleCreateTab()}
            onDuplicateTab={handleDuplicateTab}
            onTogglePinTab={handleTogglePinTab}
          />
        </div>

        {/* Draggable Titlebar Spacer */}
        <div
          style={{
            flexGrow: 1,
            height: '100%',
            WebkitAppRegion: 'drag',
            cursor: 'default',
          } as React.CSSProperties}
          onDoubleClick={handleMaximize}
        />

        {/* Window Controls Only (titlebar kept clean) */}
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', WebkitAppRegion: 'no-drag' } as React.CSSProperties}>
          <button
            onClick={handleMinimize}
            title="Minimize"
            style={{
              border: 'none',
              background: 'transparent',
              color: 'var(--text-2)',
              padding: '6px 12px',
              cursor: 'pointer',
              fontSize: '12px',
              transition: 'background 0.12s ease',
            }}
            onMouseEnter={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.background = 'var(--bg-3)')}
            onMouseLeave={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.background = 'transparent')}
          >
            ─
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={handleMaximize}
            title="Maximize"
            style={{
              border: 'none',
              background: 'transparent',
              color: 'var(--text-2)',
              padding: '6px 12px',
              cursor: 'pointer',
              fontSize: '12px',
              transition: 'background 0.12s ease',
            }}
            onMouseEnter={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.background = 'var(--bg-3)')}
            onMouseLeave={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.background = 'transparent')}
          >
            □
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={handleCloseWindow}
            title="Close"
            style={{
              border: 'none',
              background: 'transparent',
              color: 'var(--text-2)',
              padding: '6px 12px',
              cursor: 'pointer',
              fontSize: '12px',
              transition: 'background 0.12s ease, color 0.12s ease',
            }}
            onMouseEnter={(e) => {
              e.currentTarget.style.background = 'var(--danger)';
              e.currentTarget.style.color = '#ffffff';
            }}
            onMouseLeave={(e) => {
              e.currentTarget.style.background = 'transparent';
              e.currentTarget.style.color = 'var(--text-2)';
            }}
          >
            ✕
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ── Toolbar (Address Bar) ──────────────────────────────── */}
      <AddressBar
        activeTab={activeTab}
        bookmarks={bookmarks}
        history={history}
        routingDecision={routingDecision}
        isSidebarOpen={isSidebarOpen}
        onToggleSidebar={toggleAndFocusAgent}
        notifications={notifications}
        onDismissNotification={handleDismissNotification}
        onClearNotifications={() => setNotifications([])}
        onNavigate={handleNavigate}
        onGoBack={handleGoBack}
        onGoForward={handleGoForward}
        onReload={handleReload}
        onToggleBookmark={handleToggleBookmark}
        onOpenPage={(page) => handleNavigate(`sentry://${page}`)}
      />

      {/* ── Bookmarks Bar (only if enabled AND has items) ──────── */}
      {showBookmarksBar && (
        <BookmarksBar
          bookmarks={bookmarks}
          onNavigate={handleNavigate}
          onRemoveBookmark={handleRemoveBookmark}
        />
      )}

      {/* ── Content & Docked Sidebar Row (Flex layout) ─────────── */}
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexGrow: 1, minHeight: 0, position: 'relative', overflow: 'hidden' }}>
        {/* Measured Content Area (Native WebContentsView sits exactly here) */}
        <div
          ref={contentRef}
          style={{
            flexGrow: 1,
            height: '100%',
            position: 'relative',
            overflow: 'auto',
            background: 'var(--bg-0)',
          }}
        >
          {activeTab?.url === 'sentry://newtab' && (
            <NewTab history={history} onNavigate={handleNavigate} />
          )}
          {activeTab?.url === 'sentry://settings' && (
            <Settings onClearHistory={handleClearHistory} />
          )}
          {activeTab?.url === 'sentry://history' && (
            <History
              history={history}
              onNavigate={handleNavigate}
              onClearHistory={handleClearHistory}
              onRemoveEntry={handleRemoveHistoryEntry}
            />
          )}
          {activeTab?.url === 'sentry://bookmarks' && (
            <Bookmarks
              bookmarks={bookmarks}
              onNavigate={handleNavigate}
              onRemoveBookmark={handleRemoveBookmark}
            />
          )}
          {activeTab?.url === 'sentry://downloads' && (
            <Downloads
              downloads={downloads}
              onOpenFile={(path) => window.sentry?.downloads.openFile(path)}
              onOpenFolder={(path) => window.sentry?.downloads.openFolder(path)}
            />
          )}
          {activeTab?.url === 'sentry://trust' && (
            <TrustDashboard />
          )}
          {activeTab?.url === 'sentry://models' && (
            <ModelSettings />
          )}
          {activeTab?.url === 'sentry://recipes' && (
            <RecipeManager />
          )}
          {activeTab?.url === 'sentry://developer' && (
            <DeveloperModePage />
          )}
          {activeTab?.url === 'sentry://extensions' && (
            <ExtensionManagerPage />
          )}

          {/* Multi-Tab Dashboard Overlay */}
          {multiTabTask && (
            <MultiTabDashboard
              task={multiTabTask}
              onPauseAll={() => {
                const updated = { ...multiTabTask };
                updated.tabs.forEach((t) => (t.status = 'paused'));
                setMultiTabTask(updated);
              }}
              onStopAll={() => setMultiTabTask(null)}
              onSwitchToTab={(tabId) => handleSwitchTab(tabId)}
            />
          )}

          {/* Agent Vision Mode Overlay */}
          {isVisionActive && (
            <AgentVision
              snapshot={activeSnapshot}
              onClose={() => setIsVisionActive(false)}
            />
          )}

          {/* Injection Alert Modal */}
          {injectionReport && (
            <InjectionAlert
              report={injectionReport}
              onDismiss={() => setInjectionReport(null)}
              onReportFalsePositive={() => {
                setInjectionReport(null);
              }}
            />
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Docked Agent Sidebar (Normal flex child, shrinks content automatically) */}
        {isSidebarOpen && (
          <AgentSidebar
            taskState={taskState}
            actionLog={actionLog}
            lastResult={lastAgentResult}
            routingDecision={routingDecision}
            lastInstruction={lastInstruction}
            onClearResult={() => setLastAgentResult(null)}
            onSendTask={handleSendTask}
            onPause={handlePause}
            onStop={handleStop}
            onResume={handleResume}
            onUndoLast={handleUndoLast}
            onClose={() => setIsSidebarOpen(false)}
            onSaveRecipe={handleSaveRecipe}
            focusTrigger={focusTrigger}
          />
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

### `src/renderer/components/WhyExplanation.tsx`

```tsx
import React from 'react';
import { HelpCircle, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { ActionLogEntry, RiskTier } from '../../shared/types';
import { useHideNativeView } from '../hooks/useHideNativeView';
import { IconButton } from './ui/IconButton';
import { Badge } from './ui/Badge';
import './WhyExplanation.css';

interface WhyExplanationProps {
  entry: ActionLogEntry;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export const WhyExplanation: React.FC<WhyExplanationProps> = ({ entry, onClose }) => {
  useHideNativeView(true);

  const { action, decision } = entry;
  const riskTier = decision?.riskTier || action?.riskTier || RiskTier.LOW;

  const getTierInfo = (tier: RiskTier): { label: string; variant: 'ok' | 'warn' | 'danger' } => {
    switch (tier) {
      case RiskTier.LOW:
        return { label: 'Low risk', variant: 'ok' };
      case RiskTier.MEDIUM:
        return { label: 'Medium risk', variant: 'warn' };
      case RiskTier.HIGH:
        return { label: 'High risk', variant: 'warn' };
      case RiskTier.CRITICAL:
        return { label: 'Critical risk', variant: 'danger' };
      default:
        return { label: 'Low risk', variant: 'ok' };
    }
  };

  const tierInfo = getTierInfo(riskTier);

  return (
    <div className="why-modal-backdrop" onClick={onClose}>
      <div className="why-card" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <div className="why-header">
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
            <HelpCircle size={16} style={{ color: 'var(--accent)' }} />
            <span style={{ fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--text)' }}>
              Action details
            </span>
          </div>
          <IconButton label="Close" onClick={onClose} style={{ width: '24px', height: '24px' }}>
            <X size={14} />
          </IconButton>
        </div>

        <div className="why-body">
          <div className="why-action-title">
            <Badge variant={tierInfo.variant} dot>
              {tierInfo.label}
            </Badge>
            <span style={{ fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--text)' }}>
              {action.type}
            </span>
            <span style={{ color: 'var(--text-3)' }}>on</span>
            <code className="why-code">{action.target || 'page'}</code>
          </div>

          <div className="why-section">
            <span className="why-section-label">Agent reason:</span>
            <p className="why-reason-text">"{action.reason || 'No explicit reason specified.'}"</p>
          </div>

          {action.value && (
            <div className="why-section">
              <span className="why-section-label">Input payload:</span>
              <code className="why-code">{action.value}</code>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="why-grid">
            <div className="why-grid-item">
              <span className="why-grid-label">Risk classification:</span>
              <span className="why-grid-val">{tierInfo.label}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="why-grid-item">
              <span className="why-grid-label">Domain trust:</span>
              <span className="why-grid-val">{decision?.siteTrust ? String(decision.siteTrust) : 'Standard'}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="why-grid-item">
              <span className="why-grid-label">Policy rules:</span>
              <span className="why-grid-val">{decision?.classification?.policyRulesFired?.join(', ') || 'Default policy'}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="why-grid-item">
              <span className="why-grid-label">Decision:</span>
              <span className="why-grid-val">{decision?.allowed ? 'Permitted' : 'Blocked / Required confirmation'}</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="why-meta">
            <span><strong>URL:</strong> {action.url || 'Active tab'}</span>
            <span><strong>Time:</strong> {new Date(action.timestamp || Date.now()).toLocaleTimeString()}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
```

```tsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Search, Globe } from 'lucide-react';
import { HistoryItem } from '../../shared/types';
import { Card } from '../components/ui/Card';

interface NewTabProps {
  history: HistoryItem[];
  onNavigate: (url: string) => void;
}

export const NewTab: React.FC<NewTabProps> = ({ history, onNavigate }) => {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');

  const topSites = history.slice(0, 6);

  const handleSearch = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (query.trim()) {
      onNavigate(query.trim());
    }
  };

  return (
    <div
      style={{
        height: '100%',
        width: '100%',
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        alignItems: 'center',
        justifyContent: 'center',
        background: 'var(--bg-0)',
        color: 'var(--text)',
        fontFamily: 'inherit',
        padding: '24px',
      }}
    >
      {/* Title */}
      <h1
        style={{
          fontSize: '24px',
          fontWeight: 600,
          color: 'var(--text)',
          marginBottom: '4px',
          letterSpacing: '-0.02em',
        }}
      >
        Sentry
      </h1>
      <p style={{ color: 'var(--text-3)', fontSize: '13px', marginBottom: '28px' }}>
        Agentic browser
      </p>

      {/* Centered Search Bar */}
      <form
        onSubmit={handleSearch}
        style={{
          width: '100%',
          maxWidth: '560px',
          marginBottom: '32px',
        }}
      >
        <div
          style={{
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            background: 'var(--bg-1)',
            border: '1px solid var(--border)',
            borderRadius: 'var(--radius-md)',
            padding: '10px 14px',
            transition: 'border-color 0.15s ease',
          }}
        >
          <Search size={16} style={{ color: 'var(--text-3)', marginRight: '10px', flexShrink: 0 }} />
          <input
            type="text"
            value={query}
            onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Search with Google or enter web address…"
            style={{
              width: '100%',
              background: 'transparent',
              border: 'none',
              outline: 'none',
              color: 'var(--text)',
              fontSize: '13px',
              fontFamily: 'inherit',
            }}
            autoFocus
          />
        </div>
      </form>

      {/* Quick Access Tiles */}
      <div style={{ width: '100%', maxWidth: '560px' }}>
        <div style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--text-3)', marginBottom: '8px', fontWeight: 500 }}>
          Frequently visited
        </div>
        <div
          style={{
            display: 'grid',
            gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(3, 1fr)',
            gap: '8px',
          }}
        >
          {(topSites.length > 0
            ? topSites
            : [
                { id: '1', title: 'Google', url: 'https://google.com' },
                { id: '2', title: 'GitHub', url: 'https://github.com' },
                { id: '3', title: 'Wikipedia', url: 'https://wikipedia.org' },
              ]
          ).map((item) => (
            <Card
              key={item.id}
              onClick={() => onNavigate(item.url)}
              style={{
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                gap: '10px',
                padding: '10px 12px',
                cursor: 'pointer',
                transition: 'background 0.12s ease',
              }}
              onMouseEnter={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.background = 'var(--bg-3)')}
              onMouseLeave={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.background = 'var(--bg-2)')}
            >
              <div
                style={{
                  width: '28px',
                  height: '28px',
                  borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)',
                  background: 'var(--bg-1)',
                  border: '1px solid var(--border)',
                  display: 'grid',
                  placeItems: 'center',
                  color: 'var(--text-2)',
                  flexShrink: 0,
                }}
              >
                <Globe size={14} />
              </div>
              <div style={{ overflow: 'hidden' }}>
                <div style={{ fontWeight: 500, fontSize: '12px', color: 'var(--text)', whiteSpace: 'nowrap', overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis' }}>
                  {item.title}
                </div>
                <div style={{ fontSize: '10px', color: 'var(--text-3)', whiteSpace: 'nowrap', overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis' }}>
                  {item.url}
                </div>
              </div>
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
```

### `src/renderer/pages/ExtensionManager.tsx`

```tsx
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Puzzle, FolderOpen, Trash2, Check, AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { LoadedExtension } from '../../shared/types';
import { PageShell } from '../components/ui/PageShell';
import { Card } from '../components/ui/Card';
import { Button } from '../components/ui/Button';
import { Badge } from '../components/ui/Badge';
import { IconButton } from '../components/ui/IconButton';

export const ExtensionManagerPage: React.FC = () => {
  const [extensions, setExtensions] = useState<LoadedExtension[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [feedback, setFeedback] = useState<{ message: string; type: 'ok' | 'error' } | null>(null);

  const fetchExtensions = async () => {
    if ((window as any).sentry?.extensions) {
      const exts = await (window as any).sentry.extensions.getAll();
      setExtensions(exts || []);
    }
    setLoading(false);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchExtensions();
  }, []);

  const handleToggle = async (id: string, enabled: boolean) => {
    if ((window as any).sentry?.extensions) {
      await (window as any).sentry.extensions.toggle(id, enabled);
      await fetchExtensions();
    }
  };

  const handleRemove = async (id: string) => {
    if ((window as any).sentry?.extensions) {
      await (window as any).sentry.extensions.unload(id);
      await fetchExtensions();
      setFeedback({ message: 'Extension removed.', type: 'ok' });
      setTimeout(() => setFeedback(null), 3000);
    }
  };

  const handleLoadUnpacked = async () => {
    if ((window as any).sentry?.extensions) {
      try {
        const loaded = await (window as any).sentry.extensions.chooseAndLoad();
        if (loaded) {
          await fetchExtensions();
          setFeedback({ message: `Loaded extension "${loaded.name}".`, type: 'ok' });
          setTimeout(() => setFeedback(null), 3000);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        setFeedback({ message: `Failed to load extension: ${String(err)}`, type: 'error' });
        setTimeout(() => setFeedback(null), 4000);
      }
    }
  };

  return (
    <PageShell
      title="Extensions"
      description="Manage Chrome extensions and background scripts."
      headerActions={
        <Button variant="primary" size="sm" onClick={handleLoadUnpacked} icon={<FolderOpen size={12} />}>
          Load unpacked extension
        </Button>
      }
    >
      {feedback && (
        <div
          style={{
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            gap: '8px',
            padding: '8px 12px',
            borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)',
            fontSize: '12px',
            background: feedback.type === 'ok' ? 'var(--ok-bg)' : 'var(--danger-bg)',
            color: feedback.type === 'ok' ? 'var(--ok)' : 'var(--danger)',
            border: `1px solid ${feedback.type === 'ok' ? 'rgba(63, 178, 127, 0.25)' : 'rgba(229, 72, 77, 0.25)'}`,
          }}
        >
          {feedback.type === 'ok' ? <Check size={14} /> : <AlertCircle size={14} />}
          <span>{feedback.message}</span>
        </div>
      )}

      {loading ? (
        <Card style={{ textAlign: 'center', color: 'var(--text-3)', padding: '32px' }}>
          Loading extensions…
        </Card>
      ) : extensions.length === 0 ? (
        <Card style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '32px', color: 'var(--text-3)' }}>
          <p style={{ fontWeight: 500, color: 'var(--text)' }}>No extensions installed</p>
          <p style={{ fontSize: '12px', marginTop: '4px' }}>
            Load an unpacked extension directory (.crx or manifest directory) to get started.
          </p>
        </Card>
      ) : (
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '8px' }}>
          {extensions.map((ext) => (
            <Card
              key={ext.id}
              style={{
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'space-between',
                padding: '12px 14px',
              }}
            >
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '10px' }}>
                <Puzzle size={16} style={{ color: 'var(--accent)' }} />
                <div>
                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
                    <span style={{ fontWeight: 500, fontSize: '13px', color: 'var(--text)' }}>
                      {ext.name}
                    </span>
                    <span style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--text-3)' }}>v{ext.version}</span>
                    <Badge variant={ext.isEnabled ? 'ok' : 'neutral'} dot>
                      {ext.isEnabled ? 'Enabled' : 'Disabled'}
                    </Badge>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
                <label style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '6px', fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--text-2)', cursor: 'pointer' }}>
                  <input
                    type="checkbox"
                    checked={ext.isEnabled}
                    onChange={(e) => handleToggle(ext.id, e.target.checked)}
                    style={{ accentColor: 'var(--accent)' }}
                  />
                  Active
                </label>
                <IconButton label="Remove extension" onClick={() => handleRemove(ext.id)}>
                  <Trash2 size={13} />
                </IconButton>
              </div>
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </PageShell>
  );
};
```

### `src/main/action-layer/action-firewall.ts`

```typescript
import {
  ProposedAction,
  PageSnapshot,
  FirewallDecision,
  RiskTier,
  SiteTrustLevel
} from '../../shared/types';
import { RiskClassifier } from './risk-classifier';
import { TrustManager } from './trust-manager';
import { PermissionManager } from './permission-manager';
import { AuditLog } from './audit-log';

export class ActionFirewall {
  constructor(
    private classifier: RiskClassifier,
    private trustManager: TrustManager,
    private permissionManager: PermissionManager,
    private auditLog: AuditLog
  ) {}

  public async evaluateAction(
    action: ProposedAction,
    pageSnapshot: PageSnapshot
  ): Promise<FirewallDecision> {
    // Safe URL parsing — sentry:// and empty URLs must not crash the firewall
    let domain = action.url || '';
    try {
      if (action.url && (action.url.startsWith('http://') || action.url.startsWith('https://'))) {
        domain = new URL(action.url).hostname;
      }
    } catch {
      domain = action.url || 'unknown';
    }
    
    // 1. Check site trust
    const siteTrust = this.trustManager.getTrustLevel(action.url || 'about:blank');

    
    // 2. Classify action
    const classification = this.classifier.classifyAction(action, pageSnapshot, siteTrust);
    
    const decision: FirewallDecision = {
      actionId: action.id,
      allowed: false,
      requiresConfirmation: false,
      riskTier: classification.tier,
      classification,
      siteTrust,
      explanation: '',
      detailedReason: classification.reasons.join('; ')
    };

    // 3. Check permission grants
    const actionPattern = `${action.type}:${action.target}`;
    const matchedGrant = this.permissionManager.hasGrant(domain, actionPattern, classification.tier);
    if (matchedGrant) {
      decision.matchedGrant = matchedGrant;
    }

    // 4. Make decision
    if (siteTrust === SiteTrustLevel.BLOCKED) {
      decision.allowed = false;
      decision.explanation = 'This site is blocked by your security settings. No actions are allowed.';
    } else if (siteTrust === SiteTrustLevel.READ_ONLY && classification.tier !== RiskTier.LOW) {
      decision.allowed = false;
      decision.explanation = 'This site is marked as Read-Only. State-changing actions are blocked.';
    } else if (classification.tier === RiskTier.CRITICAL) {
      decision.allowed = false;
      decision.requiresConfirmation = true;
      decision.explanation = 'CRITICAL action requires explicit approval.';
    } else if (matchedGrant) {
      decision.allowed = true;
      decision.explanation = 'Action pre-approved by user grant.';
    } else if (classification.tier === RiskTier.LOW) {
      decision.allowed = true;
      decision.explanation = 'Low risk action allowed automatically.';
    } else if (classification.tier === RiskTier.MEDIUM) {
      decision.allowed = true;
      decision.explanation = 'Medium risk action allowed with undo option.';
    } else if (classification.tier === RiskTier.HIGH) {
      decision.allowed = false;
      decision.requiresConfirmation = true;
      decision.explanation = 'HIGH risk action requires confirmation.';
    }

    // 5. Log the decision
    this.auditLog.log({
      actionId: action.id,
      action,
      decision,
      wasExecuted: decision.allowed,
      wasUndone: false
    });

    return decision;
  }
}
```

### `src/renderer/components/NotificationCenter.tsx`

```tsx
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
  Bell,
  CheckCircle2,
  AlertTriangle,
  AlertOctagon,
  Info,
  X,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { AgentNotification } from '../../shared/types';
import { IconButton } from './ui/IconButton';
import { useHideNativeView } from '../hooks/useHideNativeView';
import './NotificationCenter.css';

interface NotificationCenterProps {
  notifications: AgentNotification[];
  onDismiss: (id: string) => void;
  onNotificationClick?: (notif: AgentNotification) => void;
  onActionClick?: (notifId: string, actionKey: string) => void;
  onClearAll?: () => void;
}

const timeAgo = (timestamp: number) => {
  const seconds = Math.floor((Date.now() - timestamp) / 1000);
  if (seconds < 60) return 'Just now';
  const minutes = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
  if (minutes < 60) return `${minutes}m ago`;
  const hours = Math.floor(minutes / 60);
  if (hours < 24) return `${hours}h ago`;
  return new Date(timestamp).toLocaleDateString();
};

export const NotificationCenter: React.FC<NotificationCenterProps> = ({
  notifications,
  onDismiss,
  onNotificationClick,
  onActionClick,
  onClearAll,
}) => {
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);

  // Hide native WebContentsView when notification drawer is open
  useHideNativeView(isOpen);

  // Auto dismiss non-critical toasts after 5 seconds
  useEffect(() => {
    const activeToasts = notifications.filter(
      (n) => n.type !== 'confirmation' && n.type !== 'security_alert'
    );
    const timers = activeToasts.map((n) =>
      setTimeout(() => {
        onDismiss(n.id);
      }, 5000)
    );
    return () => timers.forEach(clearTimeout);
  }, [notifications, onDismiss]);

  const unreadCount = notifications.filter((n) => !n.read).length;
  const recentToasts = notifications.slice(-2); // max 2 toasts

  const renderIcon = (type: AgentNotification['type']) => {
    switch (type) {
      case 'success':
        return <CheckCircle2 size={14} color="var(--ok)" />;
      case 'confirmation':
      case 'warning' as any:
        return <AlertTriangle size={14} color="var(--warn)" />;
      case 'error':
      case 'security_alert':
        return <AlertOctagon size={14} color="var(--danger)" />;
      case 'info':
      default:
        return <Info size={14} color="var(--accent)" />;
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="notification-center-wrapper">
      {/* Bell Button */}
      <div style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
        <IconButton
          label="Notifications"
          active={isOpen}
          onClick={() => setIsOpen(!isOpen)}
        >
          <Bell size={15} />
        </IconButton>
        {unreadCount > 0 && (
          <span className="bell-unread-badge">{unreadCount}</span>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Floating Toast Stack */}
      {recentToasts.length > 0 && !isOpen && (
        <div className="toast-stack">
          {recentToasts.map((notif) => (
            <div
              key={notif.id}
              className={`toast-item ${notif.type}`}
              onClick={() => onNotificationClick && onNotificationClick(notif)}
            >
              <span className="toast-icon">{renderIcon(notif.type)}</span>
              <div className="toast-content">
                <strong>{notif.title}</strong>
                <p>{notif.message}</p>
                {notif.actions && notif.actions.length > 0 && (
                  <div className="toast-actions">
                    {notif.actions.map((action) => (
                      <button
                        key={action.actionKey}
                        onClick={(e) => {
                          e.stopPropagation();
                          if (onActionClick) onActionClick(notif.id, action.actionKey);
                        }}
                        className="toast-action-btn"
                      >
                        {action.label}
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
              <IconButton
                label="Dismiss"
                onClick={(e) => {
                  e.stopPropagation();
                  onDismiss(notif.id);
                }}
                style={{ width: '20px', height: '20px' }}
              >
                <X size={12} />
              </IconButton>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Drawer */}
      {isOpen && (
        <div className="notification-drawer">
          <div className="drawer-header">
            <span style={{ fontWeight: 600, fontSize: '13px' }}>
              Notifications {unreadCount > 0 && `(${unreadCount})`}
            </span>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '6px', alignItems: 'center' }}>
              {notifications.length > 0 && onClearAll && (
                <button
                  onClick={onClearAll}
                  style={{
                    background: 'transparent',
                    border: 'none',
                    color: 'var(--text-3)',
                    fontSize: '11px',
                    cursor: 'pointer',
                  }}
                >
                  Clear all
                </button>
              )}
              <IconButton label="Close" onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)} style={{ width: '24px', height: '24px' }}>
                <X size={14} />
              </IconButton>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="drawer-body">
            {notifications.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="empty-notifs">No notifications</div>
            ) : (
              notifications.map((n) => (
                <div key={n.id} className={`drawer-notif-item ${n.type}`}>
                  <span className="notif-icon">{renderIcon(n.type)}</span>
                  <div className="notif-info">
                    <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'baseline' }}>
                      <strong>{n.title}</strong>
                      <span style={{ fontSize: '10px', color: 'var(--text-3)' }}>{timeAgo(n.timestamp)}</span>
                    </div>
                    <p>{n.message}</p>
                    {n.actions && n.actions.length > 0 && (
                      <div className="notif-actions">
                        {n.actions.map((action) => (
                          <button
                            key={action.actionKey}
                            onClick={() => {
                              if (onActionClick) onActionClick(n.id, action.actionKey);
                            }}
                            className="notif-action-btn"
                          >
                            {action.label}
                          </button>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};
```

### `src/main/action-layer/trust-manager.ts`

```typescript
import { SiteTrustEntry, SiteTrustLevel } from '../../shared/types';
import * as fs from 'fs';
import * as path from 'path';

export class TrustManager {
  private trustList: Map<string, SiteTrustEntry> = new Map();
  
  public readonly HIGHLY_RESTRICTED_DEFAULTS: string[] = [
    // Banking
    'chase.com', 'bankofamerica.com', 'wellsfargo.com', 'citibank.com',
    // Email
    'gmail.com', 'outlook.com', 'mail.yahoo.com', 'protonmail.com',
    // Password managers
    'bitwarden.com', '1password.com', 'lastpass.com', 'dashlane.com',
    // Payment
    'paypal.com', 'venmo.com', 'cash.app', 'stripe.com',
    // Financial
    'robinhood.com', 'fidelity.com', 'schwab.com', 'vanguard.com',
    // Social
    'facebook.com', 'twitter.com', 'instagram.com', 'linkedin.com',
    // Government
    'irs.gov', 'ssa.gov', 'healthcare.gov',
  ];

  constructor(private userDataPath?: string) {
    this.loadTrustList();
  }

  public getTrustLevel(url: string): SiteTrustLevel {
    if (!url || url.startsWith('sentry://') || url.startsWith('about:') || url === 'sentry://newtab') {
      return SiteTrustLevel.READ_ACT; // Internal pages are always trusted
    }
    const domain = this.extractDomain(url);
    
    // Check exact domain
    if (this.trustList.has(domain)) {
      return this.trustList.get(domain)!.trustLevel;
    }
    
    // Check base domain (subdomain handling)
    const baseDomain = this.extractBaseDomain(domain);
    if (baseDomain !== domain && this.trustList.has(baseDomain)) {
      return this.trustList.get(baseDomain)!.trustLevel;
    }
    
    return this.getDefaultLevel(domain);
  }

  public getDefaultLevel(domain: string): SiteTrustLevel {
    const baseDomain = this.extractBaseDomain(domain);
    if (this.HIGHLY_RESTRICTED_DEFAULTS.includes(baseDomain) || this.HIGHLY_RESTRICTED_DEFAULTS.includes(domain)) {
      return SiteTrustLevel.HIGHLY_RESTRICTED;
    }
    return SiteTrustLevel.READ_ACT;
  }

  public setTrustLevel(domain: string, level: SiteTrustLevel, notes?: string): void {
    const entry: SiteTrustEntry = {
      domain,
      trustLevel: level,
      setBy: 'user',
      lastModified: Date.now(),
      notes,
    };
    this.trustList.set(domain, entry);
    this.saveTrustList();
  }

  public getAllTrustEntries(): SiteTrustEntry[] {
    return Array.from(this.trustList.values());
  }

  private extractDomain(url: string): string {
    try {
      if (!url.startsWith('http')) return url;
      const urlObj = new URL(url);
      return urlObj.hostname;
    } catch {
      return url;
    }
  }
  
  private extractBaseDomain(domain: string): string {
    const parts = domain.split('.');
    if (parts.length > 2) {
      return parts.slice(-2).join('.'); // very basic, enough for most cases
    }
    return domain;
  }

  private loadTrustList() {
    if (!this.userDataPath) return;
    try {
      const file = path.join(this.userDataPath, 'trust-list.json');
      if (fs.existsSync(file)) {
        const data = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf-8'));
        for (const entry of data) {
          this.trustList.set(entry.domain, entry);
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('Failed to load trust list', e);
    }
  }

  private saveTrustList() {
    if (!this.userDataPath) return;
    try {
      const file = path.join(this.userDataPath, 'trust-list.json');
      fs.writeFileSync(file, JSON.stringify(this.getAllTrustEntries(), null, 2));
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('Failed to save trust list', e);
    }
  }
}
```

### `src/renderer/pages/Settings.tsx`

```tsx
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Check } from 'lucide-react';
import { UserSettings } from '../../shared/types';
import { PageShell } from '../components/ui/PageShell';
import { Card } from '../components/ui/Card';
import { Button } from '../components/ui/Button';
import { ForgetControls } from '../components/ForgetControls';

interface SettingsProps {
  onClearHistory: () => void;
}

export const Settings: React.FC<SettingsProps> = ({ onClearHistory }) => {
  const [settings, setSettings] = useState<UserSettings>({
    searchEngine: 'google',
    startupBehavior: 'newtab',
    theme: 'dark',
    showBookmarksBar: true,
    defaultDownloadPath: '',
    askDownloadPath: false,
  });
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (window.sentry) {
      window.sentry.settings.get().then((data: UserSettings) => {
        if (data) setSettings(data);
      });
    }
  }, []);

  const handleUpdate = (updates: Partial<UserSettings>) => {
    const updated = { ...settings, ...updates };
    setSettings(updated);
    if (window.sentry) {
      window.sentry.settings.update(updates);
    }
    setNotice('Settings saved.');
    setTimeout(() => setNotice(null), 2500);
  };

  const handleClearData = () => {
    onClearHistory();
    setNotice('Browsing data cleared.');
    setTimeout(() => setNotice(null), 3000);
  };

  return (
    <PageShell
      title="Settings"
      description="Configure browser preferences, search, privacy, and developer tools."
    >
      {notice && (
        <div
          style={{
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            gap: '6px',
            padding: '8px 12px',
            borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)',
            fontSize: '12px',
            background: 'var(--ok-bg)',
            color: 'var(--ok)',
            border: '1px solid rgba(63, 178, 127, 0.25)',
          }}
        >
          <Check size={14} />
          <span>{notice}</span>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Search & Navigation */}
      <Card style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '12px' }}>
        <h2 style={{ fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--text)' }}>
          Search & startup
        </h2>

        <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>
          <div>
            <label style={{ fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: 500, color: 'var(--text)' }}>
              Default search engine
            </label>
            <p style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--text-3)' }}>
              Used when entering queries in the address bar.
            </p>
          </div>
          <select
            value={settings.searchEngine}
            onChange={(e) => handleUpdate({ searchEngine: e.target.value as UserSettings['searchEngine'] })}
            style={{
              background: 'var(--bg-1)',
              color: 'var(--text)',
              border: '1px solid var(--border)',
              padding: '4px 8px',
              borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)',
              fontSize: '12px',
              fontFamily: 'inherit',
              outline: 'none',
            }}
          >
            <option value="google">Google</option>
            <option value="duckduckgo">DuckDuckGo</option>
            <option value="bing">Bing</option>
          </select>
        </div>

        <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', paddingTop: '8px', borderTop: '1px solid var(--border)' }}>
          <div>
            <label style={{ fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: 500, color: 'var(--text)' }}>
              On startup
            </label>
            <p style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--text-3)' }}>
              Choose what happens when opening a new browser window.
            </p>
          </div>
          <select
            value={settings.startupBehavior}
            onChange={(e) => handleUpdate({ startupBehavior: e.target.value as UserSettings['startupBehavior'] })}
            style={{
              background: 'var(--bg-1)',
              color: 'var(--text)',
              border: '1px solid var(--border)',
              padding: '4px 8px',
              borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)',
              fontSize: '12px',
              fontFamily: 'inherit',
              outline: 'none',
            }}
          >
            <option value="newtab">Open new tab page</option>
            <option value="restore">Restore previous session</option>
          </select>
        </div>

        <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', paddingTop: '8px', borderTop: '1px solid var(--border)' }}>
          <div>
            <label style={{ fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: 500, color: 'var(--text)' }}>
              Bookmarks bar
            </label>
            <p style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--text-3)' }}>
              Show the bookmarks toolbar beneath the address bar when bookmarks exist.
            </p>
          </div>
          <label style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px', cursor: 'pointer' }}>
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={settings.showBookmarksBar ?? true}
              onChange={(e) => handleUpdate({ showBookmarksBar: e.target.checked })}
              style={{ width: '16px', height: '16px', accentColor: 'var(--accent)' }}
            />
          </label>
        </div>
      </Card>

      {/* Privacy & Browsing Data */}
      <Card style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '12px' }}>
        <h2 style={{ fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--text)' }}>
          Browsing data
        </h2>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>
          <div>
            <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: 500, color: 'var(--text)' }}>
              Clear browsing history
            </span>
            <p style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--text-3)' }}>
              Removes all visited URLs and history entries.
            </p>
          </div>
          <Button variant="danger" size="sm" onClick={handleClearData}>
            Clear history
          </Button>
        </div>
      </Card>

      {/* Memory Management */}
      <ForgetControls />

      {/* Advanced & Developer Mode */}
      <Card style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '10px' }}>
        <h2 style={{ fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--text)' }}>
          Developer & research tools
        </h2>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'flex-start', justifyContent: 'space-between', gap: '16px' }}>
          <div>
            <label style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px', cursor: 'pointer', fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: 500, color: 'var(--text)' }}>
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                checked={settings.developerMode ?? false}
                onChange={(e) => handleUpdate({ developerMode: e.target.checked })}
                style={{ width: '16px', height: '16px', accentColor: 'var(--accent)' }}
              />
              Enable developer mode
            </label>
            <p style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--text-3)', marginTop: '4px', lineHeight: 1.4 }}>
              Exposes step execution traces, CDP latency metrics, Ollama inspection, and security benchmark suites.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </Card>
    </PageShell>
  );
};
```

### `src/main/cdp/cdp-manager.ts`

```typescript
/**
 * CDPManager — Task 3.1
 *
 * Manages one CDP debugger session per tab using Electron's built-in
 * `webContents.debugger` API.  Falls back gracefully if a session is
 * unavailable — never crashes the browser.
 */

import { WebContentsView } from 'electron';

export interface CDPConnection {
  tabId: string;
  /** Whether the debugger is currently attached */
  isConnected: boolean;
  /** Underlying webContents ID (for bookkeeping) */
  webContentsId: number;
}

export class CDPManager {
  private connections: Map<string, CDPConnection> = new Map();
  /** Reference to view lookup — injected by TabManager */
  private viewResolver: (tabId: string) => WebContentsView | undefined;

  constructor(viewResolver: (tabId: string) => WebContentsView | undefined) {
    this.viewResolver = viewResolver;
  }

  // ─── Public API ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  /**
   * Attach a CDP debugger session to the given tab.
   * Safe to call multiple times — idempotent.
   */
  public async attach(tabId: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const view = this.viewResolver(tabId);
    if (!view) {
      console.warn(`[CDPManager] Tab ${tabId} not found — cannot attach`);
      return false;
    }

    const wc = view.webContents;
    const existing = this.connections.get(tabId);

    // If already connected (and not destroyed), do nothing
    if (existing?.isConnected && !wc.isDestroyed()) {
      return true;
    }

    try {
      if (!wc.debugger.isAttached()) {
        wc.debugger.attach('1.3');
      }

      const conn: CDPConnection = {
        tabId,
        isConnected: true,
        webContentsId: wc.id,
      };
      this.connections.set(tabId, conn);

      // Listen for detach events so we can mark the session as disconnected
      wc.debugger.on('detach', (_event, reason) => {
        console.warn(`[CDPManager] Debugger detached from tab ${tabId}: ${reason}`);
        const c = this.connections.get(tabId);
        if (c) c.isConnected = false;
      });

      // Auto-reconnect on same-process navigation (CDP sessions survive these)
      wc.on('did-navigate', () => {
        const c = this.connections.get(tabId);
        if (c) c.isConnected = wc.debugger.isAttached();
      });

      return true;
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(`[CDPManager] Failed to attach to tab ${tabId}:`, err);
      return false;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Detach and clean up the CDP session for a tab.
   * Called when the tab is closed.
   */
  public detach(tabId: string): void {
    const view = this.viewResolver(tabId);
    if (view && !view.webContents.isDestroyed()) {
      try {
        if (view.webContents.debugger.isAttached()) {
          view.webContents.debugger.detach();
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn(`[CDPManager] Error detaching tab ${tabId}:`, err);
      }
    }
    this.connections.delete(tabId);
  }

  /**
   * Send a CDP command to the given tab.
   * Returns undefined and logs a warning if the session is not ready.
   */
  public async send<T = unknown>(
    tabId: string,
    method: string,
    params?: Record<string, unknown>,
  ): Promise<T | undefined> {
    // Ensure session is attached
    const attached = await this.attach(tabId);
    if (!attached) return undefined;

    const view = this.viewResolver(tabId);
    if (!view || view.webContents.isDestroyed()) return undefined;

    try {
      const result = await view.webContents.debugger.sendCommand(method, params ?? {});
      return result as T;
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
      // Suppress noise from harmless "not attached" errors during rapid navigation
      if (!message.includes('detached') && !message.includes('Cannot send')) {
        console.error(`[CDPManager] CDP error on ${method} for tab ${tabId}:`, message);
      }
      return undefined;
    }
  }

  /** Check whether a tab's CDP session is currently active */
  public isConnected(tabId: string): boolean {
    return this.connections.get(tabId)?.isConnected ?? false;
  }

  /** Returns metadata for all active connections (useful for debugging) */
  public getConnections(): CDPConnection[] {
    return Array.from(this.connections.values());
  }
}
```

### `src/renderer/pages/ModelSettings.tsx`

```tsx
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Cpu, Check } from 'lucide-react';
import { PageShell } from '../components/ui/PageShell';
import { Card } from '../components/ui/Card';
import { Button } from '../components/ui/Button';
import { Badge } from '../components/ui/Badge';

export const ModelSettings: React.FC = () => {
  const [routingMode, setRoutingMode] = useState('auto');
  const [localModel, setLocalModel] = useState('llama3.1:8b');
  const [cloudModel, setCloudModel] = useState('gpt-4o');
  const [ollamaModels, setOllamaModels] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [ollamaOnline, setOllamaOnline] = useState<boolean | null>(null);
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const sentry = (window as any).sentry;
    sentry?.agent?.getModelConfig?.().then((cfg: any) => {
      if (cfg) {
        setRoutingMode(cfg.routingMode || 'auto');
        setLocalModel(cfg.localModel || 'llama3.1:8b');
        setCloudModel(cfg.cloudModel || 'gpt-4o');
      }
    });
    sentry?.agent?.checkOllama?.().then((ok: boolean) => setOllamaOnline(ok));
    sentry?.agent?.listOllamaModels?.().then((models: string[]) => setOllamaModels(models || []));
  }, []);

  const handleSave = async () => {
    const sentry = (window as any).sentry;
    await sentry?.agent?.setModelConfig?.({ localModel, cloudModel, routingMode });
    setSaved(true);
    setTimeout(() => setSaved(false), 2500);
  };

  const routingOptions = [
    { value: 'auto', label: 'Auto (Recommended)', desc: 'Routes by task complexity. Uses local for simple DOM actions and cloud for multi-step reasoning.' },
    { value: 'local_only', label: 'Local only', desc: 'All processing stays on your device via Ollama. Maximum privacy, zero API costs.' },
    { value: 'prefer_local', label: 'Prefer local', desc: 'Uses local Ollama by default, only failing over to cloud if the local model stalls.' },
    { value: 'cloud_only', label: 'Cloud only', desc: 'Routes all prompts to frontier models (OpenAI/Anthropic) using your API keys.' },
  ];

  return (
    <PageShell
      title="AI Models"
      description="Configure model routing between local on-device LLMs and cloud providers."
      headerActions={
        <Button variant="primary" size="sm" onClick={handleSave} icon={saved ? <Check size={13} /> : undefined}>
          {saved ? 'Saved' : 'Save changes'}
        </Button>
      }
    >
      {/* Routing Mode */}
      <Card style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '12px' }}>
        <h2 style={{ fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--text)' }}>
          Routing mode
        </h2>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '8px' }}>
          {routingOptions.map((opt) => (
            <label
              key={opt.value}
              style={{
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'flex-start',
                gap: '10px',
                padding: '10px 12px',
                borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)',
                background: routingMode === opt.value ? 'var(--bg-3)' : 'var(--bg-1)',
                border: `1px solid ${routingMode === opt.value ? 'var(--accent)' : 'var(--border)'}`,
                cursor: 'pointer',
                transition: 'background 0.1s ease, border-color 0.1s ease',
              }}
            >
              <input
                type="radio"
                name="routing"
                value={opt.value}
                checked={routingMode === opt.value}
                onChange={(e) => setRoutingMode(e.target.value)}
                style={{ marginTop: '2px', accentColor: 'var(--accent)' }}
              />
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '2px' }}>
                <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: 500, color: 'var(--text)' }}>
                  {opt.label}
                </span>
                <span style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--text-2)', lineHeight: 1.4 }}>
                  {opt.desc}
                </span>
              </div>
            </label>
          ))}
        </div>
      </Card>

      {/* Local Model (Ollama) */}
      <Card style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '12px' }}>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>
          <div>
            <h2 style={{ fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--text)' }}>
              Local model (Ollama)
            </h2>
            <p style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--text-3)', marginTop: '2px' }}>
              Connects to your local Ollama daemon on localhost:11434.
            </p>
          </div>
          <Badge variant={ollamaOnline ? 'ok' : ollamaOnline === false ? 'danger' : 'neutral'} dot>
            {ollamaOnline === null ? 'Checking…' : ollamaOnline ? 'Online' : 'Offline'}
          </Badge>
        </div>

        {ollamaModels.length > 0 ? (
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '6px' }}>
            <span style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--text-3)' }}>Installed models:</span>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '6px', flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
              {ollamaModels.map((m) => (
                <button
                  key={m}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setLocalModel(m)}
                  style={{
```
