```tsx
                    padding: '4px 10px',
                    borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)',
                    fontSize: '11px',
                    fontFamily: 'inherit',
                    cursor: 'pointer',
                    background: localModel === m ? 'var(--accent-bg)' : 'var(--bg-1)',
                    color: localModel === m ? 'var(--accent)' : 'var(--text-2)',
                    border: `1px solid ${localModel === m ? 'var(--accent)' : 'var(--border)'}`,
                  }}
                >
                  {m}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <p style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--text-3)' }}>
            No Ollama models detected. Run <code style={{ color: 'var(--accent)' }}>ollama pull llama3.1:8b</code> in your terminal.
          </p>
        )}
      </Card>

      {/* Cloud Model */}
      <Card style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '12px' }}>
        <h2 style={{ fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--text)' }}>
          Cloud fallback model
        </h2>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>
          <div>
            <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: 500, color: 'var(--text)' }}>
              Provider model
            </span>
            <p style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--text-3)' }}>
              Frontier model used when cloud execution is required.
            </p>
          </div>
          <select
            value={cloudModel}
            onChange={(e) => setCloudModel(e.target.value)}
            style={{
              background: 'var(--bg-1)',
              color: 'var(--text)',
              border: '1px solid var(--border)',
              padding: '4px 8px',
              borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)',
              fontSize: '12px',
              fontFamily: 'inherit',
              outline: 'none',
            }}
          >
            <option value="gpt-4o">OpenAI gpt-4o</option>
            <option value="gpt-4o-mini">OpenAI gpt-4o-mini</option>
            <option value="claude-3-5-sonnet-20241022">Anthropic Claude 3.5 Sonnet</option>
            <option value="claude-3-5-haiku-20241022">Anthropic Claude 3.5 Haiku</option>
          </select>
        </div>
      </Card>
    </PageShell>
  );
};
```

### `src/renderer/overlays/ActionLog.tsx`

```tsx
import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { History, X } from 'lucide-react';
import { ActionLogEntry, RiskTier } from '../../shared/types';
import { WhyExplanation } from '../components/WhyExplanation';
import { useHideNativeView } from '../hooks/useHideNativeView';
import { IconButton } from '../components/ui/IconButton';
import './ActionLog.css';

interface ActionLogProps {
  entries: ActionLogEntry[];
  onClearLog?: () => void;
}

export const ActionLog: React.FC<ActionLogProps> = ({ entries, onClearLog }) => {
  const [filterTier, setFilterTier] = useState<string>('all');
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [selectedWhyEntry, setSelectedWhyEntry] = useState<ActionLogEntry | null>(null);
  const [selectedSnapshotEntry, setSelectedSnapshotEntry] = useState<ActionLogEntry | null>(null);
  const logEndRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  // Hide native WebContentsView when Time Machine snapshot modal is open
  useHideNativeView(!!selectedSnapshotEntry);

  useEffect(() => {
    logEndRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
  }, [entries.length]);

  const filteredEntries = entries.filter((e) => {
    const tier = e.decision?.riskTier || e.action.riskTier || RiskTier.LOW;
    if (filterTier !== 'all' && tier !== filterTier) return false;
    if (searchQuery) {
      const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
      const matchAction = e.action.type.toLowerCase().includes(q);
      const matchTarget = (e.action.target || '').toLowerCase().includes(q);
      const matchReason = (e.action.reason || '').toLowerCase().includes(q);
      return matchAction || matchTarget || matchReason;
    }
    return true;
  });

  const getTierDot = (tier: RiskTier) => {
    switch (tier) {
      case RiskTier.LOW: return <span className="status-dot ok" />;
      case RiskTier.MEDIUM:
      case RiskTier.HIGH: return <span className="status-dot warn" />;
      case RiskTier.CRITICAL: return <span className="status-dot danger" />;
      default: return <span className="status-dot neutral" />;
    }
  };

  const exportLog = (format: 'json' | 'csv') => {
    let content = '';
    let mimeType = 'text/plain';
    const filename = `sentry-actions-${Date.now()}.${format}`;

    if (format === 'json') {
      content = JSON.stringify(entries, null, 2);
      mimeType = 'application/json';
    } else {
      const headers = ['Timestamp', 'Type', 'Target', 'RiskTier', 'Allowed', 'Reason'];
      const rows = entries.map((e) => [
        new Date(e.timestamp || Date.now()).toISOString(),
        e.action.type,
        `"${(e.action.target || '').replace(/"/g, '""')}"`,
        e.decision?.riskTier || e.action.riskTier || RiskTier.LOW,
        e.decision?.allowed ?? true,
        `"${(e.action.reason || '').replace(/"/g, '""')}"`
      ]);
      content = [headers.join(','), ...rows.map(r => r.join(','))].join('\n');
      mimeType = 'text/csv';
    }

    const blob = new Blob([content], { type: mimeType });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url;
    a.download = filename;
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  return (
    <div className="action-log-container">
      <div className="action-log-content">
        <div className="log-toolbar">
          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder="Search actions..."
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
            className="log-search-input"
          />
          <select
            value={filterTier}
            onChange={(e) => setFilterTier(e.target.value)}
            className="log-filter-select"
          >
            <option value="all">All tiers</option>
            <option value={RiskTier.LOW}>Low</option>
            <option value={RiskTier.MEDIUM}>Medium</option>
            <option value={RiskTier.HIGH}>High</option>
            <option value={RiskTier.CRITICAL}>Critical</option>
          </select>
          <button className="export-btn" onClick={() => exportLog('json')}>JSON</button>
          <button className="export-btn" onClick={() => exportLog('csv')}>CSV</button>
          {onClearLog && <button className="clear-btn" onClick={onClearLog}>Clear</button>}
        </div>

        <div className="log-timeline" role="log" aria-label="Agent action log" aria-live="polite">
          {filteredEntries.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="empty-log-state">No agent actions recorded yet.</div>
          ) : (
            filteredEntries.map((entry, idx) => {
              const tier = entry.decision?.riskTier || entry.action.riskTier || RiskTier.LOW;
              const timeStr = new Date(entry.timestamp || Date.now()).toLocaleTimeString();
              return (
                <div 
                  key={idx} 
                  className="timeline-item" 
                  role="listitem" 
                  aria-label={`Action ${entry.action.type} on ${entry.action.target || 'page'}, ${tier} risk`}
                >
                  <span className="time-col">{timeStr}</span>
                  <span className="tier-col" title={`Risk tier: ${tier}`}>
                    {getTierDot(tier)}
                  </span>
                  <div className="action-desc" onClick={() => {
                    if (entry.result?.beforeSnapshot) {
                      setSelectedSnapshotEntry(entry);
                    }
                  }}>
                    <span className="action-type">{entry.action.type}</span>
                    <span className="action-target">{entry.action.target || 'page'}</span>
                    {entry.action.value && <code className="action-val">{entry.action.value}</code>}
                    {!entry.executed && <span className="blocked-tag">Blocked</span>}
                  </div>
                  <button
                    className="why-btn"
                    onClick={(e) => {
                      e.stopPropagation();
                      setSelectedWhyEntry(entry);
                    }}
                  >
                    Why?
                  </button>
                </div>
              );
            })
          )}
          <div ref={logEndRef} />
        </div>
      </div>

      {selectedWhyEntry && (
        <WhyExplanation
          entry={selectedWhyEntry}
          onClose={() => setSelectedWhyEntry(null)}
        />
      )}

      {selectedSnapshotEntry && (
        <div className="time-machine-modal-backdrop" onClick={() => setSelectedSnapshotEntry(null)}>
          <div className="time-machine-card" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
            <div className="time-machine-header">
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
                <History size={16} style={{ color: 'var(--accent)' }} />
                <span style={{ fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600 }}>Step snapshot</span>
              </div>
              <IconButton label="Close" onClick={() => setSelectedSnapshotEntry(null)} style={{ width: '24px', height: '24px' }}>
                <X size={14} />
              </IconButton>
            </div>
            <div className="time-machine-body">
              <p><strong>Action:</strong> {selectedSnapshotEntry.action.type} on {selectedSnapshotEntry.action.target}</p>
              <p><strong>Reason:</strong> {selectedSnapshotEntry.action.reason}</p>
              <div className="snapshot-preview-box">
                <h4>URL:</h4>
                <code>{selectedSnapshotEntry.result?.beforeSnapshot?.url || selectedSnapshotEntry.action.url}</code>
                <h4>Page Title:</h4>
                <p>{selectedSnapshotEntry.result?.beforeSnapshot?.title || 'Snapshot available'}</p>
                <h4>Interactive Elements:</h4>
                <p>{selectedSnapshotEntry.result?.beforeSnapshot?.interactiveElements?.length ?? 'Available'}</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};
```

### `src/renderer/components/TabBar.tsx`

```tsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Plus, X, Pin } from 'lucide-react';
import { TabItem } from '../../shared/types';
import { useHideNativeView } from '../hooks/useHideNativeView';

interface TabBarProps {
  tabs: TabItem[];
  activeTabId: string | null;
  onSwitchTab: (id: string) => void;
  onCloseTab: (id: string) => void;
  onCreateTab: () => void;
  onDuplicateTab: (id: string) => void;
  onTogglePinTab: (id: string) => void;
}

export const TabBar: React.FC<TabBarProps> = ({
  tabs,
  activeTabId,
  onSwitchTab,
  onCloseTab,
  onCreateTab,
  onDuplicateTab,
  onTogglePinTab,
}) => {
  const [contextMenu, setContextMenu] = useState<{ x: number; y: number; tabId: string } | null>(null);

  // Automatically hide native WebContentsView when context menu is open
  useHideNativeView(!!contextMenu);

  const handleContextMenu = (e: React.MouseEvent, tabId: string) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setContextMenu({ x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY, tabId });
  };

  const closeContextMenu = () => setContextMenu(null);

  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display: 'flex',
        alignItems: 'center',
        background: 'var(--bg-0)',
        padding: '6px 8px 0 8px',
        gap: '4px',
        userSelect: 'none',
        overflowX: 'auto',
        WebkitAppRegion: 'drag',
      } as React.CSSProperties}
      onClick={closeContextMenu}
    >
      {tabs.map((tab) => {
        const isActive = tab.id === activeTabId;
        return (
          <div
            key={tab.id}
            onClick={() => onSwitchTab(tab.id)}
            onAuxClick={(e) => {
              if (e.button === 1) onCloseTab(tab.id); // Middle click to close
            }}
            onContextMenu={(e) => handleContextMenu(e, tab.id)}
            title={tab.title || 'New Tab'}
            style={{
              display: 'flex',
              alignItems: 'center',
              gap: '6px',
              padding: tab.isPinned ? '0 8px' : '0 10px',
              maxWidth: tab.isPinned ? '38px' : '190px',
              minWidth: tab.isPinned ? '38px' : '110px',
              height: '32px',
              borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm) var(--radius-sm) 0 0',
              background: isActive ? 'var(--bg-1)' : 'transparent',
              color: isActive ? 'var(--text)' : 'var(--text-2)',
              fontSize: '12px',
              cursor: 'pointer',
              borderTop: isActive ? '2px solid var(--accent)' : '2px solid transparent',
              borderLeft: isActive ? '1px solid var(--border)' : '1px solid transparent',
              borderRight: isActive ? '1px solid var(--border)' : '1px solid transparent',
              transition: 'background 0.12s ease, color 0.12s ease',
              position: 'relative',
              WebkitAppRegion: 'no-drag',
              flexShrink: 0,
            } as React.CSSProperties}
          >
            {/* Status dot or pin icon */}
            {tab.isPinned ? (
              <Pin size={12} style={{ color: 'var(--text-2)', transform: 'rotate(45deg)' }} />
            ) : (
              <span
                style={{
                  width: '7px',
                  height: '7px',
                  borderRadius: '50%',
                  background: tab.isLoading ? 'var(--accent)' : isActive ? 'var(--text)' : 'var(--text-3)',
                  flexShrink: 0,
                }}
              />
            )}

            {!tab.isPinned && (
              <span
                style={{
                  overflow: 'hidden',
                  textOverflow: 'ellipsis',
                  whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                  flexGrow: 1,
                  fontWeight: isActive ? 500 : 400,
                }}
              >
                {tab.title || 'New Tab'}
              </span>
            )}

            {!tab.isPinned && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={(e) => {
                  e.stopPropagation();
                  onCloseTab(tab.id);
                }}
                style={{
                  border: 'none',
                  background: 'transparent',
                  color: 'var(--text-3)',
                  cursor: 'pointer',
                  borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)',
                  width: '16px',
                  height: '16px',
                  display: 'inline-flex',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  justifyContent: 'center',
                  padding: 0,
                  WebkitAppRegion: 'no-drag',
                  flexShrink: 0,
                } as React.CSSProperties}
                onMouseEnter={(e) => {
                  e.currentTarget.style.color = 'var(--danger)';
                  e.currentTarget.style.background = 'var(--danger-bg)';
                }}
                onMouseLeave={(e) => {
                  e.currentTarget.style.color = 'var(--text-3)';
                  e.currentTarget.style.background = 'transparent';
                }}
              >
                <X size={11} />
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        );
      })}

      {/* New Tab Button */}
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={onCreateTab}
        title="New Tab (Ctrl+T)"
        style={{
          border: 'none',
          background: 'transparent',
          color: 'var(--text-2)',
          width: '26px',
          height: '26px',
          borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)',
          cursor: 'pointer',
          display: 'inline-flex',
          alignItems: 'center',
          justifyContent: 'center',
          WebkitAppRegion: 'no-drag',
          flexShrink: 0,
          transition: 'background 0.12s ease',
        } as React.CSSProperties}
        onMouseEnter={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.background = 'var(--bg-3)')}
        onMouseLeave={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.background = 'transparent')}
      >
        <Plus size={14} />
      </button>

      {/* Context Menu Popup */}
      {contextMenu && (
        <div
          style={{
            position: 'fixed',
            top: contextMenu.y,
            left: contextMenu.x,
            background: 'var(--bg-1)',
            border: '1px solid var(--border)',
            borderRadius: 'var(--radius-md)',
            boxShadow: '0 8px 24px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5)',
            zIndex: 10000,
            padding: '4px',
            width: '140px',
          }}
          onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
        >
          <div
            style={{
              padding: '6px 10px',
              cursor: 'pointer',
              fontSize: '12px',
              color: 'var(--text)',
              borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)',
            }}
            onMouseEnter={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.background = 'var(--bg-3)')}
            onMouseLeave={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.background = 'transparent')}
            onClick={() => {
              onCloseTab(contextMenu.tabId);
              closeContextMenu();
            }}
          >
            Close Tab
          </div>
          <div
            style={{
              padding: '6px 10px',
              cursor: 'pointer',
              fontSize: '12px',
              color: 'var(--text)',
              borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)',
            }}
            onMouseEnter={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.background = 'var(--bg-3)')}
            onMouseLeave={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.background = 'transparent')}
            onClick={() => {
              onDuplicateTab(contextMenu.tabId);
              closeContextMenu();
            }}
          >
            Duplicate
          </div>
          <div
            style={{
              padding: '6px 10px',
              cursor: 'pointer',
              fontSize: '12px',
              color: 'var(--text)',
              borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)',
            }}
            onMouseEnter={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.background = 'var(--bg-3)')}
            onMouseLeave={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.background = 'transparent')}
            onClick={() => {
              onTogglePinTab(contextMenu.tabId);
              closeContextMenu();
            }}
          >
            Pin / Unpin
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};
```

### `src/renderer/pages/RecipeManager.tsx`

```tsx
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Play, Trash2, Globe, Clock, Check, AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { Recipe } from '../../shared/types';
import { PageShell } from '../components/ui/PageShell';
import { Card } from '../components/ui/Card';
import { Button } from '../components/ui/Button';
import { Modal } from '../components/ui/Modal';
import { Badge } from '../components/ui/Badge';

export const RecipeManager: React.FC = () => {
  const [recipes, setRecipes] = useState<Recipe[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [feedback, setFeedback] = useState<{ message: string; type: 'ok' | 'error' } | null>(null);

  // Run modal state
  const [runningRecipe, setRunningRecipe] = useState<Recipe | null>(null);
  const [variableValues, setVariableValues] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});

  // Delete modal state
  const [deletingRecipeId, setDeletingRecipeId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadRecipes();
  }, []);

  const loadRecipes = async () => {
    try {
      const data = await window.sentry?.agent?.getRecipes();
      if (data) setRecipes(data);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(e);
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const openRunModal = (recipe: Recipe) => {
    if (recipe.variables.length === 0) {
      executeRun(recipe, {});
      return;
    }
    const defaults: Record<string, string> = {};
    recipe.variables.forEach((v) => {
      defaults[v.name] = v.defaultValue || '';
    });
    setVariableValues(defaults);
    setRunningRecipe(recipe);
  };

  const executeRun = async (recipe: Recipe, vars: Record<string, string>) => {
    try {
      await window.sentry?.agent?.replayRecipe(recipe.id, vars);
      setFeedback({ message: `Recipe "${recipe.name}" started successfully.`, type: 'ok' });
    } catch (e: any) {
      setFeedback({ message: `Replay failed: ${e.message}`, type: 'error' });
    } finally {
      setRunningRecipe(null);
      setTimeout(() => setFeedback(null), 4000);
    }
  };

  const confirmDelete = async () => {
    if (!deletingRecipeId) return;
    try {
      await window.sentry?.agent?.deleteRecipe(deletingRecipeId);
      loadRecipes();
      setFeedback({ message: 'Recipe removed.', type: 'ok' });
    } catch {
      setFeedback({ message: 'Failed to delete recipe.', type: 'error' });
    } finally {
      setDeletingRecipeId(null);
      setTimeout(() => setFeedback(null), 3000);
    }
  };

  return (
    <PageShell
      title="Recipes"
      description="Automate repeatable web workflows with saved multi-step tasks."
    >
      {feedback && (
        <div
          style={{
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            gap: '8px',
            padding: '8px 12px',
            borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)',
            fontSize: '12px',
            background: feedback.type === 'ok' ? 'var(--ok-bg)' : 'var(--danger-bg)',
            color: feedback.type === 'ok' ? 'var(--ok)' : 'var(--danger)',
            border: `1px solid ${feedback.type === 'ok' ? 'rgba(63, 178, 127, 0.25)' : 'rgba(229, 72, 77, 0.25)'}`,
          }}
        >
          {feedback.type === 'ok' ? <Check size={14} /> : <AlertCircle size={14} />}
          <span>{feedback.message}</span>
        </div>
      )}

      {isLoading ? (
        <Card style={{ textAlign: 'center', color: 'var(--text-3)', padding: '32px' }}>
          Loading recipes…
        </Card>
      ) : recipes.length === 0 ? (
        <Card style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '32px', color: 'var(--text-2)' }}>
          <p style={{ fontWeight: 500, color: 'var(--text)' }}>No saved recipes yet</p>
          <p style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: 'var(--text-3)', marginTop: '4px' }}>
            Complete an agent task and click "Save as recipe" in the sidebar to create one.
          </p>
        </Card>
      ) : (
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '8px' }}>
          {recipes.map((r) => (
            <Card
              key={r.id}
              style={{
                display: 'flex',
                flexDirection: 'column',
                gap: '8px',
              }}
            >
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'flex-start' }}>
                <div>
                  <h3 style={{ fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--text)' }}>
                    {r.name}
                  </h3>
                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '12px', marginTop: '4px', fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--text-3)' }}>
                    <span style={{ display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '4px' }}>
                      <Globe size={11} /> {r.sitePatterns.join(', ') || 'Any site'}
                    </span>
                    <span style={{ display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '4px' }}>
                      <Clock size={11} /> Used {r.useCount} times
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '6px' }}>
                  <Button
                    variant="primary"
                    size="sm"
                    onClick={() => openRunModal(r)}
                    icon={<Play size={12} />}
                  >
                    Run
                  </Button>
                  <Button
                    variant="danger"
                    size="sm"
                    onClick={() => setDeletingRecipeId(r.id)}
                    icon={<Trash2 size={12} />}
                  >
                    Delete
                  </Button>
                </div>
              </div>

              {r.variables.length > 0 && (
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '6px', alignItems: 'center', flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
                  <span style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--text-3)' }}>Variables:</span>
                  {r.variables.map((v) => (
                    <Badge key={v.name} variant="neutral">
                      {v.name}
                    </Badge>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Run with variables Modal */}
      {runningRecipe && (
        <Modal
          isOpen={true}
          onClose={() => setRunningRecipe(null)}
          title={`Run: ${runningRecipe.name}`}
          description="Provide parameter values for this recipe before executing."
          footer={
            <>
              <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={() => setRunningRecipe(null)}>
                Cancel
              </Button>
              <Button
                variant="primary"
                size="sm"
                onClick={() => executeRun(runningRecipe, variableValues)}
                icon={<Play size={12} />}
              >
                Run recipe
              </Button>
            </>
          }
        >
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '12px' }}>
            {runningRecipe.variables.map((v) => (
              <div key={v.name} style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '4px' }}>
                <label style={{ fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: 500, color: 'var(--text)' }}>
                  {v.name}
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={variableValues[v.name] || ''}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setVariableValues({ ...variableValues, [v.name]: e.target.value })
                  }
                  placeholder={v.defaultValue || `Enter ${v.name}`}
                  style={{
                    background: 'var(--bg-2)',
                    border: '1px solid var(--border)',
                    borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)',
                    padding: '6px 10px',
                    color: 'var(--text)',
                    fontSize: '12px',
                    fontFamily: 'inherit',
                    outline: 'none',
                  }}
                />
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </Modal>
      )}

      {/* Delete Confirmation Modal */}
      {deletingRecipeId && (
        <Modal
          isOpen={true}
          onClose={() => setDeletingRecipeId(null)}
          title="Delete recipe"
          description="Are you sure you want to remove this saved recipe? This action cannot be undone."
          footer={
            <>
              <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={() => setDeletingRecipeId(null)}>
                Cancel
              </Button>
              <Button variant="danger" size="sm" onClick={confirmDelete}>
                Delete
              </Button>
            </>
          }
        >
          <p style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: 'var(--text-2)' }}>
            The recipe steps and associated parameters will be permanently erased from your local storage.
          </p>
        </Modal>
      )}
    </PageShell>
  );
};
```

### `src/main/cdp/page-observer.ts`

```typescript
import {
  PageSnapshot,
  SimplifiedDOM,
  A11yNode,
  InteractiveElement,
  FormInfo,
  LinkInfo,
  BoundingBox,
} from '../../shared/types';
import { CDPManager } from './cdp-manager';

interface CdpDomNode {
  nodeId: number;
  nodeType: number;
  nodeName: string;
  nodeValue?: string;
  attributes?: string[];
  children?: CdpDomNode[];
  shadowRoots?: CdpDomNode[];
}

interface CdpAXNode {
  role?: { value?: string };
  name?: { value?: string };
  value?: { value?: string };
  description?: { value?: string };
  properties?: Array<{ name: string; value?: { value?: unknown } }>;
  children?: CdpAXNode[];
}

interface CdpBoxModel {
  content?: number[];
}

const STRIP_TAGS = new Set([
  'script', 'style', 'noscript', 'link', 'meta', 'head',
  'template', 'svg', 'canvas', 'object', 'embed',
]);

const SEMANTIC_TAGS = new Set([
  'a', 'button', 'input', 'select', 'textarea', 'form',
  'nav', 'main', 'article', 'section', 'header', 'footer', 'aside',
  'h1', 'h2', 'h3', 'h4', 'h5', 'h6',
  'p', 'ul', 'ol', 'li', 'table', 'tr', 'td', 'th',
  'img', 'video', 'audio', 'label', 'fieldset', 'legend',
  'details', 'summary', 'dialog',
]);

const INTERACTIVE_TAGS = new Set([
  'a', 'button', 'input', 'select', 'textarea',
]);

export class PageObserver {
  private cdp: CDPManager;
  private interactiveIndex = 0;

  constructor(cdp: CDPManager) {
    this.cdp = cdp;
  }

  public async getPageSnapshot(tabId: string): Promise<PageSnapshot> {
    const start = Date.now();

    const [documentResult, axResult, metaResult] = await Promise.all([
      this.cdp.send<{ root: CdpDomNode }>(tabId, 'DOM.getDocument', { depth: -1, pierce: true }),
      this.cdp.send<{ nodes: CdpAXNode[] }>(tabId, 'Accessibility.getFullAXTree'),
      this.cdp.send<{ result: { value: string } }>(tabId, 'Runtime.evaluate', {
        expression: 'JSON.stringify({ url: location.href, title: document.title, innerText: (document.body ? document.body.innerText.slice(0, 30000) : "") })',
        returnByValue: true,
      }),
    ]);

    let url = '';
    let title = '';
    let bodyInnerText = '';
    try {
      const meta = JSON.parse(metaResult?.result?.value ?? '{}');
      url = meta.url ?? '';
      title = meta.title ?? '';
      bodyInnerText = (meta.innerText ?? '').trim();
    } catch {
      // ignore parse errors
    }

    const interactiveElements: InteractiveElement[] = [];
    this.interactiveIndex = 0;

    const simplifiedDom = documentResult?.root
      ? this.simplifyNode(documentResult.root, interactiveElements)
      : { tag: 'body', children: [] };

    const accessibilityTree = this.convertAXNodes(axResult?.nodes ?? []);
    const domText = this.extractVisibleText(simplifiedDom);
    const visibleText = bodyInnerText && bodyInnerText.length > domText.length ? bodyInnerText : (domText || bodyInnerText);
    const forms = this.extractForms(simplifiedDom);
    const links = this.extractLinks(simplifiedDom);

    const snapshot: PageSnapshot = {
      url,
      title,
      timestamp: Date.now(),
      dom: simplifiedDom,
      accessibilityTree,
      visibleText,
      forms,
      links,
      interactiveElements,
      estimatedTokenCount: Math.ceil(visibleText.length / 4),
    };

    console.debug(`[PageObserver] snapshot in ${Date.now() - start}ms`);

    return snapshot;
  }

  public async getAccessibilityTree(tabId: string): Promise<A11yNode[]> {
    const result = await this.cdp.send<{ nodes: CdpAXNode[] }>(
      tabId,
      'Accessibility.getFullAXTree',
    );
    return this.convertAXNodes(result?.nodes ?? []);
  }

  private simplifyNode(
    node: CdpDomNode,
    interactive: InteractiveElement[],
  ): SimplifiedDOM {
    const tag = node.nodeName.toLowerCase();

    if (node.nodeType === 3) {
      const text = (node.nodeValue ?? '').trim();
      return { tag: '#text', text, children: [] };
    }

    if (STRIP_TAGS.has(tag)) {
      return { tag, children: [] };
    }

    const attrs = this.parseAttributes(node.attributes ?? []);

    if (
      attrs['aria-hidden'] === 'true' ||
      attrs['hidden'] !== undefined ||
      attrs['type'] === 'hidden'
    ) {
      return { tag, children: [] };
    }

    const allChildren = [
      ...(node.children ?? []),
      ...(node.shadowRoots ?? []),
    ];

    const children = allChildren
      .map((c) => this.simplifyNode(c, interactive))
      .filter((c) => this.isNonEmpty(c));

    const simplified: SimplifiedDOM = { tag, children };

    if (attrs['id']) simplified.id = attrs['id'];
    if (attrs['class']) simplified.classes = attrs['class'].split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
    if (attrs['role']) simplified.role = attrs['role'];
    if (attrs['aria-label']) simplified.label = attrs['aria-label'];
    if (attrs['aria-labelledby']) simplified.label = simplified.label ?? attrs['aria-labelledby'];
    if (attrs['href']) simplified.href = attrs['href'];
    if (attrs['type']) simplified.type = attrs['type'];
    if (attrs['value']) simplified.value = attrs['value'];
    if (attrs['placeholder']) simplified.placeholder = attrs['placeholder'];

    const textNode = children.find((c) => c.tag === '#text' && c.text);
    if (textNode?.text && !simplified.text) {
      simplified.text = textNode.text;
    }

    if (INTERACTIVE_TAGS.has(tag) || attrs['role'] === 'button' || attrs['tabindex'] !== undefined) {
      const label = this.resolveLabel(simplified);
      const selector = this.buildSelector(attrs, tag);

      interactive.push({
        index: this.interactiveIndex++,
        tag,
        type: attrs['type'],
        role: attrs['role'] ?? this.inferRole(tag),
        label,
        selector,
        boundingBox: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 0, height: 0 },
        isVisible: true,
        isEnabled: attrs['disabled'] === undefined && attrs['aria-disabled'] !== 'true',
        value: attrs['value'],
      });
    }

    return simplified;
  }

  private isNonEmpty(node: SimplifiedDOM): boolean {
    if (node.tag === '#text') return !!(node.text?.trim());
    if (STRIP_TAGS.has(node.tag)) return false;
    if (node.text || node.href || node.value) return true;
    if (INTERACTIVE_TAGS.has(node.tag) || SEMANTIC_TAGS.has(node.tag)) return true;
    return node.children.some((c) => this.isNonEmpty(c));
  }

  private parseAttributes(raw: string[]): Record<string, string> {
    const result: Record<string, string> = {};
    for (let i = 0; i < raw.length - 1; i += 2) {
      result[raw[i]!] = raw[i + 1] ?? '';
    }
    return result;
  }

  private escapeCss(str: string): string {
    return str.replace(/([!"#$%&'()*+,.\/:;<=>?@[\\\]^`{|}~])/g, '\\$1');
  }

  private buildSelector(attrs: Record<string, string>, tag: string): string {
    if (attrs['id']) return `#${this.escapeCss(attrs['id'])}`;
    if (attrs['name']) return `${tag}[name="${attrs['name']}"]`;
    if (attrs['data-testid']) return `[data-testid="${attrs['data-testid']}"]`;
    if (attrs['class']) {
      const firstClass = attrs['class'].split(/\s+/)[0];
      if (firstClass) return `${tag}.${this.escapeCss(firstClass)}`;
    }
    return tag;
  }

  private resolveLabel(node: SimplifiedDOM): string {
    if (node.label) return node.label;
    if (node.text) return node.text.substring(0, 80);
    if (node.placeholder) return node.placeholder;
    if (node.value) return node.value.substring(0, 80);
    if (node.href) return node.href.substring(0, 80);
    return node.tag;
  }

  private inferRole(tag: string): string {
    const roleMap: Record<string, string> = {
      a: 'link', button: 'button', input: 'textbox', select: 'combobox', textarea: 'textbox',
      h1: 'heading', h2: 'heading', h3: 'heading', nav: 'navigation', main: 'main', form: 'form',
    };
    return roleMap[tag] ?? 'generic';
  }

  private convertAXNodes(nodes: CdpAXNode[]): A11yNode[] {
    return nodes
      .filter((n) => n.role?.value && n.role.value !== 'none' && n.role.value !== 'ignored')
      .map((n): A11yNode => {
        const isInteractive = [
          'button', 'link', 'textbox', 'combobox', 'checkbox',
          'radio', 'slider', 'spinbutton', 'switch', 'tab',
          'menuitem', 'option', 'searchbox',
        ].includes(n.role?.value ?? '');

        return {
          role: n.role?.value ?? 'generic',
          name: n.name?.value ?? '',
          value: n.value?.value ?? undefined,
          description: n.description?.value ?? undefined,
          isInteractive,
          boundingBox: undefined,
          children: this.convertAXNodes(n.children ?? []),
        };
      });
  }

  private extractVisibleText(node: SimplifiedDOM): string {
    if (node.tag === '#text') return node.text ?? '';
    if (node.children.length === 0) return node.text ?? '';
    const parts: string[] = [];
    for (const child of node.children) {
      const t = this.extractVisibleText(child);
      if (t) parts.push(t);
    }
    return parts.join(' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
  }

  private extractForms(root: SimplifiedDOM): FormInfo[] {
    const forms: FormInfo[] = [];
    this.walkNodes(root, (node) => {
      if (node.tag === 'form') {
        const fields: FormInfo['fields'] = [];
        this.walkNodes(node, (child) => {
          if (['input', 'select', 'textarea'].includes(child.tag)) {
            fields.push({
              selector: child.id ? `#${child.id}` : child.tag,
              label: child.label ?? child.placeholder ?? child.tag,
              type: child.type ?? child.tag,
              name: undefined,
              value: child.value,
              placeholder: child.placeholder,
              required: false,
            });
          }
        });
        forms.push({ formId: root.id, fields });
      }
    });
    return forms;
  }

  private extractLinks(root: SimplifiedDOM): LinkInfo[] {
    const links: LinkInfo[] = [];
    this.walkNodes(root, (node) => {
      if (node.tag === 'a' && node.href) {
        links.push({
          href: node.href,
          text: node.text ?? node.label ?? node.href,
          selector: node.id ? `#${node.id}` : 'a',
          isExternal: node.href.startsWith('http') && !node.href.startsWith(root.href ?? ''),
        });
      }
    });
    return links;
  }

  private walkNodes(node: SimplifiedDOM, visitor: (n: SimplifiedDOM) => void): void {
    visitor(node);
    for (const child of node.children) {
      this.walkNodes(child, visitor);
    }
  }

  public async getBoundingBox(tabId: string, selector: string): Promise<BoundingBox | undefined> {
    try {
      const nodeResult = await this.cdp.send<{ nodeId: number }>(
        tabId, 'DOM.querySelector',
        { nodeId: 1, selector },
      );
      if (!nodeResult?.nodeId) return undefined;

      const boxResult = await this.cdp.send<{ model: CdpBoxModel }>(
        tabId, 'DOM.getBoxModel',
        { nodeId: nodeResult.nodeId },
      );
      if (!boxResult?.model?.content) return undefined;

      const [x1, y1, x2, , , , x3, y3] = boxResult.model.content as number[];
      return {
        x: Math.min(x1 ?? 0, x3 ?? 0),
        y: Math.min(y1 ?? 0, y3 ?? 0),
        width: Math.abs((x2 ?? 0) - (x1 ?? 0)),
        height: Math.abs((y3 ?? 0) - (y1 ?? 0)),
      };
    } catch {
      return undefined;
    }
  }
}
```

### `src/renderer/pages/TrustDashboard.tsx`

```tsx
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Plus, Search, Trash2, Shield, AlertTriangle } from 'lucide-react';
import { SiteTrustEntry, SiteTrustLevel } from '../../shared/types';
import { PageShell } from '../components/ui/PageShell';
import { Card } from '../components/ui/Card';
import { Button } from '../components/ui/Button';
import { IconButton } from '../components/ui/IconButton';
import { Modal } from '../components/ui/Modal';
import { Badge } from '../components/ui/Badge';
import { ForgetControls } from '../components/ForgetControls';

export const TrustDashboard: React.FC = () => {
  const [entries, setEntries] = useState<SiteTrustEntry[]>([]);
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState('');
  const [newDomain, setNewDomain] = useState('');
  const [isAdding, setIsAdding] = useState(false);
  const [pendingWarning, setPendingWarning] = useState<{
    domain: string;
    targetLevel: SiteTrustLevel;
  } | null>(null);

  const loadEntries = async () => {
    const sentry = (window as any).sentry;
    if (sentry?.trust?.getAll) {
      const liveEntries = await sentry.trust.getAll();
      setEntries(liveEntries || []);
    } else {
      setEntries([
        { domain: 'chase.com', trustLevel: SiteTrustLevel.HIGHLY_RESTRICTED, setBy: 'default', lastModified: Date.now() },
        { domain: 'gmail.com', trustLevel: SiteTrustLevel.HIGHLY_RESTRICTED, setBy: 'default', lastModified: Date.now() },
        { domain: 'example.com', trustLevel: SiteTrustLevel.READ_ACT, setBy: 'user', lastModified: Date.now() },
        { domain: 'malicious.com', trustLevel: SiteTrustLevel.BLOCKED, setBy: 'user', lastModified: Date.now() },
      ]);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    loadEntries();
  }, []);

  const handleTrustChange = async (domain: string, newLevel: SiteTrustLevel) => {
    const entry = entries.find((e) => e.domain === domain);
    if (!entry) return;

    if (entry.trustLevel === SiteTrustLevel.HIGHLY_RESTRICTED && newLevel !== SiteTrustLevel.HIGHLY_RESTRICTED) {
      setPendingWarning({ domain, targetLevel: newLevel });
      return;
    }

    applyTrustChange(domain, newLevel);
  };

  const applyTrustChange = async (domain: string, newLevel: SiteTrustLevel) => {
    const sentry = (window as any).sentry;
    if (sentry?.trust?.setLevel) {
      await sentry.trust.setLevel(domain, newLevel);
    }
    setEntries((prev) =>
      prev.map((e) => (e.domain === domain ? { ...e, trustLevel: newLevel, setBy: 'user', lastModified: Date.now() } : e))
    );
  };

  const handleAddDomain = async () => {
    const domain = newDomain.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/^https?:\/\//, '');
    if (!domain) return;
    const sentry = (window as any).sentry;
    if (sentry?.trust?.setLevel) {
      await sentry.trust.setLevel(domain, SiteTrustLevel.READ_ACT);
    }
    setEntries((prev) => [...prev, { domain, trustLevel: SiteTrustLevel.READ_ACT, setBy: 'user', lastModified: Date.now() }]);
    setNewDomain('');
    setIsAdding(false);
  };

  const handleRemoveDomain = async (domain: string) => {
    setEntries((prev) => prev.filter((e) => e.domain !== domain));
  };

  const getLevelBadge = (level: SiteTrustLevel) => {
    switch (level) {
      case SiteTrustLevel.READ_ACT:
        return <Badge variant="ok" dot>Read & Act</Badge>;
      case SiteTrustLevel.READ_ONLY:
        return <Badge variant="warn" dot>Read only</Badge>;
      case SiteTrustLevel.HIGHLY_RESTRICTED:
        return <Badge variant="danger" dot>Restricted</Badge>;
      case SiteTrustLevel.BLOCKED:
        return <Badge variant="neutral" dot>Blocked</Badge>;
    }
  };

  const filteredEntries = entries.filter((e) => e.domain.toLowerCase().includes(filter.toLowerCase()));

  return (
    <PageShell
      title="Security & Trust"
      description="Define permissions for how the AI agent interacts with specific web domains."
    >
      {/* Controls Bar */}
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px', alignItems: 'center' }}>
        <div
          style={{
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            gap: '8px',
            background: 'var(--bg-1)',
            border: '1px solid var(--border)',
            borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)',
            padding: '4px 10px',
            flex: 1,
          }}
        >
          <Search size={14} style={{ color: 'var(--text-3)' }} />
          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder="Search domains…"
            value={filter}
            onChange={(e) => setFilter(e.target.value)}
            style={{
              flex: 1,
              background: 'transparent',
              border: 'none',
              outline: 'none',
              color: 'var(--text)',
              fontSize: '12px',
              fontFamily: 'inherit',
            }}
          />
        </div>

        {isAdding ? (
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '6px' }}>
            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder="domain.com"
              value={newDomain}
              onChange={(e) => setNewDomain(e.target.value)}
              onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === 'Enter' && handleAddDomain()}
              style={{
                background: 'var(--bg-1)',
                border: '1px solid var(--border)',
                borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)',
                padding: '4px 8px',
                fontSize: '12px',
                color: 'var(--text)',
                fontFamily: 'inherit',
                outline: 'none',
                width: '140px',
              }}
              autoFocus
            />
            <Button variant="primary" size="sm" onClick={handleAddDomain}>
              Add
            </Button>
            <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={() => { setIsAdding(false); setNewDomain(''); }}>
              Cancel
            </Button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={() => setIsAdding(true)} icon={<Plus size={13} />}>
            Add domain
          </Button>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Trust Table */}
      <Card style={{ padding: 0, overflow: 'hidden' }}>
        <div style={{ overflowX: 'auto' }}>
          <table style={{ width: '100%', borderCollapse: 'collapse', fontSize: '12px', textAlign: 'left' }}>
            <thead>
              <tr style={{ background: 'var(--bg-2)', borderBottom: '1px solid var(--border)', color: 'var(--text-2)' }}>
                <th style={{ padding: '8px 12px', fontWeight: 500 }}>Domain</th>
                <th style={{ padding: '8px 12px', fontWeight: 500 }}>Trust status</th>
                <th style={{ padding: '8px 12px', fontWeight: 500 }}>Policy selector</th>
                <th style={{ padding: '8px 12px', fontWeight: 500 }}>Source</th>
                <th style={{ padding: '8px 12px', textAlign: 'right', fontWeight: 500 }}>Actions</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {filteredEntries.map((entry) => (
                <tr
                  key={entry.domain}
                  style={{ borderBottom: '1px solid var(--border)' }}
                >
                  <td style={{ padding: '8px 12px', fontWeight: 500, color: 'var(--text)' }}>
                    {entry.domain}
                  </td>
                  <td style={{ padding: '8px 12px' }}>
                    {getLevelBadge(entry.trustLevel)}
                  </td>
                  <td style={{ padding: '8px 12px' }}>
                    <select
                      value={entry.trustLevel}
                      onChange={(e) => handleTrustChange(entry.domain, e.target.value as SiteTrustLevel)}
                      style={{
                        background: 'var(--bg-1)',
                        color: 'var(--text)',
                        border: '1px solid var(--border)',
                        padding: '3px 8px',
                        borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)',
                        fontSize: '11px',
                        fontFamily: 'inherit',
                        outline: 'none',
                      }}
                    >
                      <option value={SiteTrustLevel.READ_ACT}>Read & Act</option>
                      <option value={SiteTrustLevel.READ_ONLY}>Read Only</option>
                      <option value={SiteTrustLevel.HIGHLY_RESTRICTED}>Highly Restricted</option>
                      <option value={SiteTrustLevel.BLOCKED}>Blocked</option>
                    </select>
                  </td>
                  <td style={{ padding: '8px 12px', color: 'var(--text-3)', fontSize: '11px' }}>
                    {entry.setBy === 'default' ? 'System default' : 'Custom'}
                  </td>
                  <td style={{ padding: '8px 12px', textAlign: 'right' }}>
                    <IconButton
                      label="Remove rule"
                      onClick={() => handleRemoveDomain(entry.domain)}
                      style={{ color: 'var(--text-3)', marginLeft: 'auto' }}
                    >
                      <Trash2 size={13} />
                    </IconButton>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
              {filteredEntries.length === 0 && (
                <tr>
                  <td colSpan={5} style={{ textAlign: 'center', color: 'var(--text-3)', padding: '24px' }}>
                    No matching domains found.
                  </td>
                </tr>
              )}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </Card>

      {/* Memory Controls */}
      <ForgetControls />

      {/* Warning Modal when downgrading restricted domains */}
      {pendingWarning && (
        <Modal
          isOpen={true}
          onClose={() => setPendingWarning(null)}
          title="Lower security restriction"
          description={`You are changing the security level of ${pendingWarning.domain} from Highly Restricted to ${pendingWarning.targetLevel}.`}
          footer={
            <>
              <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={() => setPendingWarning(null)}>
                Cancel
              </Button>
              <Button
                variant="danger"
                size="sm"
                onClick={() => {
                  applyTrustChange(pendingWarning.domain, pendingWarning.targetLevel);
                  setPendingWarning(null);
                }}
              >
                Apply change
              </Button>
            </>
          }
        >
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '10px', alignItems: 'flex-start' }}>
            <AlertTriangle size={18} style={{ color: 'var(--warn)', flexShrink: 0 }} />
            <p style={{ fontSize: '12px', color: 'var(--text-2)', lineHeight: 1.4 }}>
              This domain is marked sensitive by default (e.g. banking or email). Lowering its security tier allows the AI agent to interact with form inputs and take actions without requiring manual confirmation.
            </p>
          </div>
        </Modal>
      )}
    </PageShell>
  );
};
```

### `src/renderer/components/AddressBar.tsx`

```tsx
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
  ArrowLeft,
  ArrowRight,
  RotateCw,
  X,
  Lock,
  Globe,
  Star,
  Bot,
  MoreHorizontal,
  History as HistoryIcon,
  Bookmark as BookmarkIcon,
  Download,
  Puzzle,
  FileText,
  ShieldCheck,
  Cpu,
  Terminal,
  Settings as SettingsIcon,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { TabItem, BookmarkItem, HistoryItem, AgentNotification } from '../../shared/types';
import { IconButton } from './ui/IconButton';
import { Button } from './ui/Button';
import { Menu, MenuItem, MenuDivider } from './ui/Menu';
import { NotificationCenter } from './NotificationCenter';
import { useHideNativeView } from '../hooks/useHideNativeView';

interface AddressBarProps {
  activeTab: TabItem | null;
  bookmarks: BookmarkItem[];
  history: HistoryItem[];
  routingDecision?: any;
  isSidebarOpen: boolean;
  onToggleSidebar: () => void;
  notifications: AgentNotification[];
  onDismissNotification: (id: string) => void;
  onClearNotifications: () => void;
  onNavigate: (url: string) => void;
  onGoBack: () => void;
  onGoForward: () => void;
  onReload: () => void;
  onToggleBookmark: (url: string, title: string) => void;
  onOpenPage: (page: string) => void;
}

export const AddressBar: React.FC<AddressBarProps> = ({
  activeTab,
  bookmarks,
  history,
  isSidebarOpen,
  onToggleSidebar,
  notifications,
  onDismissNotification,
  onClearNotifications,
  onNavigate,
  onGoBack,
  onGoForward,
  onReload,
  onToggleBookmark,
  onOpenPage,
}) => {
  const [inputUrl, setInputUrl] = useState('');
  const [isFocused, setIsFocused] = useState(false);
  const [suggestions, setSuggestions] = useState<{ title: string; url: string; type: string }[]>([]);
  const [isMenuOpen, setIsMenuOpen] = useState(false);

  const activeUrl = activeTab?.url;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeUrl) {
      setInputUrl(activeUrl === 'sentry://newtab' ? '' : activeUrl);
    }
  }, [activeUrl]);

  // Hide native WebContentsView when suggestions are showing so they aren't cut in half
  const showSuggestions = isFocused && suggestions.length > 0;
  useHideNativeView(showSuggestions);

  const handleInputChange = (val: string) => {
    setInputUrl(val);
    if (!val.trim()) {
      setSuggestions([]);
      return;
    }

    const query = val.toLowerCase();
    const matches: { title: string; url: string; type: string }[] = [];

    // History matches
    history.forEach((h) => {
      if (h.url.toLowerCase().includes(query) || h.title.toLowerCase().includes(query)) {
        if (matches.length < 5) matches.push({ title: h.title, url: h.url, type: 'history' });
      }
    });

    // Bookmark matches
    bookmarks.forEach((b) => {
      if (b.url.toLowerCase().includes(query) || b.title.toLowerCase().includes(query)) {
        if (matches.length < 8) matches.push({ title: b.title, url: b.url, type: 'bookmark' });
      }
    });

    setSuggestions(matches);
  };

  const handleSubmit = (e?: React.FormEvent) => {
    if (e) e.preventDefault();
    if (inputUrl.trim()) {
      onNavigate(inputUrl.trim());
      setIsFocused(false);
    }
  };

  const isBookmarked = activeTab ? bookmarks.some((b) => b.url === activeTab.url) : false;
  const isHttps = activeTab?.url.startsWith('https://');
  const isInternal = activeTab?.url.startsWith('sentry://');

  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display: 'flex',
        alignItems: 'center',
        background: 'var(--bg-1)',
        padding: '6px 12px',
        gap: '6px',
        borderBottom: '1px solid var(--border)',
        position: 'relative',
        height: '42px',
        flexShrink: 0,
        zIndex: 50,
      }}
    >
      {/* Navigation Buttons */}
      <IconButton
        id="nav-btn-back"
        label="Go Back (Alt+Left)"
        disabled={!activeTab?.canGoBack}
        onClick={onGoBack}
      >
        <ArrowLeft size={15} />
      </IconButton>

      <IconButton
        id="nav-btn-forward"
        label="Go Forward (Alt+Right)"
        disabled={!activeTab?.canGoForward}
        onClick={onGoForward}
      >
        <ArrowRight size={15} />
      </IconButton>

      <IconButton
        id="nav-btn-reload"
        label={activeTab?.isLoading ? 'Stop loading (Esc)' : 'Reload page (Ctrl+R)'}
        onClick={onReload}
      >
        {activeTab?.isLoading ? <X size={15} /> : <RotateCw size={14} />}
      </IconButton>

      {/* Address Bar Form */}
      <form
        onSubmit={handleSubmit}
        style={{
          flexGrow: 1,
          minWidth: 0,
          position: 'relative',
          display: 'flex',
        }}
      >
        <div
          style={{
            display: 'flex',
            alignItems: 'center',
            width: '100%',
            height: 'var(--control-h)',
            background: 'var(--bg-2)',
            borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)',
            padding: '0 8px',
            border: isFocused ? '1px solid var(--accent)' : '1px solid var(--border)',
            transition: 'border-color 0.15s ease',
          }}
        >
          {/* Security / Scheme Icon */}
          <span
            style={{
              display: 'inline-flex',
              alignItems: 'center',
              color: isInternal ? 'var(--accent)' : isHttps ? 'var(--ok)' : 'var(--text-3)',
              marginRight: '6px',
              flexShrink: 0,
            }}
          >
            {isInternal ? (
              <ShieldCheck size={14} />
            ) : isHttps ? (
              <Lock size={13} />
            ) : (
              <Globe size={13} />
            )}
          </span>

          <input
            type="text"
            value={inputUrl}
            onFocus={() => setIsFocused(true)}
            onBlur={() => setTimeout(() => setIsFocused(false), 200)}
            onChange={(e) => handleInputChange(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Search with Google or enter address"
            style={{
              flexGrow: 1,
              minWidth: 0,
              background: 'transparent',
              border: 'none',
              outline: 'none',
              color: 'var(--text)',
              fontSize: '12px',
              fontFamily: 'inherit',
            }}
          />

          {/* Bookmark Star Button */}
          {activeTab && !isInternal && (
            <IconButton
              type="button"
              label={isBookmarked ? 'Remove bookmark' : 'Bookmark this tab (Ctrl+D)'}
              onClick={() => onToggleBookmark(activeTab.url, activeTab.title)}
              style={{
                width: '20px',
                height: '20px',
                color: isBookmarked ? 'var(--warn)' : 'var(--text-3)',
              }}
            >
              <Star size={13} fill={isBookmarked ? 'var(--warn)' : 'none'} />
            </IconButton>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Suggestions Dropdown */}
        {showSuggestions && (
          <div
            style={{
              position: 'absolute',
              top: '34px',
              left: 0,
              right: 0,
              background: 'var(--bg-1)',
              border: '1px solid var(--border)',
              borderRadius: 'var(--radius-md)',
              boxShadow: '0 12px 28px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.55)',
              zIndex: 9999,
              overflow: 'hidden',
            }}
          >
            {suggestions.map((s, idx) => (
              <div
                key={idx}
                onClick={() => {
                  onNavigate(s.url);
                  setIsFocused(false);
                }}
                style={{
                  padding: '7px 12px',
                  display: 'flex',
                  alignItems: 'center',
                  gap: '8px',
                  cursor: 'pointer',
                  fontSize: '12px',
                  color: 'var(--text)',
                  borderBottom: idx === suggestions.length - 1 ? 'none' : '1px solid var(--border)',
                }}
                onMouseEnter={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.background = 'var(--bg-3)')}
                onMouseLeave={(e) => (e.currentTarget.style.background = 'transparent')}
              >
                <span style={{ color: 'var(--text-3)', display: 'inline-flex' }}>
                  {s.type === 'bookmark' ? <Star size={12} fill="var(--warn)" color="var(--warn)" /> : <HistoryIcon size={12} />}
                </span>
                <span style={{ fontWeight: 500, overflow: 'hidden', textOverflow: 'ellipsis', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
                  {s.title}
                </span>
                <span style={{ color: 'var(--text-3)', fontSize: '11px', marginLeft: 'auto', flexShrink: 0 }}>
                  {s.url}
                </span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </form>

      {/* Agent Toggle Button */}
      <Button
        variant={isSidebarOpen ? 'primary' : 'secondary'}
        onClick={onToggleSidebar}
        title="Toggle AI Agent Sidebar (Ctrl+Shift+Space)"
        style={{
          height: 'var(--control-h)',
          padding: '0 10px',
          fontWeight: 500,
        }}
        icon={<Bot size={14} />}
      >
        Agent
      </Button>

      {/* Notifications Drawer */}
      <NotificationCenter
        notifications={notifications}
        onDismiss={onDismissNotification}
        onClearAll={onClearNotifications}
      />

      {/* More ("⋯") Menu Button */}
      <div style={{ position: 'relative' }}>
        <IconButton
          label="More options"
          active={isMenuOpen}
          onClick={() => setIsMenuOpen(!isMenuOpen)}
        >
          <MoreHorizontal size={16} />
        </IconButton>

        <Menu
          isOpen={isMenuOpen}
          onClose={() => setIsMenuOpen(false)}
          top="34px"
          right={0}
          width={220}
        >
          <MenuItem
            icon={<HistoryIcon size={14} />}
            label="History"
            shortcut="Ctrl+H"
            onClick={() => {
              onOpenPage('history');
              setIsMenuOpen(false);
            }}
          />
          <MenuItem
            icon={<BookmarkIcon size={14} />}
            label="Bookmarks"
            shortcut="Ctrl+D"
            onClick={() => {
              onOpenPage('bookmarks');
              setIsMenuOpen(false);
            }}
          />
          <MenuItem
            icon={<Download size={14} />}
            label="Downloads"
            shortcut="Ctrl+J"
            onClick={() => {
              onOpenPage('downloads');
              setIsMenuOpen(false);
            }}
          />

          <MenuDivider />

          <MenuItem
            icon={<Puzzle size={14} />}
            label="Extensions"
            onClick={() => {
              onOpenPage('extensions');
              setIsMenuOpen(false);
            }}
          />
          <MenuItem
            icon={<FileText size={14} />}
            label="Recipes"
            onClick={() => {
              onOpenPage('recipes');
              setIsMenuOpen(false);
            }}
          />
          <MenuItem
            icon={<ShieldCheck size={14} />}
            label="Trust & Security"
            onClick={() => {
              onOpenPage('trust');
              setIsMenuOpen(false);
            }}
          />
          <MenuItem
            icon={<Cpu size={14} />}
            label="AI Models"
            onClick={() => {
              onOpenPage('models');
              setIsMenuOpen(false);
            }}
          />
          <MenuItem
            icon={<Terminal size={14} />}
            label="Developer Mode"
            onClick={() => {
              onOpenPage('developer');
              setIsMenuOpen(false);
            }}
          />

          <MenuDivider />

          <MenuItem
            icon={<SettingsIcon size={14} />}
            label="Settings"
            shortcut="Ctrl+,"
            onClick={() => {
              onOpenPage('settings');
              setIsMenuOpen(false);
            }}
          />
        </Menu>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
```

### `src/renderer/components/AgentSidebar.tsx`

```tsx
import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
  Bot,
  X,
  Play,
  Pause,
  Square,
  ChevronDown,
  ChevronRight,
  Send,
  Sparkles,
  BookmarkPlus,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { TaskState, ActionLogEntry, TaskPlan } from '../../shared/types';
import { AgentResultCard } from './AgentResultCard';
import { ActionLog } from '../overlays/ActionLog';
import { VoiceControls } from './VoiceControls';
import { IconButton } from './ui/IconButton';
import { Button } from './ui/Button';
import { Badge } from './ui/Badge';
import './AgentSidebar.css';

export interface AgentResultData {
  instruction: string;
  summary: string;
  timestamp: number;
}

interface AgentSidebarProps {
  taskState: TaskState;
  actionLog: ActionLogEntry[];
  plan?: TaskPlan;
  currentStep?: number;
  totalSteps?: number;
  lastMessage?: string;
  lastResult?: AgentResultData | null;
  routingDecision?: any;
  lastInstruction?: string;
  onClearResult?: () => void;
  onSendTask: (instruction: string) => void;
  onPause: () => void;
  onStop: () => void;
  onResume: () => void;
  onUndoLast: (entry: ActionLogEntry) => Promise<boolean>;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSaveRecipe?: (instruction: string) => void;
  onToggleVisionMode?: () => void;
  isVisionModeActive?: boolean;
  focusTrigger?: number;
}

const STATE_NAMES: Record<TaskState, { label: string; variant: 'ok' | 'warn' | 'danger' | 'accent' | 'neutral' }> = {
  [TaskState.IDLE]: { label: 'Ready', variant: 'neutral' },
  [TaskState.PLANNING]: { label: 'Planning…', variant: 'accent' },
  [TaskState.AWAITING_CONFIRMATION]: { label: 'Awaiting approval', variant: 'warn' },
  [TaskState.EXECUTING]: { label: 'Executing', variant: 'accent' },
  [TaskState.PAUSED]: { label: 'Paused', variant: 'warn' },
  [TaskState.COMPLETED]: { label: 'Completed', variant: 'ok' },
  [TaskState.FAILED]: { label: 'Failed', variant: 'danger' },
};

export const AgentSidebar: React.FC<AgentSidebarProps> = ({
  taskState,
  actionLog,
  plan,
  currentStep,
  totalSteps,
  lastMessage,
  lastResult,
  routingDecision,
  lastInstruction,
  onClearResult,
  onSendTask,
  onPause,
  onStop,
  onResume,
  onClose,
  onSaveRecipe,
  focusTrigger,
}) => {
  const [input, setInput] = useState('');
  const [showLog, setShowLog] = useState(false);
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement>(null);

  // Focus textarea when focusTrigger changes
  useEffect(() => {
    inputRef.current?.focus();
  }, [focusTrigger]);

  const isActive = [TaskState.PLANNING, TaskState.EXECUTING, TaskState.AWAITING_CONFIRMATION].includes(taskState);
  const isPaused = taskState === TaskState.PAUSED;
  const isIdle = !isActive && !isPaused;

  const handleSend = () => {
    const text = input.trim();
    if (!text || !isIdle) return;
    onSendTask(text);
    setInput('');
    if (inputRef.current) {
      inputRef.current.style.height = '36px';
    }
  };

  const handleKeyDown = (e: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLTextAreaElement>) => {
    if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.shiftKey) {
      e.preventDefault();
      handleSend();
    }
  };

  const handleTextareaInput = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLTextAreaElement>) => {
    setInput(e.target.value);
    e.target.style.height = 'auto';
    const nextHeight = Math.min(120, Math.max(36, e.target.scrollHeight));
    e.target.style.height = `${nextHeight}px`;
  };

  const isCloud = routingDecision?.isCloud ?? false;
  const provider = routingDecision?.adapter?.providerId || (isCloud ? 'Cloud' : 'Local');
  const stateInfo = STATE_NAMES[taskState] || { label: 'Ready', variant: 'neutral' };

  return (
    <aside className="agent-sidebar" aria-label="Agent Sidebar">
      {/* ── Header ────────────────────────────────────────────── */}
      <div className="agent-sidebar-header">
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '8px' }}>
          <Bot size={16} style={{ color: 'var(--accent)' }} />
          <span style={{ fontWeight: 600, fontSize: '13px', color: 'var(--text)' }}>
            Agent
          </span>
          <Badge variant={isCloud ? 'accent' : 'neutral'} dot>
            {isCloud ? `Cloud: ${provider}` : 'Local'}
          </Badge>
        </div>

        <IconButton label="Close Agent (Ctrl+Shift+Space)" onClick={onClose}>
          <X size={15} />
        </IconButton>
      </div>

      {/* ── Scrollable Body ───────────────────────────────────── */}
      <div className="agent-sidebar-body">
        {/* Status bar if active / paused */}
        {(isActive || isPaused) && (
          <div
            style={{
              display: 'flex',
              alignItems: 'center',
              justifyContent: 'space-between',
              padding: '8px 10px',
              background: 'var(--bg-2)',
              borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)',
              border: '1px solid var(--border)',
            }}
          >
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: '6px' }}>
              <span className={`status-dot ${stateInfo.variant}`} />
              <span style={{ fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: 500, color: 'var(--text)' }}>
                {stateInfo.label}
              </span>
              {currentStep !== undefined && (
                <span style={{ fontSize: '11px', color: 'var(--text-3)' }}>
                  ({currentStep}{totalSteps ? `/${totalSteps}` : ''})
                </span>
              )}
            </div>

            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '4px' }}>
              {isActive && (
                <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onClick={onPause} icon={<Pause size={12} />}>
                  Pause
                </Button>
              )}
              {isPaused && (
                <Button variant="primary" size="sm" onClick={onResume} icon={<Play size={12} />}>
                  Resume
                </Button>
              )}
              <Button variant="danger" size="sm" onClick={onStop} icon={<Square size={11} />}>
                Stop
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Live message if running */}
        {lastMessage && (
          <div
            style={{
              fontSize: '11px',
              color: 'var(--text-2)',
              padding: '6px 10px',
              background: 'var(--bg-2)',
              borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)',
              border: '1px solid var(--border)',
              lineHeight: 1.4,
            }}
          >
            {lastMessage}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* AI Result Card */}
        {lastResult && (
          <AgentResultCard
            instruction={lastResult.instruction}
            summary={lastResult.summary}
            timestamp={lastResult.timestamp}
            onClear={onClearResult}
          />
        )}

        {/* Save Recipe Action */}
        {taskState === TaskState.COMPLETED && lastInstruction && onSaveRecipe && (
          <div style={{ display: 'flex' }}>
            <Button
              variant="secondary"
              size="sm"
              onClick={() => onSaveRecipe(lastInstruction)}
              icon={<BookmarkPlus size={13} style={{ color: 'var(--ok)' }} />}
              style={{ width: '100%', justifyContent: 'center' }}
            >
              Save as recipe
            </Button>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Plan card if available */}
        {plan && plan.steps.length > 0 && (
          <div
            style={{
              padding: '10px',
              background: 'var(--bg-2)',
              borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)',
              border: '1px solid var(--border)',
              display: 'flex',
              flexDirection: 'column',
              gap: '6px',
            }}
          >
            <div style={{ fontSize: '11px', fontWeight: 600, color: 'var(--text-2)' }}>
              Plan ({plan.steps.length} steps)
            </div>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '4px' }}>
              {plan.steps.map((step) => {
                const isDone = currentStep !== undefined && step.stepNumber < currentStep;
                const isCurrent = currentStep === step.stepNumber;
                return (
                  <div
                    key={step.stepNumber}
                    style={{
                      display: 'flex',
                      alignItems: 'center',
                      gap: '6px',
                      fontSize: '11px',
                      color: isDone ? 'var(--text-3)' : isCurrent ? 'var(--text)' : 'var(--text-2)',
                    }}
                  >
                    <span
                      className={`status-dot ${isDone ? 'ok' : isCurrent ? 'accent' : 'neutral'}`}
                      style={{ width: '6px', height: '6px' }}
                    />
                    <span style={{ textDecoration: isDone ? 'line-through' : 'none' }}>
                      {step.description}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Action Log Accordion */}
        {actionLog.length > 0 && (
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '4px' }}>
            <button
              onClick={() => setShowLog(!showLog)}
              style={{
                display: 'flex',
                alignItems: 'center',
                justifyContent: 'space-between',
                padding: '6px 8px',
                background: 'transparent',
                border: '1px solid var(--border)',
                borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)',
                color: 'var(--text-2)',
                fontSize: '11px',
                cursor: 'pointer',
              }}
            >
              <span>Activity log ({actionLog.length})</span>
              {showLog ? <ChevronDown size={14} /> : <ChevronRight size={14} />}
            </button>

            {showLog && (
              <div
                style={{
                  maxHeight: '280px',
                  overflowY: 'auto',
                  border: '1px solid var(--border)',
                  borderRadius: 'var(--radius-sm)',
                  padding: '4px',
                  background: 'var(--bg-0)',
                }}
              >
                <ActionLog entries={actionLog} />
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Empty state hint */}
        {!lastResult && actionLog.length === 0 && !isActive && (
          <div className="agent-empty-state">
            <Sparkles size={24} style={{ color: 'var(--accent)', opacity: 0.8 }} />
            <p className="empty-title">Ask Sentry to do something…</p>
            <p className="empty-desc">
              Browse, summarize, compare tabs, or interact with this page.
            </p>
            <span className="empty-shortcut">Ctrl+Shift+Space to focus</span>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* ── Pinned Footer: Prompt Input Box ───────────────────── */}
      <div className="agent-sidebar-footer">
        <div className="agent-input-container">
          <textarea
            ref={inputRef}
            className="agent-textarea"
            placeholder="Ask Sentry to do something…"
            value={input}
            onChange={handleTextareaInput}
            onKeyDown={handleKeyDown}
            rows={1}
            disabled={!isIdle}
          />

          <div className="agent-input-actions">
            <VoiceControls
              onTranscriptReceived={(transcript) => {
                setInput((prev) => (prev ? `${prev} ${transcript}` : transcript));
              }}
            />

            <button
              className="agent-send-button"
              onClick={handleSend}
              disabled={!input.trim() || !isIdle}
              title="Run instruction (Enter)"
            >
              <Send size={13} />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
};
```

### `src/renderer/components/CloudIndicator.css`

```css
.cloud-indicator {
  position: relative;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 6px;
  padding: 3px 8px;
  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 500;
  cursor: default;
  transition: all 0.15s ease;
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
}

.cloud-indicator.cloud {
  background: var(--accent-bg);
  color: var(--accent);
  border-color: rgba(76, 141, 255, 0.25);
}

.cloud-indicator.local {
  background: var(--ok-bg);
  color: var(--ok);
  border-color: rgba(63, 178, 127, 0.25);
}

.cloud-indicator-tooltip {
  position: absolute;
  top: calc(100% + 6px);
  right: 0;
  width: 260px;
  background: var(--bg-1);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  box-shadow: 0 8px 24px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.45);
  z-index: 1000;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  overflow: hidden;
  color: var(--text);
  text-align: left;
}

.tooltip-header {
  background: var(--bg-2);
  padding: 8px 12px;
  font-weight: 600;
  font-size: 11px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border);
}

.cloud .tooltip-header {
  color: var(--accent);
}

.local .tooltip-header {
  color: var(--ok);
}

.tooltip-body {
  padding: 10px 12px;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 6px;
}

.detail-row {
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  font-size: 11px;
  color: var(--text-2);
}

.detail-row strong {
  color: var(--text);
  font-weight: 500;
}
```

```css
.voice-controls {

}
```
