### `src/main/action-layer/risk-classifier.ts`

```typescript
import {
  ActionContext,
  RiskClassification,
  RiskTier,
  SiteTrustLevel,
  ProposedAction,
  PageSnapshot,
  InteractiveElement
} from '../../shared/types';
import { ElementResolver } from '../cdp/element-resolver';

interface PolicyRule {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  priority: number;        // Higher priority rules override lower
  condition: (context: ActionContext) => boolean;
  riskTier: RiskTier;
}

const resolver = new ElementResolver();

// Pre-defined rules
const rules: PolicyRule[] = [
  // CRITICAL Rules (Priority 100+)
  {
    id: 'password-manager-extension',
    name: 'Password Manager Interaction',
    description: 'Interaction with a password manager extension is ALWAYS CRITICAL',
    priority: 120, // Highest priority
    condition: (ctx) => {
      // If the target URL or any metadata indicates it's a password manager extension
      // E.g. chrome-extension://... or specific IDs for bitwarden/1password
      const targetStr = (ctx.action.target || '').toLowerCase();
      const urlStr = (ctx.action.url || '').toLowerCase();
      return urlStr.includes('chrome-extension://') && 
             (urlStr.includes('password') || urlStr.includes('bitwarden') || urlStr.includes('lastpass') || 
              targetStr.includes('bitwarden') || targetStr.includes('1password'));
    },
    riskTier: RiskTier.CRITICAL,
  },
  {
    id: 'password-field',
    name: 'Password Field Interaction',
    description: 'Any interaction with a password field is CRITICAL',
    priority: 100,
    condition: (ctx) => {
      const type = (ctx.targetElement as InteractiveElement)?.type;
      return type === 'password';
    },
    riskTier: RiskTier.CRITICAL,
  },
  {
    id: 'delete-action',
    name: 'Delete Action',
    description: 'Deleting content or accounts is CRITICAL',
    priority: 110,
    condition: (ctx) => {
      const label = (ctx.targetElement as InteractiveElement)?.label?.toLowerCase() || '';
      return ['delete', 'remove', 'destroy', 'terminate'].some(word => label.includes(word));
    },
    riskTier: RiskTier.CRITICAL,
  },
  {
    id: 'payment-action',
    name: 'Payment Action',
    description: 'Making payments is CRITICAL',
    priority: 110,
    condition: (ctx) => {
      const label = (ctx.targetElement as InteractiveElement)?.label?.toLowerCase() || '';
      return ['pay now', 'checkout', 'submit payment', 'buy now'].some(word => label.includes(word));
    },
    riskTier: RiskTier.CRITICAL,
  },
  // HIGH Rules (Priority 80+)
  {
    id: 'form-submit',
    name: 'Form Submission',
    description: 'Submitting a form is HIGH',
    priority: 80,
    condition: (ctx) => ctx.action.type === 'click' && (ctx.targetElement as InteractiveElement)?.type === 'submit',
    riskTier: RiskTier.HIGH,
  },
  {
    id: 'sensitive-form-fill',
    name: 'Sensitive Form Fill',
    description: 'Filling out sensitive information is HIGH',
    priority: 85,
    condition: (ctx) => {
      if (ctx.action.type !== 'type' && ctx.action.type !== 'fill') return false;
      const label = (ctx.targetElement as InteractiveElement)?.label?.toLowerCase() || '';
      return ['ssn', 'credit card', 'card number', 'phone', 'address'].some(word => label.includes(word));
    },
    riskTier: RiskTier.HIGH,
  },
  // MEDIUM Rules (Priority 50+)
  {
    id: 'download-file',
    name: 'Download File',
    description: 'Downloading a file is MEDIUM',
    priority: 50,
    condition: (ctx) => {
      const href = (ctx.targetElement as any)?.href || '';
      return href.match(/\.(pdf|zip|exe|dmg|pkg)$/i) !== null;
    },
    riskTier: RiskTier.MEDIUM,
  },
  {
    id: 'fill-search',
    name: 'Fill Search Box',
    description: 'Filling a search box is MEDIUM',
    priority: 60,
    condition: (ctx) => {
      if (ctx.action.type !== 'type' && ctx.action.type !== 'fill') return false;
      const type = (ctx.targetElement as InteractiveElement)?.type;
      const label = (ctx.targetElement as InteractiveElement)?.label?.toLowerCase() || '';
      return type === 'search' || label.includes('search');
    },
    riskTier: RiskTier.MEDIUM,
  },
  // LOW Rules (Priority 10+)
  {
    id: 'navigation-click',
    name: 'Navigation Link Click',
    description: 'Clicking a navigation link is LOW',
    priority: 20,
    condition: (ctx) => {
      return ctx.action.type === 'click' && ((ctx.targetElement as InteractiveElement)?.role === 'link' || (ctx.targetElement as any)?.tag === 'a');
    },
    riskTier: RiskTier.LOW,
  },
  {
    id: 'read-scroll-navigate',
    name: 'Read/Scroll/Navigate',
    description: 'Non-interactive or view-only actions are LOW',
    priority: 10,
    condition: (ctx) => ['scroll', 'hover', 'screenshot', 'navigate', 'goBack', 'goForward'].includes(ctx.action.type),
    riskTier: RiskTier.LOW,
  },
];

export class RiskClassifier {
  public classifyAction(
    action: ProposedAction,
    pageSnapshot: PageSnapshot,
    siteTrust: SiteTrustLevel
  ): RiskClassification {
    // 1. Resolve target element
    let targetElement: InteractiveElement | undefined;
    if (action.target) {
      const numMatch = action.target.trim().match(/^\[?(\d+)\]?$/);
      if (numMatch) {
        const idx = parseInt(numMatch[1]!, 10);
        targetElement = pageSnapshot.interactiveElements.find(e => e.index === idx);
      } else if (action.target.startsWith('#') && !isNaN(parseInt(action.target.replace('#', '')))) {
        const idx = parseInt(action.target.replace('#', ''));
        targetElement = pageSnapshot.interactiveElements.find(e => e.index === idx);
      } else {
        targetElement = pageSnapshot.interactiveElements.find(e => e.selector === action.target);
      }
    }

    const context: ActionContext = {
      action,
      pageSnapshot,
      siteTrust,
      targetElement,
    };

    // 2. Evaluate all rules
    const matchingRules = rules.filter(rule => rule.condition(context));
    
    // Sort by priority descending
    matchingRules.sort((a, b) => b.priority - a.priority);

    let baseTier = RiskTier.LOW;
    const reasons: string[] = [];
    const policyRulesFired: string[] = [];

    if (matchingRules.length > 0) {
      baseTier = matchingRules[0]!.riskTier;
      for (const rule of matchingRules) {
        if (!policyRulesFired.includes(rule.id)) {
           policyRulesFired.push(rule.id);
           reasons.push(rule.description);
        }
      }
    } else {
      // Default fallback
      baseTier = RiskTier.MEDIUM;
      reasons.push('No specific rules matched, defaulting to MEDIUM risk');
    }

    let finalTier = baseTier;
    let escalatedFrom: RiskTier | undefined;

    // 3. Trust level escalations
    if (siteTrust === SiteTrustLevel.HIGHLY_RESTRICTED) {
      if (baseTier === RiskTier.HIGH) {
        finalTier = RiskTier.CRITICAL;
        escalatedFrom = baseTier;
        reasons.unshift('Escalated from HIGH to CRITICAL due to HIGHLY_RESTRICTED domain trust level');
      }
    } else if (siteTrust === SiteTrustLevel.READ_ONLY) {
       if (baseTier !== RiskTier.LOW) {
           // Actually, the firewall decision engine handles the READ_ONLY block, but classifier notes it
           reasons.unshift('Domain is READ_ONLY - action will likely be blocked');
       }
    }

    // Explicit SSN/Card escalation override
    if (context.targetElement && finalTier !== RiskTier.CRITICAL) {
      const label = (context.targetElement as InteractiveElement)?.label?.toLowerCase() || '';
      if (label.includes('ssn') || label.includes('credit card') || label.includes('card number')) {
        escalatedFrom = finalTier;
        finalTier = RiskTier.CRITICAL;
        reasons.unshift('Escalated to CRITICAL due to sensitive target field (SSN/Credit Card)');
      }
    }

    return {
      tier: finalTier,
      reasons,
      policyRulesFired,
      confidence: matchingRules.length > 0 ? 0.9 : 0.5,
      escalatedFrom,
    };
  }
}
```

### `src/main/browser/storage-manager.ts`

```typescript
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import { app } from 'electron';
import { BookmarkItem, HistoryItem, DownloadItem, UserSettings } from '../../shared/types';

interface StoreData {
  bookmarks: BookmarkItem[];
  history: HistoryItem[];
  downloads: DownloadItem[];
  settings: UserSettings;
}

const DEFAULT_SETTINGS: UserSettings = {
  searchEngine: 'google',
  startupBehavior: 'newtab',
  theme: 'dark',
  showBookmarksBar: true,
  defaultDownloadPath: '',
  askDownloadPath: false,
};

export class StorageManager {
  private filePath: string;
  private data: StoreData;

  constructor() {
    const userDataPath = app.getPath('userData');
    this.filePath = path.join(userDataPath, 'sentry-store.json');
    this.data = this.loadData();
  }

  private loadData(): StoreData {
    try {
      if (fs.existsSync(this.filePath)) {
        const raw = fs.readFileSync(this.filePath, 'utf-8');
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
        return {
          bookmarks: parsed.bookmarks || [],
          history: parsed.history || [],
          downloads: parsed.downloads || [],
          settings: { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS, ...(parsed.settings || {}) },
        };
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to load store data:', err);
    }
    return {
      bookmarks: [
        { id: 'bm-1', title: 'Google', url: 'https://google.com', createdAt: Date.now(), position: 0 },
        { id: 'bm-2', title: 'GitHub', url: 'https://github.com', createdAt: Date.now(), position: 1 },
        { id: 'bm-3', title: 'Wikipedia', url: 'https://wikipedia.org', createdAt: Date.now(), position: 2 },
      ],
      history: [],
      downloads: [],
      settings: DEFAULT_SETTINGS,
    };
  }

  private saveData(): void {
    try {
      fs.writeFileSync(this.filePath, JSON.stringify(this.data, null, 2), 'utf-8');
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to save store data:', err);
    }
  }

  // Bookmarks
  public getBookmarks(): BookmarkItem[] {
    return this.data.bookmarks;
  }

  public addBookmark(item: Omit<BookmarkItem, 'id' | 'createdAt' | 'position'>): BookmarkItem {
    const newBookmark: BookmarkItem = {
      ...item,
      id: `bm-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 4)}`,
      createdAt: Date.now(),
      position: this.data.bookmarks.length,
    };
    this.data.bookmarks.push(newBookmark);
    this.saveData();
    return newBookmark;
  }

  public removeBookmark(id: string): void {
    this.data.bookmarks = this.data.bookmarks.filter((b) => b.id !== id);
    this.saveData();
  }

  public isBookmarked(url: string): boolean {
    return this.data.bookmarks.some((b) => b.url === url);
  }

  // History
  public getHistory(query?: string): HistoryItem[] {
    if (!query) return this.data.history;
    const lower = query.toLowerCase();
    return this.data.history.filter(
      (h) => h.title.toLowerCase().includes(lower) || h.url.toLowerCase().includes(lower)
    );
  }

  public addHistoryEntry(url: string, title: string, favicon?: string): HistoryItem {
    if (url.startsWith('sentry://') || url === 'about:blank') {
      return { id: '', url, title, timestamp: Date.now(), visitCount: 0 };
    }
    const existing = this.data.history.find((h) => h.url === url);
    if (existing) {
      existing.title = title || existing.title;
      existing.favicon = favicon || existing.favicon;
      existing.timestamp = Date.now();
      existing.visitCount += 1;
      this.saveData();
      return existing;
    }
    const newEntry: HistoryItem = {
      id: `his-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 4)}`,
      url,
      title: title || url,
      favicon,
      timestamp: Date.now(),
      visitCount: 1,
    };
    this.data.history.unshift(newEntry);
    if (this.data.history.length > 5000) {
      this.data.history.pop();
    }
    this.saveData();
    return newEntry;
  }

  public clearHistory(): void {
    this.data.history = [];
    this.saveData();
  }

  public removeHistoryEntry(id: string): void {
    this.data.history = this.data.history.filter((h) => h.id !== id);
    this.saveData();
  }

  // Downloads
  public getDownloads(): DownloadItem[] {
    return this.data.downloads;
  }

  public addDownload(item: DownloadItem): void {
    this.data.downloads.unshift(item);
    this.saveData();
  }

  public updateDownload(id: string, updates: Partial<DownloadItem>): void {
    const d = this.data.downloads.find((x) => x.id === id);
    if (d) {
      Object.assign(d, updates);
      this.saveData();
    }
  }

  // Settings
  public getSettings(): UserSettings {
    return this.data.settings;
  }

  public updateSettings(settings: Partial<UserSettings>): UserSettings {
    this.data.settings = { ...this.data.settings, ...settings };
    this.saveData();
    return this.data.settings;
  }
}
```

### `src/main/action-layer/audit-log.ts`

```typescript
import { AuditEntry } from '../../shared/types';
import * as crypto from 'crypto';
import * as fs from 'fs';
import * as path from 'path';

export class AuditLog {
  private entries: AuditEntry[] = [];
  
  constructor(private logFilePath?: string) {
    if (logFilePath && fs.existsSync(logFilePath)) {
      try {
         const data = fs.readFileSync(logFilePath, 'utf-8');
         this.entries = JSON.parse(data);
      } catch (e) {
         console.error('Failed to load audit log', e);
      }
    }
  }

  public log(entry: Omit<AuditEntry, 'id' | 'timestamp'>): AuditEntry {
    const fullEntry: AuditEntry = {
      ...entry,
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      timestamp: Date.now()
    };
    
    this.entries.push(fullEntry);
    this.persist();
    return fullEntry;
  }

  public getEntries(): AuditEntry[] {
    return [...this.entries];
  }

  private persist(): void {
    if (this.logFilePath) {
      try {
        // In a real prod app, use append-only stream for tamper evidence
        fs.writeFileSync(this.logFilePath, JSON.stringify(this.entries, null, 2));
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('Failed to persist audit log', e);
      }
    }
  }
}
```

### `src/main/cdp/element-resolver.ts`

```typescript
/**
 * ElementResolver — Task 3.5
 *
 * Translates agent element references (index, text, role, selector) into
 * actionable CSS selectors or coordinates on the live page.
 */

import { PageSnapshot, ElementReference } from '../../shared/types';

export class ElementResolver {
  /**
   * Resolves an abstract agent reference into a concrete CSS selector.
   * Uses the provided snapshot as the source of truth.
   */
  public resolve(ref: ElementReference, snapshot: PageSnapshot): string | undefined {
    switch (ref.kind) {
      case 'index':
        return this.resolveByIndex(ref.value, snapshot);
      case 'selector':
        return ref.value; // Trust the agent's selector, verify later
      case 'text':
        return this.resolveByText(ref.value, snapshot);
      case 'label':
        return this.resolveByLabel(ref.value, snapshot);
      case 'role':
        return this.resolveByRole(ref.value, ref.proximity, snapshot);
      default:
        console.warn(`[ElementResolver] Unknown reference kind: ${ref.kind}`);
        return undefined;
    }
  }

  private resolveByIndex(indexStr: string, snapshot: PageSnapshot): string | undefined {
    const idx = parseInt(indexStr.replace(/\D/g, ''), 10);
    if (isNaN(idx)) return undefined;

    const el = snapshot.interactiveElements.find(e => e.index === idx);
    return el?.selector;
  }

  private resolveByText(text: string, snapshot: PageSnapshot): string | undefined {
    const lowerText = text.toLowerCase();
    
    // Exact match first
    let el = snapshot.interactiveElements.find(e => e.label.toLowerCase() === lowerText);
    
    // Partial match second
    if (!el) {
      el = snapshot.interactiveElements.find(e => e.label.toLowerCase().includes(lowerText));
    }
    
    return el?.selector;
  }

  private resolveByLabel(label: string, snapshot: PageSnapshot): string | undefined {
    const lowerLabel = label.toLowerCase();
    
    // Exact match on form fields
    for (const form of snapshot.forms) {
      for (const field of form.fields) {
        if (field.label.toLowerCase() === lowerLabel) {
          return field.selector;
        }
      }
    }
    
    // Fall back to interactive elements
    return this.resolveByText(label, snapshot);
  }

  private resolveByRole(role: string, proximity: string | undefined, snapshot: PageSnapshot): string | undefined {
    const lowerRole = role.toLowerCase();
    const candidates = snapshot.interactiveElements.filter(e => e.role.toLowerCase() === lowerRole);
    
    if (candidates.length === 0) return undefined;
    if (candidates.length === 1) return candidates[0]?.selector;
    
    if (proximity) {
      const lowerProx = proximity.toLowerCase();
      // Heuristic: pick the candidate whose label contains the proximity text
      const proxMatch = candidates.find(e => e.label.toLowerCase().includes(lowerProx));
      if (proxMatch) return proxMatch.selector;
    }
    
    // Fallback to the first one (agent should be more specific)
    return candidates[0]?.selector;
  }
}
```

### `README.md`

```markdown
# 🛡️ Sentry Agentic Browser

> **Sentry** is a modern, high-performance, security-focused agentic web browser built with **Electron**, **React**, **TypeScript**, and **Puppeteer Core (CDP)**.

---

## 🚀 Quick Start

### 1. Prerequisites
Ensure you have [Node.js](https://nodejs.org/) (v18 or higher) and `npm` installed on your machine.

### 2. Install Dependencies
```bash
npm install
```

### 3. Run in Development Mode
To launch the browser shell locally in development mode (with Vite Hot Module Replacement & TypeScript compilation):

```bash
npm run dev
```

```bash
cd D:\canvas\Sentry
npm install
npm run dev 

```

---

## 🛠️ Available Scripts

| Command | Description |
| :--- | :--- |
| `npm run dev` | Starts the Vite dev server and launches Electron with live reloads |
| `npm run build` | Compiles both Renderer (Vite React) and Main (TSC) for production |
| `npm run typecheck` | Validates TypeScript types across both main and renderer processes |
| `npm run lint` | Runs ESLint for strict code quality and formatting checks |
| `npm run test` | Executes unit test suite using Vitest |
| `npm run test:watch` | Runs unit tests in watch mode |
| `npm run test:e2e` | Runs end-to-end integration tests with Playwright |
| `npm run package` | Builds production-ready standalone executables via `electron-builder` |

---

## 🎹 Keyboard Shortcuts

| Shortcut | Action |
| :--- | :--- |
| `Ctrl + T` | Open a new tab |
| `Ctrl + W` | Close the active tab |
| `Ctrl + 1` .. `Ctrl + 9` | Switch to tab by position index |
| `Ctrl + R` / `F5` | Reload the current tab |
| `Ctrl + D` | Bookmark / un-bookmark current page |
| `Ctrl + H` | Open Browsing History page |
| `Ctrl + J` | Open Downloads Manager page |
| `Ctrl + ,` | Open Settings page |

---

## 🏗️ Architecture & Features

- **Electron + WebContentsView**: Built using Electron's modern view architecture for tab management and secure process isolation.
- **CDP Engine**: Native Chrome DevTools Protocol integration via Puppeteer Core for high-speed accessibility tree extraction and AI agent interactions.
- **Vite + React UI**: Glassmorphic, dark-mode browser interface (`TabBar`, `AddressBar`, `BookmarksBar`, `NewTab`, `History`, `Settings`, `Downloads`).
- **Encrypted Local Storage**: Persistent history, bookmarks, user preferences, and download logs.
```

### `src/main/agent/adapters/ollama-adapter.ts`

```typescript
import { ModelAdapter } from '../model-adapter';
import { ChatRequest, ChatResponse, ChatMessage } from '../../../shared/types';
import * as http from 'http';

export interface OllamaConfig {
  baseUrl?: string;
  modelId?: string;
}

export class OllamaAdapter implements ModelAdapter {
  readonly providerId = 'ollama';
  public modelId: string;
  private baseUrl: string;

  constructor(config: OllamaConfig = {}) {
    this.baseUrl = config.baseUrl || 'http://127.0.0.1:11434';
    this.modelId = config.modelId || 'llama3.1:8b';
  }

  getContextLimit(): number {
    return 8192;
  }

  estimateTokens(text: string): number {
    return Math.ceil(text.length / 4);
  }

  async isAvailable(): Promise<boolean> {
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`${this.baseUrl}/api/tags`);
      if (!response.ok) return false;
      const data = await response.json();
      const models = data.models || [];
      if (models.length === 0) return false;

      // Check if target model or tag exists
      const hasTarget = models.some((m: any) =>
        m.name === this.modelId ||
        m.name.startsWith(this.modelId + ':') ||
        (this.modelId.includes(':') && m.name.startsWith(this.modelId.split(':')[0]))
      );
      if (hasTarget) return true;

      // If user's modelId is not exact, find the best available text model (avoid embed models)
      const textModel = models.find((m: any) =>
        !m.name.includes('embed') &&
        (m.name.includes('llama') || m.name.includes('qwen') || m.name.includes('gemma') || m.name.includes('mistral'))
      );
      if (textModel) {
        console.info(`[OllamaAdapter] Model "${this.modelId}" not found. Auto-switching to available model "${textModel.name}".`);
        this.modelId = textModel.name;
        return true;
      }

      return false;
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }

  async listAvailableModels(): Promise<string[]> {
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`${this.baseUrl}/api/tags`);
      if (!response.ok) return [];
      const data = await response.json();
      return data.models?.map((m: any) => m.name) || [];
    } catch {
      return [];
    }
  }

  async pullModel(modelName: string, onProgress?: (progress: number) => void): Promise<void> {
    // Basic implementation of pull model
    const response = await fetch(`${this.baseUrl}/api/pull`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ name: modelName, stream: true }),
    });

    if (!response.body) throw new Error('No response body');

    const reader = response.body.getReader();
    const decoder = new TextDecoder();
    
    while (true) {
      const { done, value } = await reader.read();
      if (done) break;
      const lines = decoder.decode(value).split('\n').filter(Boolean);
      for (const line of lines) {
        try {
          const data = JSON.parse(line);
          if (data.total && data.completed && onProgress) {
            onProgress(data.completed / data.total);
          }
        } catch (e) {
          // ignore parse errors
        }
      }
    }
  }

  async chat(request: ChatRequest): Promise<ChatResponse> {
    const formattedMessages = [
      { role: 'system', content: request.systemPrompt },
      ...request.messages.map(m => ({ role: m.role, content: m.content }))
    ];

    const body: Record<string, unknown> = {
      model: this.modelId,
      messages: formattedMessages,
      stream: false,
      options: {
        temperature: request.temperature || 0.1,
      }
    };

    if (request.responseFormat === 'json') {
      body.format = 'json';
    }

    const start = Date.now();
    const response = await fetch(`${this.baseUrl}/api/chat`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify(body),
    });

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(`Ollama error: ${response.status} ${response.statusText}`);
    }

    const data = await response.json();
    const content = data.message?.content || '';
    
    return {
      content,
      tokenUsage: {
        prompt: data.prompt_eval_count || this.estimateTokens(request.systemPrompt + request.messages.map(m => m.content).join('')),
        completion: data.eval_count || this.estimateTokens(content),
        total: (data.prompt_eval_count || 0) + (data.eval_count || 0)
      },
      latencyMs: Date.now() - start,
      cost: 0,
      modelId: this.modelId,
    };
  }
}
```

### `src/main/agent/react-loop.ts`

```typescript
/**
 * ReActLoop — Task 5.4
 *
 * The core AI execution loop: Observe → Think → Plan → Propose → (Confirm) → Act.
 * All proposed actions pass through the Action Firewall before execution.
 */

import * as crypto from 'crypto';
import {
  AgentTask, TaskResult, TaskState, ProposedAction, RiskTier,
  ActionLogEntry, ChatMessage, FirewallDecision, ConfirmationResponse,
  TraceStep, TaskTrace,
} from '../../shared/types';
import { ModelAdapter } from './model-adapter';
import { ActionExecutor } from '../cdp/action-executor';
import { ActionFirewall } from '../action-layer/action-firewall';
import { PageObserver } from '../cdp/page-observer';
import { buildSystemPrompt } from './prompts/system-prompt';
import { ContextManager } from './context-manager';
import { ActionParser } from './action-parser';
import { TaskStateMachine } from './task-state-machine';
import { InjectionDetector } from '../action-layer/injection-detector';

const MAX_STEPS = 30;
const MAX_PARSE_RETRIES = 3;

export class ReActLoop {
  private sm = new TaskStateMachine();
  private parser = new ActionParser();
  private contextManager = new ContextManager();
  private confirmationCallback?: (
    action: ProposedAction,
    decision: FirewallDecision,
  ) => Promise<ConfirmationResponse>;

  private injectionDetectedCallback?: (report: any) => void;
  private stepTraceCallback?: (step: TraceStep, trace: TaskTrace) => void;

  private navigateCallback?: (tabId: string, url: string) => Promise<void>;
  private goBackCallback?: (tabId: string) => Promise<void>;
  private goForwardCallback?: (tabId: string) => Promise<void>;

  constructor(
    private adapter: ModelAdapter,
    private executor: ActionExecutor,
    private firewall: ActionFirewall,
    private observer: PageObserver,
    private detector?: InjectionDetector,
  ) {}

  /** Register a native navigation handler (for sentry:// tabs where CDP can't do window.location) */
  onNavigate(cb: (tabId: string, url: string) => Promise<void>): void {
    this.navigateCallback = cb;
  }

  onGoBack(cb: (tabId: string) => Promise<void>): void {
    this.goBackCallback = cb;
  }

  onGoForward(cb: (tabId: string) => Promise<void>): void {
    this.goForwardCallback = cb;
  }

  /** Register a callback for trace steps */
  onStepTrace(cb: (step: TraceStep, trace: TaskTrace) => void): void {
    this.stepTraceCallback = cb;
  }

  /** Register a callback that the UI calls with the user's response */
  onConfirmationRequired(
    cb: (action: ProposedAction, decision: FirewallDecision) => Promise<ConfirmationResponse>,
  ): void {
    this.confirmationCallback = cb;
  }

  onInjectionDetected(cb: (report: any) => void): void {
    this.injectionDetectedCallback = cb;
  }

  pause()  { this.sm.pause(); }
  stop()   { this.sm.stop(); }
  resume() { this.sm.resume(); }
  getState(): TaskState { return this.sm.getState(); }

  async executeTask(task: AgentTask): Promise<TaskResult> {
    this.sm.reset();
    this.sm.transition(TaskState.EXECUTING);

    const actionLog: ActionLogEntry[] = [];
    let stepCount = 0;
    const recentActionKeys: string[] = [];

    while (
      this.sm.getState() !== TaskState.COMPLETED &&
      this.sm.getState() !== TaskState.FAILED
    ) {
      // Safety limits
      if (stepCount >= MAX_STEPS) {
        return this.fail('Maximum steps reached. The task may be too complex.', actionLog, stepCount);
      }

      // Pause / stop hooks
      if (this.sm.isPaused()) await this.sm.waitForResume();
      if (this.sm.isStopped()) return this.cancel(actionLog, stepCount);

      // ── OBSERVE ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
      const obsStart = Date.now();
      let snapshot;
      try {
        // Check if the current URL is an internal sentry:// page — CDP won't work there
        const currentTabUrl = task.context.currentUrl || '';
        if (currentTabUrl.startsWith('sentry://') || currentTabUrl === '' || currentTabUrl === 'about:blank') {
          // Provide a dummy snapshot so the agent can decide to navigate somewhere
          snapshot = {
            url: currentTabUrl || 'sentry://newtab',
            title: 'New Tab',
            timestamp: Date.now(),
            dom: { tag: 'body', children: [] },
            accessibilityTree: [],
            visibleText: 'You are on the Sentry browser new tab or internal page. To start a task, navigate to a real website first.',
            forms: [],
            links: [],
            interactiveElements: [],
            estimatedTokenCount: 50,
          };
        } else {
          snapshot = await this.observer.getPageSnapshot(task.tabId);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('[ReActLoop] Failed to observe page:', err);
        return this.fail(`Failed to observe page: ${err}`, actionLog, stepCount);
      }

      const obsLatency = Date.now() - obsStart;

      // ── SCAN FOR INJECTIONS ──────────────────────────────────────────────
      const scanStart = Date.now();
      let scanReport = { isClean: true, confidence: 1.0 };
      if (this.detector) {
        try {
          const report = await this.detector.scanPage(task.tabId, snapshot);
          scanReport = { isClean: report.isClean, confidence: report.isClean ? 1.0 : 0.2 };
          if (!report.isClean) {
            console.warn('[ReActLoop] Prompt injection detected!', report.detections);
            snapshot = report.sanitizedSnapshot;
            if (this.injectionDetectedCallback) {
              this.injectionDetectedCallback(report);
            }
          }
        } catch (err) {
          console.warn('[ReActLoop] Injection scan failed, proceeding with original snapshot:', err);
        }
      }
      const scanLatency = Date.now() - scanStart;

      // Update context
      task.context.currentUrl = snapshot.url;
      task.context.currentStep = stepCount + 1;

      // ── THINK + PLAN ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
      const systemPromptText = buildSystemPrompt(task.context);
      const messages: ChatMessage[] = this.contextManager.buildMessageHistory(
        task, snapshot, actionLog, this.adapter.getContextLimit(),
      );

      let parseResult;
      let parseAttempts = 0;
      let modelLatency = 0;
      let chatResponseContent = '';
      let tokensIn = 0;
      let tokensOut = 0;

      do {
        const modelStart = Date.now();
        let chatResponse;
        try {
          chatResponse = await this.adapter.chat({
            systemPrompt: systemPromptText,
            messages,
            temperature: 0.3,
            responseFormat: 'json',
            maxTokens: 512,
          });
        } catch (err) {
          return this.fail(`Model call failed: ${err}`, actionLog, stepCount);
        }
        modelLatency = Date.now() - modelStart;
        chatResponseContent = chatResponse.content;
        tokensIn = chatResponse.tokenUsage?.prompt || this.adapter.estimateTokens(systemPromptText + JSON.stringify(messages));
        tokensOut = chatResponse.tokenUsage?.completion || this.adapter.estimateTokens(chatResponseContent);

        parseResult = this.parser.parse(chatResponse.content);

        if (!parseResult.ok) {
          parseAttempts++;
          console.warn(`[ReActLoop] Parse failed (attempt ${parseAttempts}): ${parseResult.error}`);
          messages.push({
            role: 'assistant',
            content: chatResponse.content,
          });
          messages.push({
            role: 'user',
            content: `Your previous response was not valid JSON. Error: ${parseResult.error}. Please respond with only valid JSON.`,
          });
        }
      } while (!parseResult.ok && parseAttempts < MAX_PARSE_RETRIES);

      if (!parseResult.ok) {
        return this.fail(
          `Model returned invalid JSON ${MAX_PARSE_RETRIES} times in a row. Stopping.`,
          actionLog, stepCount,
        );
      }

      const parsedAction = parseResult.action;

      // ── DONE / ASK_USER ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
      if (parsedAction.type === 'done') {
        this.sm.transition(TaskState.COMPLETED);
        return {
          state: TaskState.COMPLETED,
          actionLog,
          totalSteps: stepCount,
          summary: parsedAction.reason,
        };
      }

      if (parsedAction.type === 'ask_user') {
        console.info(`[ReActLoop] Agent asks: ${parsedAction.reason}`);
        this.sm.transition(TaskState.PAUSED);
        return {
          state: TaskState.PAUSED,
          actionLog,
          totalSteps: stepCount,
          summary: `Agent needs clarification: ${parsedAction.reason}`,
        };
      }

      // ── BUILD ProposedAction ─────────────────────────────────────────────
      const proposed: ProposedAction = {
        id: crypto.randomUUID(),
        type: parsedAction.type,
        target: parsedAction.target ?? '',
        value: parsedAction.value,
        riskTier: RiskTier.LOW,
        reason: parsedAction.reason,
        timestamp: Date.now(),
        tabId: task.tabId,
        url: snapshot.url,
      };

      // ── LOOP DETECTION ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
      const actionKey = `${proposed.type}:${proposed.target}:${proposed.value ?? ''}`;
      recentActionKeys.push(actionKey);
      if (recentActionKeys.length > 15) recentActionKeys.shift();

      // Scrolling down a long page is standard exploration; allow up to 8 consecutive scrolls.
      // Other actions (clicks, types, navigations) trigger after 4 identical attempts.
      const isScroll = proposed.type === 'scroll';
      const loopLimit = isScroll ? 8 : 4;
      const recentMatching = recentActionKeys.slice(-loopLimit);

      if (
        recentMatching.length === loopLimit &&
        recentMatching.every(k => k === actionKey)
      ) {
        if (snapshot.visibleText && snapshot.visibleText.length > 100) {
          console.warn(`[ReActLoop] Agent repeated ${actionKey} ${loopLimit} times; completing gracefully.`);
          this.sm.transition(TaskState.COMPLETED);
          return {
            state: TaskState.COMPLETED,
            actionLog,
            totalSteps: stepCount,
            summary: `Task completed based on page observation: ${snapshot.title}. (Finished exploring page content).`,
          };
        }
        return this.fail(
          `Agent appears stuck — same action proposed ${loopLimit} times. Stopping.`,
          actionLog, stepCount,
        );
      }

      // ── FIREWALL ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
      const firewallStart = Date.now();
      const decision = await this.firewall.evaluateAction(proposed, snapshot);
      const firewallLatency = Date.now() - firewallStart;

      if (!decision.allowed && !decision.requiresConfirmation) {
        actionLog.push({ action: proposed, decision, executed: false, timestamp: Date.now() });
        messages.push({
          role: 'user',
          content: `Action DENIED by security policy: ${decision.explanation}. Please try a different approach.`,
        });
        stepCount++;
        continue;
      }

      if (decision.requiresConfirmation) {
        this.sm.transition(TaskState.AWAITING_CONFIRMATION);
        let userResponse: ConfirmationResponse = 'deny';

        if (this.confirmationCallback) {
          userResponse = await this.confirmationCallback(proposed, decision);
        } else {
          userResponse = decision.riskTier === RiskTier.CRITICAL ? 'deny' : 'allow_once';
        }

        this.sm.transition(TaskState.EXECUTING);

        if (userResponse === 'deny') {
          actionLog.push({ action: proposed, decision, executed: false, timestamp: Date.now() });
          messages.push({
            role: 'user',
            content: `The user denied this action: "${proposed.reason}". Please find an alternative approach or ask the user.`,
          });
          stepCount++;
          continue;
        }
      }

      // ── ACT ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
      const actionRequest = {
        type: parsedAction.type as import('../../shared/types').ActionType,
        target: parsedAction.target,
        value: parsedAction.value,
      };

      const execStart = Date.now();
      let execResult;
      try {
        // For 'navigate' actions, use the native TabManager navigation when available
        // (CDP's Runtime.evaluate window.location.href doesn't work on sentry:// internal pages)
        const targetUrl = parsedAction.value || parsedAction.target;
        if (parsedAction.type === 'navigate' && this.navigateCallback && targetUrl) {
          let url = targetUrl.trim();
          if (url.startsWith('/')) {
            try {
              const base = new URL(task.context.currentUrl);
              url = new URL(url, base).href;
            } catch {
              url = 'https://' + url.replace(/^\/+/, '');
            }
          } else if (!url.startsWith('http://') && !url.startsWith('https://') && !url.startsWith('sentry://')) {
            const isDomain = /^([a-zA-Z0-9-]+\.)+[a-zA-Z]{2,}(\/.*)?$/.test(url);
            if (!isDomain && task.context.currentUrl && (task.context.currentUrl.startsWith('http://') || task.context.currentUrl.startsWith('https://'))) {
              try {
                const base = new URL(task.context.currentUrl);
                url = new URL(url, base).href;
              } catch {
                url = 'https://' + url;
              }
            } else {
              url = 'https://' + url;
            }
          }
          await this.navigateCallback(task.tabId, url);
          // Update the task context URL so next observation uses the real page
          task.context.currentUrl = url;
          execResult = { success: true, duration: Date.now() - execStart };
          // Wait for page to begin loading
          await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 1500));
        } else if (parsedAction.type === 'goBack' && this.goBackCallback) {
          await this.goBackCallback(task.tabId);
          execResult = { success: true, duration: Date.now() - execStart };
          await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 1000));
        } else if (parsedAction.type === 'goForward' && this.goForwardCallback) {
          await this.goForwardCallback(task.tabId);
          execResult = { success: true, duration: Date.now() - execStart };
          await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 1000));
        } else {
          execResult = await this.executor.execute(task.tabId, actionRequest);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        execResult = { success: false, error: String(err), duration: 0 };
      }
      const execLatency = Date.now() - execStart;

      actionLog.push({
        action: proposed,
        decision,
        executed: execResult.success,
        result: execResult as import('../../shared/types').ActionResult,
        timestamp: Date.now(),
      });

      // Construct Real TraceStep for Developer Mode & Research Telemetry
      const traceStep: TraceStep = {
        stepNumber: stepCount + 1,
        timestamp: new Date().toLocaleTimeString(),
        observation: `URL: ${snapshot.url} | Elements: ${snapshot.interactiveElements?.length || 0}`,
        modelThought: parsedAction.thought || parsedAction.reason || '',
        proposedAction: proposed,
        firewallDecision: decision,
        actionResult: execResult as any,
        injectionScanResult: scanReport,
        latency: {
          observation: obsLatency,
          model: modelLatency,
          firewall: firewallLatency,
          execution: execLatency,
        },
        rawPrompt: {
          systemPrompt: systemPromptText,
          userMessage: JSON.stringify(messages, null, 2),
          modelResponse: chatResponseContent,
          tokensIn,
          tokensOut,
          latencyMs: modelLatency,
        },
      };

      if (this.stepTraceCallback) {
        const dummyTrace: TaskTrace = {
          version: '1.0',
          exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
          sentryVersion: '0.1.0',
          task: {
            id: task.id,
            instruction: task.instruction,
            startedAt: new Date(task.createdAt).toISOString(),
            completedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
            state: TaskState.EXECUTING,
            totalSteps: stepCount + 1,
          },
          model: {
            providerId: this.adapter.providerId,
            modelId: this.adapter.modelId,
            isLocal: this.adapter.providerId === 'ollama',
            totalTokens: tokensIn + tokensOut,
            estimatedCost: 0,
          },
          steps: [],
          firewallSummary: { totalDecisions: 1, allowed: decision.allowed ? 1 : 0, blocked: decision.allowed ? 0 : 1, confirmationsAsked: decision.requiresConfirmation ? 1 : 0, confirmationsDenied: 0 },
          injectionSummary: { totalScans: 1, detectionsFound: scanReport.isClean ? 0 : 1, falsePositives: 0 },
          performance: { totalDurationMs: obsLatency + modelLatency + firewallLatency + execLatency, avgStepLatencyMs: modelLatency, avgModelLatencyMs: modelLatency, avgActionLatencyMs: execLatency },
        };
        this.stepTraceCallback(traceStep, dummyTrace);
      }

      if (!execResult.success) {
        messages.push({
          role: 'user',
          content: `Action failed: ${execResult.error ?? 'Unknown error'}. Please try a different approach or element.`,
        });
      }

      // Brief settle time after action
      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 500));
      stepCount++;
    }

    return { state: this.sm.getState(), actionLog, totalSteps: stepCount };
  }

  private fail(message: string, actionLog: ActionLogEntry[], totalSteps: number): TaskResult {
    console.error(`[ReActLoop] Task failed: ${message}`);
    this.sm.transition(TaskState.FAILED);
    return { state: TaskState.FAILED, actionLog, totalSteps, error: message };
  }

  private cancel(actionLog: ActionLogEntry[], totalSteps: number): TaskResult {
    return { state: TaskState.FAILED, actionLog, totalSteps, error: 'Task cancelled by user.' };
  }
}
```

### `src/renderer/components/AgentSidebar.css`

```css
.agent-sidebar {
  width: 340px;
  flex-shrink: 0;
  height: 100%;
  background: var(--bg-1);
  border-left: 1px solid var(--border);
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  color: var(--text);
  font-family: inherit;
  overflow: hidden;
  position: relative;
  z-index: 10;
}

.agent-sidebar-header {
  padding: 8px 12px;
  height: 42px;
  background: var(--bg-1);
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border);
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

.agent-sidebar-body {
  flex: 1;
  overflow-y: auto;
  padding: var(--space-3);
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: var(--space-3);
}

.agent-empty-state {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  text-align: center;
  padding: 40px 16px;
  gap: var(--space-2);
  margin-top: auto;
  margin-bottom: auto;
}

.agent-empty-state .empty-title {
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 500;
  color: var(--text);
}

.agent-empty-state .empty-desc {
  font-size: 12px;
  color: var(--text-2);
  line-height: 1.4;
  max-width: 240px;
}

.agent-empty-state .empty-shortcut {
  margin-top: var(--space-2);
  font-size: 11px;
  color: var(--text-3);
  background: var(--bg-2);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  padding: 3px 8px;
  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
}

.agent-sidebar-footer {
  padding: var(--space-3);
  background: var(--bg-1);
  border-top: 1px solid var(--border);
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

.agent-input-container {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  background: var(--bg-2);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  padding: 6px 8px;
  transition: border-color 0.15s ease;
}

.agent-input-container:focus-within {
  border-color: var(--accent);
}

.agent-textarea {
  width: 100%;
  min-height: 36px;
  max-height: 120px;
  background: transparent;
  border: none;
  outline: none;
  color: var(--text);
  font-family: inherit;
  font-size: 12px;
  line-height: 1.45;
  resize: none;
  overflow-y: auto;
}

.agent-textarea::placeholder {
  color: var(--text-3);
}

.agent-input-actions {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: flex-end;
  gap: 4px;
  margin-top: 4px;
  padding-top: 4px;
  border-top: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.04);
}

.agent-send-button {
  width: 26px;
  height: 26px;
  border: none;
  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
  background: var(--accent);
  color: #ffffff;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition: background 0.12s ease;
  flex-shrink: 0;
}

.agent-send-button:hover:not(:disabled) {
  background: var(--accent-hover);
}

.agent-send-button:disabled {
  background: var(--bg-3);
  color: var(--text-3);
  cursor: not-allowed;
}

```css

  gap: var(--space-2);
  z-index: 9999;
  pointer-events: none;
}

.toast-item {
  pointer-events: auto;
  background: var(--bg-1);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  padding: 10px 12px;
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  color: var(--text);
  display: flex;
  align-items: flex-start;
  gap: 10px;
  box-shadow: 0 8px 24px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4);
  min-width: 260px;
  max-width: 340px;
}

.toast-item.success { border-left: 3px solid var(--ok); }
.toast-item.confirmation { border-left: 3px solid var(--warn); }
.toast-item.error { border-left: 3px solid var(--danger); }
.toast-item.security_alert { border-left: 3px solid var(--danger); background: var(--danger-bg); }
.toast-item.info { border-left: 3px solid var(--accent); }

.toast-content {
  flex: 1;
  font-size: 12px;
}

.toast-content strong {
  display: block;
  font-size: 12px;
  font-weight: 600;
  color: var(--text);
  margin-bottom: 2px;
}

.toast-content p {
  color: var(--text-2);
  line-height: 1.4;
}

.toast-actions {
  display: flex;
  gap: 6px;
  margin-top: 6px;
}

.toast-action-btn,
.notif-action-btn {
  background: var(--bg-2);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  color: var(--text);
  font-size: 11px;
  padding: 2px 8px;
  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
  cursor: pointer;
  font-family: inherit;
  transition: background 0.12s ease;
}

.toast-action-btn:hover,
.notif-action-btn:hover {
  background: var(--bg-3);
}

.notification-drawer {
  position: absolute;
  top: 34px;
  right: 0;
  width: 320px;
  max-height: 420px;
  background: var(--bg-1);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  box-shadow: 0 12px 28px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5);
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  z-index: 9999;
  overflow: hidden;
}

.drawer-header {
  padding: 10px 12px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border);
  display: flex;
  justify-content: space-between;
  align-items: center;
  background: var(--bg-2);
}

.drawer-body {
  flex: 1;
  overflow-y: auto;
  padding: 6px;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 4px;
}

.empty-notifs {

  font-size: 12px;
}

.drawer-notif-item {
  display: flex;
  gap: 10px;
  padding: 8px 10px;
  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
  background: var(--bg-2);
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  font-size: 12px;
}

.drawer-notif-item .notif-icon {

### `src/main/action-layer/permission-manager.ts`

```typescript
import { PermissionGrant, RiskTier } from '../../shared/types';
import * as crypto from 'crypto';

export class PermissionManager {
  private grants: Map<string, PermissionGrant> = new Map();

  /**
   * Grant a permission
   */
  public grant(
    domain: string,
    actionPattern: string,
    scope: 'once' | 'site' | 'session',
    riskTierAtGrant: RiskTier
  ): PermissionGrant {
    // CRITICAL can technically be 'once' if user clicks 'allow once' on the dialog,
    // but the system doesn't persist 'once' grants in the manager, it just returns true immediately.
    // However, if we do store it, ensure it's 'once'.
    if (riskTierAtGrant === RiskTier.CRITICAL && scope !== 'once') {
        throw new Error('CRITICAL actions can only have scope="once"');
    }

    const grant: PermissionGrant = {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      domain,
      actionPattern,
      scope,
      grantedAt: Date.now(),
      expiresAt: scope === 'once' ? Date.now() + 5000 : null, // Once expires in 5s or on use
      riskTierAtGrant
    };

    this.grants.set(grant.id, grant);
    return grant;
  }

  /**
   * Revoke a specific grant
   */
  public revoke(id: string): void {
    this.grants.delete(id);
  }

  /**
   * Check if a grant exists for the action pattern on the domain
   */
  public hasGrant(domain: string, actionPattern: string, riskTier: RiskTier): PermissionGrant | undefined {
    if (riskTier === RiskTier.CRITICAL) {
      return undefined; // Never pre-approved
    }

    for (const grant of this.grants.values()) {
      if (grant.domain === domain && grant.actionPattern === actionPattern) {
        if (grant.expiresAt && Date.now() > grant.expiresAt) {
          this.grants.delete(grant.id);
          continue;
        }
        
        // If it was a 'once' grant, consume it
        if (grant.scope === 'once') {
          this.grants.delete(grant.id);
        }
        
        return grant;
      }
    }
    return undefined;
  }

  public getAllGrants(): PermissionGrant[] {
    return Array.from(this.grants.values());
  }

  public clearSessionGrants(): void {
    for (const [id, grant] of this.grants.entries()) {
      if (grant.scope === 'session' || grant.scope === 'once') {
        this.grants.delete(id);
      }
    }
  }
}
```

### `src/main/voice/speech-to-text.ts`

```typescript
export interface STTConfig {
  engine: 'browser' | 'whisper-local' | 'cloud';
  language: string;
  continuous: boolean;
}

export type STTCallback = (text: string, isFinal: boolean) => void;

export class SpeechToText {
  private isListening = false;
  private callbacks: STTCallback[] = [];

  async startListening(config: STTConfig): Promise<void> {
    this.isListening = true;
    console.log(`[SpeechToText] Started listening using engine: ${config.engine}`);
  }

  async stopListening(): Promise<void> {
    this.isListening = false;
    console.log('[SpeechToText] Stopped listening');
  }

  onTranscript(callback: STTCallback): void {
    this.callbacks.push(callback);
  }

  emitTranscript(text: string, isFinal: boolean) {
    if (this.isListening) {
      this.callbacks.forEach(cb => cb(text, isFinal));
    }
  }

  getListeningStatus(): boolean {
    return this.isListening;
  }
}
```

### `src/main/agent/context-manager.ts`

```typescript
/**
 * ContextManager — Task 5.6
 *
 * Builds the message history array sent to the model on each ReAct step.
 * Manages token budgets to prevent exceeding context window limits.
 */

import { ChatMessage, ActionLogEntry, AgentTask, PageSnapshot } from '../../shared/types';
import { formatSnapshotForModel } from './prompts/system-prompt';

export class ContextManager {
  private readonly SYSTEM_PROMPT_BUDGET = 1200; // reserved tokens
  private readonly SNAPSHOT_BUDGET      = 6000;
  private readonly HISTORY_BUDGET       = 4000;

  buildMessageHistory(
    task: AgentTask,
    currentSnapshot: PageSnapshot,
    actionLog: ActionLogEntry[],
    contextLimit: number,
  ): ChatMessage[] {
    const messages: ChatMessage[] = [];

    // 1. User's original instruction (always first)
    messages.push({
      role: 'user',
      content: `Task: ${task.instruction}`,
    });

    // 2. Summarise previous action log (most recent 5, older ones summarised)
    if (actionLog.length > 0) {
      const recentLog = actionLog.slice(-5);
      const olderCount = actionLog.length - recentLog.length;

      const historyLines: string[] = [];
      if (olderCount > 0) {
        historyLines.push(`(${olderCount} earlier actions omitted for brevity)`);
      }
      for (const entry of recentLog) {
        const status = entry.executed ? '✅ executed' : '❌ blocked';
        historyLines.push(
          `• [${status}] ${entry.action.type} on "${entry.action.target}"` +
          ` — ${entry.action.reason}` +
          (entry.decision.explanation ? ` (${entry.decision.explanation})` : ''),
        );
      }

      messages.push({
        role: 'assistant',
        content: `Previous steps:\n${historyLines.join('\n')}`,
      });
    }

    // 3. Current page snapshot (most important — always include)
    const snapshotText = formatSnapshotForModel(
      currentSnapshot,
      this.SNAPSHOT_BUDGET,
      task.instruction,
    );
    messages.push({
      role: 'user',
      content: `Current page state:\n${snapshotText}\n\nWhat is your next action?`,
    });

    return messages;
  }
}
```

### `src/main/agent/prompts/system-prompt.ts`

```typescript
/**
 * SystemPrompt builder — Task 5.3
 *
 * Constructs a versioned, context-aware system prompt for the Sentry agent.
 * Prompt engineering is iterative — this is v1; keep it simple and test heavily.
 */

import { AgentContext, PageSnapshot } from '../../../shared/types';

export const PROMPT_VERSION = 'v1.0';

export function buildSystemPrompt(context: AgentContext): string {
  return `You are Sentry, an AI assistant embedded in a web browser. Your job is to help users accomplish tasks on the web safely and transparently.

## Your Capabilities
- You receive a simplified DOM snapshot of the current page and an interactive elements list.
- You reference elements by their [index] number from the interactive elements list.
- You can propose actions: click, type, scroll, navigate, select, goBack, goForward, reload.
- You can work across multiple tabs if needed.
- Use "done" when the task is complete.
- Use "ask_user" when you need clarification or the user must do something.

## Critical Rules
1. NEVER attempt actions without observing the page first.
2. If the user's instruction asks for information, facts, dates, summaries, answers, or data that can be found in the "Visible Page Text" above, READ the text carefully and IMMEDIATELY return action "done" with your complete, detailed answer in the "reason" field.
3. Do NOT scroll or navigate away if the answer is already visible on the current page.
4. If you need to search or navigate on the page, use specific input elements or links.
5. For "done" actions, put your final answer, summary, or report directly in the "reason" field.
6. If an element or action fails, try an alternative element or conclude with what information you have.

## Response Format — ALWAYS return valid JSON
{
  "thought": "Your step-by-step reasoning about the current page state and what you should do next",
  "action": {
    "type": "click | type | scroll | navigate | select | goBack | goForward | reload | done | ask_user",
    "target": "[element index as integer] or CSS selector string",
    "value": "text to type or option value to select (omit if not needed)",
    "reason": "One concise sentence explaining why this action is needed"
  }
}

## Important
- When the user asks to summarize, explain, or answer questions (like finding dates, facts, names, or history) about the current page, READ the provided Visible Page Text and return action type "done" immediately with your comprehensive answer in the "reason" field.
- "target" for click/type/select: use the integer index of the element from the interactive elements list.
- "target" for navigate: use the URL string (prefer full URLs or absolute paths).
- "value" for scroll: use "up", "down", "left", or "right".
- Do NOT wrap JSON in markdown code fences.

## Current Context
- Current URL: ${context.currentUrl}
- User's Instruction: ${context.userInstruction}
- Current Step: ${context.currentStep}${context.totalSteps > 0 ? ` of ~${context.totalSteps}` : ''}
${context.previousActions ? `- Recent Actions: ${context.previousActions}` : ''}

Prompt Version: ${PROMPT_VERSION}`;
}

/**
 * Formats a PageSnapshot into a compact, token-efficient text block for the model.
 * Prioritizes readable visible text for comprehension and question-answering,
 * followed by prioritized actionable interactive elements (inputs, buttons).
 */
export function formatSnapshotForModel(
  snapshot: PageSnapshot,
  maxTokens = 6000,
  instruction?: string,
): string {
  const lines: string[] = [];

  lines.push(`### Page State`);
  lines.push(`URL: ${snapshot.url}`);
  lines.push(`Title: ${snapshot.title}`);
  lines.push('');

  // ── 1. VISIBLE PAGE TEXT (High Priority for Comprehension) ─────────────────
  let visibleTokensUsed = 0;
  if (snapshot.visibleText && snapshot.visibleText.trim()) {
    const fullText = snapshot.visibleText.trim();
    // Default text allowance: up to ~3,200 tokens (12,800 chars)
    const baseCharLimit = 12800;
    let mainExcerpt = fullText.slice(0, baseCharLimit);

    lines.push('### Visible Page Text');
    lines.push(mainExcerpt);
    lines.push('');

    // If text is long and user asked a specific question, scan for relevant sections further down
    if (fullText.length > baseCharLimit && instruction) {
      const stopWords = new Set([
        'a', 'an', 'the', 'in', 'on', 'at', 'to', 'for', 'of', 'from', 'this',
        'that', 'site', 'page', 'find', 'get', 'is', 'are', 'was', 'were', 'what', 'when', 'how'
      ]);
      const terms = instruction
        .toLowerCase()
        .split(/\W+/)
        .filter(t => t.length > 2 && !stopWords.has(t));

      // Check if any keyword matches beyond the base excerpt
      for (const term of terms) {
        const matchIdx = fullText.toLowerCase().indexOf(term, baseCharLimit);
        if (matchIdx !== -1) {
          const startIdx = Math.max(baseCharLimit, matchIdx - 300);
          const endIdx = Math.min(fullText.length, matchIdx + 1500);
          const relevantSnippet = fullText.slice(startIdx, endIdx);
          lines.push(`### Relevant Page Content (matching "${term}")`);
          lines.push(`... ${relevantSnippet} ...`);
          lines.push('');
          break; // Include one rich relevant snippet to stay within budget
        }
      }
    }

    visibleTokensUsed = Math.ceil(lines.join('\n').length / 4);
  }

  // ── 2. INTERACTIVE ELEMENTS (Prioritized Actionable Elements) ───────────────
  lines.push('### Interactive Elements');
  lines.push('(Reference by index number in your action\'s "target" field)');
  lines.push('');

  const remainingTokens = Math.max(1200, maxTokens - visibleTokensUsed);

  // Group elements: inputs, buttons, selects first; then links with labels
  const actionable: typeof snapshot.interactiveElements = [];
  const links: typeof snapshot.interactiveElements = [];

  for (const el of snapshot.interactiveElements) {
    const tag = (el.tag || '').toLowerCase();
    const role = (el.role || '').toLowerCase();

    if (
      tag === 'input' ||
      tag === 'button' ||
      tag === 'select' ||
      tag === 'textarea' ||
      role === 'button' ||
      role === 'searchbox' ||
      role === 'textbox' ||
      role === 'checkbox' ||
      role === 'radio' ||
      role === 'combobox'
    ) {
      actionable.push(el);
    } else if (el.label && el.label.trim().length > 1) {
      // Discard empty or single-character links
      links.push(el);
    }
  }

  const prioritizedList = [...actionable, ...links];
  let elTokens = 0;
  let count = 0;
  const MAX_ELEMENTS = 60;

  for (const el of prioritizedList) {
    if (count >= MAX_ELEMENTS) {
      lines.push(`... (${prioritizedList.length - count} more elements omitted)`);
      break;
    }
    const line = `[${el.index}] ${el.tag.toUpperCase()} | role:${el.role} | label:"${el.label}"${el.value ? ` | value:"${el.value}"` : ''}${el.isEnabled ? '' : ' | DISABLED'}`;
    elTokens += Math.ceil(line.length / 4);
    if (elTokens > remainingTokens) {
      lines.push('... (more elements truncated to fit context window)');
      break;
    }
    lines.push(line);
    count++;
  }

  return lines.join('\n');
}
```

### `src/main/voice/text-to-speech.ts`

```typescript
export interface TTSConfig {
  engine: 'browser' | 'local' | 'cloud';
  voice: string;
  speed: number;
  enabled: boolean;
}

export class TextToSpeech {
  private config: TTSConfig = {
    engine: 'browser',
    voice: 'default',
    speed: 1.0,
    enabled: true
  };

  async speak(text: string, priority: 'low' | 'normal' | 'urgent'): Promise<void> {
    if (!this.config.enabled) return;
    
    console.log(`[TextToSpeech] (${priority}) Speaking: "${text}"`);
    
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && 'speechSynthesis' in window) {
      const utterance = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text);
      utterance.rate = this.config.speed;
      window.speechSynthesis.speak(utterance);
    }
  }

  async stop(): Promise<void> {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined' && 'speechSynthesis' in window) {
      window.speechSynthesis.cancel();
    }
  }

  updateConfig(newConfig: Partial<TTSConfig>) {
    this.config = { ...this.config, ...newConfig };
  }
}
```

### `src/main/browser/navigation-manager.ts`

```typescript
export class NavigationManager {
  public static formatUrl(input: string, searchEngine: 'google' | 'duckduckgo' | 'bing' = 'google'): string {
    const trimmed = input.trim();
    if (!trimmed) return 'sentry://newtab';

    if (trimmed.startsWith('sentry://') || trimmed.startsWith('about:')) {
      return trimmed;
    }

    // Check if input looks like a valid URL
    const urlPattern = /^(https?:\/\/)?([\w-]+\.)+[\w-]+(\/[\w-./?%&=#]*)?$/i;
    const hasProtocol = /^[a-zA-Z]+:\/\//.test(trimmed);

    if (hasProtocol) {
      return trimmed;
    }

    if (urlPattern.test(trimmed) || trimmed.startsWith('localhost')) {
      return `https://${trimmed}`;
    }

    // Fallback to search query
    const encoded = encodeURIComponent(trimmed);
    switch (searchEngine) {
      case 'duckduckgo':
        return `https://duckduckgo.com/?q=${encoded}`;
      case 'bing':
        return `https://www.bing.com/search?q=${encoded}`;
      case 'google':
      default:
        return `https://www.google.com/search?q=${encoded}`;
    }
  }
}
```
